patippurai
tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟai  eṉum innūl tamiḻaṟiñar 
na. cuppureṭṭiyār avarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭu 1957 il mutal patippākavum, 1964 il tiruttam ceyyappaṭṭu iraṇṭām patippākavum veḷi vantuḷḷatu. itaṉai empatippakattiṉ vāyilāka veḷiyiṭa muṉvantuḷḷōm.
tāymoḻivaḻik kalviyiṉ mēṉmai kuṟittum ataṉ payaṉpāṭu kuṟittum, vaḷarccik kuṟittum tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷum, araciya lāḷarkaḷum atikam pēci varum kālamitu. inta nērattil innūl veḷivaruvatu mika poruttamāka irukkum eṉṟu karutukiṟōm.
tamiḻmoḻiyaip payiṟṟum nōkkaṅkaḷ  toṭaṅki, muṭivāka kalvittuṟai aḷaviyal eṉṉum 16 talaippukaḷai uḷḷuṟaiyākak koṇṭu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. innūliluḷḷa ceytikaḷ kalviyāḷar kaḷukkum, māṇavarkaḷukkum peritum payaṉpaṭattakkavai.
innūliṉ arumaiperumaikaḷai  mēṉāḷ kalviyamaiccar aviṉācaliṅkam  avarkaḷiṉ aṇinturaiyilum, āṅkila muṉṉurai yil  tiruvēṅkaṭaccāri avarkaḷum, nūlmukam talaippil in nūlāciriyariṉ kuṟippukaḷum innūlaip paṭikkum muṉ  aṉaivarum paṭikka vēṇṭiya ceytikaḷākum. tāymoḻivaḻikkalviyiṉ nōkkaṅkaḷ kuṟittum iṉṟiyamaiyāmai kuṟittum pēcappaṭum ikkāla
kaṭṭattil innūlai veḷiyiṭuvataik kaṭamaiyāka koḷkiṟōm.

eṉ aṉṉaikku
aṉpup paṭaiyal

	naṟṟavat tāyē! vāḻi! pallāṇṭu
		nalaṉuṟap peṟṟeṉai vaḷarttuk
	kaṟṟava ṉākkip pēṇiṉai ! ulakiṟ
		kāṇuṟu poruḷelām koṇarntuṉ 
	ciṟṟaṭip puṟattil vaippiṉum avainiṉ 
		cīraruṭ koppeṉa lāmō? 
	maṟṟitai yuṇarvēṉ ! eṉiṉumin nūlniṉ 
	malarppatat turimaicey taṉeṉē.

ceṉṉai  mānila muṉṉāḷ kalviyamaiccar
uyar tiru. ti. cu. aviṉāciliṅkam avarkaḷ
maṉamuvantu aḷitta 

aṇinturai
oru nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ mēṉmaikku aṟikuṟiyāka iruppatu avarkaḷ moḻiyiṉ ciṟappu, piṟa moḻikaḷiṉ mūlam aṟivu peṟalām. āṉāl maṉap paṇpum cīriya cintaiyum tam conta moḻiyai āḻntu payiluvataṉāltāṉ peṟa muṭiyum. eṉavē, nam nāṭu putu vāḻvu peṟṟip peru vāḻvai nōkkic cellum ikkālattil namatu moḻiyiṉ vaḷarcciyaippaṟṟi aṟiñarkaḷ niṉaippatil āccariyam illai.
moḻiyiṉ vaḷarcci eṉpatu veṟum vāyppēccāl maṭṭilum uṇṭāvatillai. ataṟkāka moḻiyiṉpāl pakti tūṇṭappeṟṟu māḻiyiṉ cēvaiyē payaṉum paṇpum eṉakkaruti uḻaippavarkaḷiṉ muyaṟciyiṉāltāṉ aḵtu uṇṭākum. ellāt tuṟaikaḷilum nalla tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ veḷivara vēṇṭum. entat tamiḻaṉum tamiḻilēyē paṭittu ettakaiya uyarnta ñāṉattaiyum peṟa vēṇṭum. tamiḻil varum puttakaṅkaḷ ārampattil moḻi peyarppāka amaiyalām. āṉāl, moḻi peyarppu nūlkaḷ mutaltaramāṉavaiyāka irukka muṭiyātu. mutaltaramāṉa puttakaṅkaḷāka irukak vēṇṭumāṉāl, makkaḷ piṟatuṟai aṟivait tāṅkaḷē peṟṟu avvaṟivuṭaṉ oṉṟi ataṉ piṉṉar avvaṟivu avarkaḷiṉ conta aṉupavamāy veḷivara vēṇṭum. ittakaiya puttakaṅkaḷ ellā aṟivut tuṟaikaḷilum ippoḻutu mikamikat tēvai.
in nōkkaṅkaḷai niṟaivēṟṟuvataṟkākap palavitamāṉa muyaṟcikaḷ ippoḻutu mēṟkoḷḷappeṟṟu varuvatu mikavum makiḻcci tarakkūṭiyatu. tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakam veḷiyiṭum kalaikkaḷañciyam  ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu. kalaikkatir pōṉṟa māta veḷiyīṭukaḷum piṟa pattirikaikaḷum aṟivut tuṟaiyil kaṭṭuraikaḷ, viḷakkaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai veḷiyiṭuvatu maṟṟoṉṟu. ivai tavira, pala tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ peṟṟa ñāṉattai eḷiya tamiḻil, aṉaivarum uṇarattakka muṟaiyil, eḻuti varuvatum mikavum nampikkai tarakkūṭiya nilaimaiyākum.
kalvit tuṟaiyil pāṭa pōtaṉaiyiṉ taram uyara vēṇṭu meṉpatu aṉaivaruṭaiya viruppam.  namatu uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷil pāṭapōtaṉai tamiḻil amaintirukkiṉṟatu. āṉāl, intap pāṭaṅkaḷaip pōtikkum āciriyarkaḷukkup payiṟciyum pāṭamum āṅkilattil aḷikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. piṉṉar avarkaḷ paḷḷikaḷil mēṟkoḷḷavirukkum paṇikaḷil avarkaḷ peṟṟa aṟivum payiṟciyum muṟṟilum payaṉpaṭāmal pōkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, mutalil āciriyark kallūrikaḷil pāṭaṅkaḷ tamiḻil collittarappaṭa vēṇṭiyatu mikavum avaciyam. itaṟkut tēvaiyāṉa puttakaṅkaḷ tamiḻil illaiyē eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. intak kuṟaiyai nīkka tiru. cuppu reṭṭiyār muṉ vantiruppatu pārāṭṭat takkatu.
avar eḻutiyirukkum tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟai periya aḷavu nūlāka amaintirukkiṉṟatu. atai muṟṟilum pārkka eṉakku nēramum ōyvum kiṭṭavillai. eṉṟālum, pārtta aḷavil tāy moḻip payiṟciyait tavira attuṭaṉ toṭarpāka irukkavēṇṭiya navīṉa muṟaikaḷ paṟṟiya piṟa ceytikaḷum cērkkap peṟṟiruppatāka aṟikiṉṟēṉ. avaruṭaiya puttakam āciriyar payiṟcik kallūrikaḷilum āciriyar payiṟcip paḷḷikaḷilum payiṟci peṟum māṇakkarkaḷukkup peritum payaṉpaṭum eṉṟu nampukiṉṟēṉ.
iṉṟu namatu uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷil pāṭapōtaṉai tamiḻil amaintirukkiṉṟatu. itaṉ kāraṇamāka avarkaḷuk kirukkum āṅkila māḻiyiṉ ñāṉam ceṉṟa talaimuṟaiyil āṅkilam pōtaṉāmoḻiyāka irunta kālattil iruntamātiri illai eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. āṉāl, palatuṟaikaḷilum aṟivuttaram mikavum uyarntirukkiṉṟateṉpatu pārapaṭca millāmal pārppavarkaḷ aṉaivarukkum teḷivākak teriyum. tām paṭippatu, āṅkilattaip paṭippatukūṭa etaṟkāka? pala tuṟaikaḷilum aṟivu peṟuvataṟkāka, tāymoḻimūlam kalvi pukaṭṭuvatutāṉ kuṟainta kālattil atika aṟivu peṟuvataṟ kēṟṟa vaḻi eṉpatu ulaka muḻuvatilumuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṉupavam. āṉāl, iṉṉum ēṉ kallūrikaḷilum pōtaṉā muṟai tāymoḻi mūlam amaiyavillai eṉṟa viṉā eḻukiṉṟatu.
intak kāraṇaṅkaḷai iṅku ārāyavēṇṭiya avaciyam illai. iṉṟillaiyāṉāl, nāḷai, allatu oṉṟiraṇṭu, allatu aintāṇṭukaḷil paṭippaṭiyākat tamiḻ pōtaṉā moḻiyāvatu uṟuti. puttakaṅkaḷ illaiyeṉṟu collu kiṉṟavarkaḷ, atu pōtaṉā muṟaiyāṉālum, veḷivarum puttakaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭu āccariyappaṭuvārkaḷ.
	āṉāl, ataṟku muṉṉamēyē ip puttakaṅkaḷai eḻuta muṉ vantiruppavarkaḷ mikavum pārāṭṭutalukkuriyavarkaḷ. tiru. cuppu reṭṭiyār mikunta ciramattuṭaṉum cirattaiyuṭaṉum paktiyuṭaṉum ip paṇiyaic ceytirukkiṉṟār. tamiḻ aṉṉaiyiṉpāl tāṉ koṇṭuḷḷa pēraṉpāl, tamiḻakkuc cēvai ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟa pērārvattāl tūṇṭap peṟṟu tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟai eṉṟa innūlai eḻutiyirukkiṉṟār. avar paṇi aṉaivarukkum payaṉpaṭuvatākuka. avar mēṟkoṇṭirukkum muyaṟcikaḷil āṇṭavaṉ tuṇai niṟpāṉāka.
					iṅṅaṉam,
ti. cu. aviṉāciliṅkam. 

srī rāmakiruṣṇa miṣaṉ vityālayam, 
periyanāyakkaṉ pāḷaiyam, 
30-9-57.
FOREWORD
Professor N. Subbu Reddiar has done much more than merely put out one more book on the pedagogy of Tamil. Most of the books on the teaching of Tamil appear to present techniques and procedures ill-suited to the teaching of the mother-tongue, based on ideas appropriate to the teaching of foreign languages. Professor Reddiar has furnished almost the first book on Tamil teaching based on principles most appropriate to it. I know that he has developed many of these principles from his careful studies of Tamil grammar and prosody. It is a pity that most previous writers have ignored the basic material contained in the Tolkappiam which, incidentally, is a mine of information on the pedagogy of Tamil. Professor Reddiar has broken new ground by resisting the temptation to which most earlier writers have succumbed, and resuscitated those educational principles and procedures outlined in Classical Tamil Literature that are still valid according to the criteria of what goes by the name of Progressive Education. The Principles handled in the book are illustrated by apt examples from Tamil literature. As Professor Reddiar has given me a carte blanche in respect of the length of the Foreword, I am permitting myself to attempt an analysis of the original contribution he has made to the teaching of Tamil, chiefly by way of helping teachers of other Indian languages to perceive the possibility of new dynamic approaches to the teaching of their own languguages.
	Professor Reddiar has identified the objectives of the learning of the mother-tongue from the mass of general objectives which hold good for all languages. He has rightly emphasised the role of the mother-tongue in providing wholesome emotional sustenance to the growing learners. The distinction between a foreign language and the mother-tongue seems to lie in the possibility of greater emotionalisation through the mother-tongue. The mother-tongue patently serves the functional purpose of communication ; but a subtle principle implied in its character, which is often missed is, that it conveys something most intimate to the life and experience of the learner, while the experiences gained through a foreign language are exotic and vicarious, though sensible by the process of comparison and imagination. The art of translation must remain an imperfect art even with the most perfect of multi-linguists because, while words may be translatable for practical purposes, most concepts must remain untranslated. It is not the perfect nature of the translation in any accredited translation of a great work that helps one to see the beauty of the original in the translation. The superior education and imagination of the reader and the circumstances of his being a good student of the original make him see in the translation what does not really exist ; viz., the beauty of the original. G. U. Pope’s translation of the Kural, for instance, can never be as perfect as Tiruvalluvar’s Tamil Kural. But if Pope is remembered better than any other translator of the Kural it is because the translation has literary value in English. It seems to be an axiom that to enjoy a work of literature there can be no short-circuiting of the process by reading it in translation. There is as much difference between an original work and its best translation as there is between an actual visit to a place and seeing the place in a three-dimensional picture.
	I have said so much about translation only to come back to the position that the study of the mother-tongue is most necessary for the proper building up of the emotions of the children. Psychic imbalance, lack of character, a negative, nihilistic attitude and frustration, result from a wrong approach to the question of the education of the emotions. It seems to be true that character is the product of a good knowledge of the mother-tongue. The Foreign language can undoubtedly enlarge the imaginative horizon of the learner, and add to his fund of knowledge; but it can never substitute the mother-tongue as the most nourishing sustenance for emotional development.
Professor Reddiar has developed this theme in the chapter on ‘Objectives’ by discussing the personality values of the mother-tongue. The mother-tongue is the entrance to the true life of the community which has a direct and permanent influence on the individual. It is a means of enriching one’s social usefulness and culture. It is also a means of self-correction and self-education. Language necessarily leads to literature, and also to the whole literary heritage. A thorough grasp of the mother-tongue is a means to participation in the pride and glory of several generations which the heritage mirrors up before the eager participant. A nation of character can be guaranteed if the mother-tongue is given the first place in the educational system, and every citizen is helped to participate in the enjoyment of the literary heritage of the mother-tongue.
Discussing the value of oral work, Professor Reddiar has rightly emphasised the role of questioning as an important technique in the promotion of oral competencies. I remember a teacher of mine in the Columbia University telling me that the correct measure of the best class-room teaching is the skill to use the question technique. How many teachers use the technique for any other purpose than for killing time ruthlessly? Good questioning is mutual inspring. While the teachers’ questions, in the ideal set-up, result in both learning and internalisation of knowledge, the students’ questions, whatever they are, result in improved teaching. Questioning is not the prerogative of the teacher. Unfortunately, in the Western pedagogical tradition, questioning is indicated mostly as a weapon in the hands of the teacher. Questioning, in its technical connotation, means mostly the questioning by the teacher. There is very little said about student-questioning. No doubt, in recent times, especially after Dewey codified the scattered bits of the democratic philosophy of education and made it the basis of a number of practicable class-room techniques, American teachers have realised the educational value of student-questioning. But it was only in India that the true value of questioning as an educational technique was developed. The concepts of vina and prasna are at least as old as the earliest books in Tamil and Sanskrit literatures extant. Much in the heritage of Tamil literature has been the inspiration of student-questioning. There is the convention of many books being in the question-answer form ; and one would suspect that the convention itself developed from a real practice. Parvati is the questioning student par excellence, and Iswara is the answering teacher par excellence, be it Astrology, Astronomy, Medicine, Physics or Metaphysics. The author has done very well by giving this technique the pride of place in oral work.
Another aspect of oral work which has come in for detailed attention at the hands of our author relates to the need for speech education as an integral part of language education. Not many, even among Tamilians, may know that the Tamil language is among the first languages of the world to have systematised its phonetics in the manner found in the Tolkappiam and later gramatical works. Ignorance of this has led to disastrous consequences. Though, unlike in Sanskrit, the letter symbols of Tamil do not cover all sounds, some of the letters in combination in a word do have, sounds other than the basic ones for which they stand in the alphabetical system. Thus, we write in Tamil Ka, m, pa, n. In combination as a word unit we do not pronounce it or transliterate it as ‘Kampan’ but as ‘Kamban’. Another instance is Pa, n, ta, m. This is not transliterated as ‘Pantam’ but as ‘Pandam’, Ko, n, tu, is not to be transliterated as ‘Kontu’ but as ‘Kondu’. Va, n, t, aa, n is not ‘Vantaan’ but ‘Vandaan’. We could multiply instances in respect of the multiple sound-purposes of certain letters in combination in words. Professor Reddiar has devoted the requisite length to the importance of the ‘Oli Iyal’ in Tamil. I am not aware of any other scholar in Tamil Who has emphasised the need for codifying the phonetic principles of Tamil as they are available in the earliest grammatical works ; and the day may not be far off when Professor Reddiar’s inspiration produces a Daniel Jones for Tamil.
The discussion on oral and silent reading is really scholarly. The author has distinguished three types of reading: developmental, recreational and functional. Language education is, to a large extent, self-education in so far as it relates to the mother-tongue. The role of the teacher will be as much a negative one as it would be a positive one. The negative aspect of the teaching work will include the undoing of the wrong influences of the home and the community in speech and writing. The mother-tongue, in its spoken form at home and in the community, is not only full of colloquialisms but also regional variations. Before my visit to England I had imagined that in the whole of the British language there was one uniform way of speaking in English Languages. But when I visited the different parts of the country, I discovered there were regional variations. I was left wondering which was the standard form. In the United States where English has been accepted as the language of the nation with certain modifications, the variations in speech result from two factors. One is, that the Americans have developed a basic speech pattern with inflections, modulations and pauses, determined more or less uniformly. Another is, that even within this uniformity there is influence of the original mother-tongue of the community. A predominantly Italian community domiciled in an area shows itself up by the way it speaks the national language which has also become the community’s mother tongue. There can be no denying the fact that there are regional variations in respect of Tamil speech also. Certain mistakes of speech are perpetuated in each area, and Professor Reddiar has listed these. The function of the teacher is to wean the learner from such incorrect habits as may be inculcated in the home and community environment of the learner. The positive role related to the inculcation of the right language habits. But even the best teaching by the ablest teacher can only be a beginning. It must be completed by the reading of the three types of reading material suggested by Professor Reddiar. No modern educationist will underrate the value of library reading as integral to the process of learning. What the teacher initiates, the library completes. The pity of it, however, is that, even in the year of grace 1964, the library is considered a luxury in not a small number of high schools in Tamilnad. A year ago I assisted in a survey of libraries, and the revelation shocked me. Only a few schools could boast of anything like a library. Some schools have a few almirahs containing books most of which are of no use either to the teachers or to the students. Even if we should concede that a collection of books, whatever they are, is a library, and apply this definition to defend an unhappy situation in our schools, there can be no escaping the fact that the number of good Tamil books in the libraries of most schools can be counted on the fingers of both hands. The current harvest in the field of Tamil literature is as rich as it should be. Excellent reading material has been put out in recent years in Tamil; but very few schools have bestowed any thought on the question of organizing a good Tamil section in the library.
The section on Tamil literature appears to be the best part of the book. I am particularly impressed with the author’s treatment of poetry. He rightly emphasizes the role of poetry as an instrument of educating the emotions. He warns the pedagogical surgeons among Tamil Pandits against performing purposeless surgery on beautiful poems in the name of grammar and paraphrase. Poetry is not for dissection; it is for enjoyment. It is one of the major ingredients in aesthetic education.
In this connection, I would commend the following books of Professor Reddiar for careful study; Kavingnan Ullam, Kalingathupparani Aaraichi, Kaathal Oviyangal, and Kavithai Anubhavam.
This place of grammar, the need to promote expression through the written word and the development of creative writing in Tamil have received really expert treatment at the author’s hands. I am fascinated by the section on ‘Teacher Planning’ in which he advocates the preparation of a blue-print of the whole year’s work by the teacher. I should like to congratulate the learned author on his masterly treatment of the concept of Evaluation in its appropriateness to Tamil.
Profesor Reddiar has not, however, discarded whais of essence in the writings of experts in Linguistics. The merit of his work lies in its eclectic approach, coalescing what is still valid in the traditional approach and what Western educational thinking has to furnish as its cream. The bibliography at the end of the book is the key to the way the author’s mind must have worked at the time of his writing the book.
The glossary of technical terms, many of which he has coined, must prove the forerunner to more exhaustive glossaries.
I am thankful to Professor Reddiar for the honour he has done me by asking me to write the Foreword to his magnum opus. The value of this work is bound to increase with the greater realization of the new contribution it has made and the new vistas it has opened up to the teachers of Tamil for whom it is primarily intended.

S. THIRUVENKATACHARI
Professor of Education
Dr. Alagappa Chettiar Training College.
Karaikudi,
20-2-1964

nūl  mukam
	nāṭip pulaṅkaḷ uḻuvār karamum nayavuraikaḷ
tēṭik koḻikkum kavivāṇar nāvum ceḻuṅkaruṇai
ōṭip perukum aṟivāḷar neñcum uvantunaṭam
āṭik kaḷikkum mayilē! uṉ pātam aṭaikkalē! 
î tēcika vināyakam piḷḷai
tāy moḻiyiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyai ellāt tuṟaikaḷi luḷḷavarkaḷum uṇarntu varukiṉṟaṉar.  makkaḷāṭciyil tāy moḻi perumpaṅku koḷḷavēṇṭiya ciṟantatoru karuviyākum.  kōṉāṭciyiṉ nōkkam vēṟu; kuṭiyāṭciyiṉ nōkkam vēṟu. kōṉāṭciyil āḷvōr tamāṇaiyaik kuṭimakkaḷiṭam aṭaiyac ceyvatu; kuṭiyāṭciyil makkaḷ tamviruppattai āḷvōr aṟiyacceyvatu. itaṟku makkaḷ aṟinta moḻitāṉ takkatoru karuviyāka irukka muṭiyum. ēṉavē, aṉaivarum tāy moḻiyil teḷivākap pēcavum eḻutavum āṟṟal peṟavēṇṭum eṉpataik kūṟavā vēṇṭum? itai mukkiya nōkkamākak koṇṭu toṭakka, iṭai, uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟum tāymoḻi āciriyarkaḷ moḻiyaik kaṟpikka vēṇṭum; tāy moḻiyilēyē ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷum kaṟpikkappeṟa vēṇṭi yatāyiruttaliṉ piṟapāṭa āciriyarkaḷum in nōkkattai niṉaivil vaittuk koṇṭu paṇiyāṟṟavēṇṭum.
ip puttakam tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟaiyaik kūṟuvatu. uḷaviyal uṇmaikaḷai aṭippaṭaiyākakkoṇṭu mēṟpula aṟiñarkaḷ ettaṉaiyō payiṟṟu muṟaikaḷaik kaṇṭaṟin tuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟaiyellām moḻiyiṉ marapu keṭātu ententa aḷavil nantamiḻ moḻiyaik kaṟpippatil mēṟkoḷḷalām eṉpatai ōraḷavu ārāyntuḷḷēṉ. potuvāka ellā āciriyarkaḷum, ciṟappākat tamiḻāciriyarkaḷum, kalvit tuṟaiyil paṇiyāṟṟum piṟarum, potumakkaḷum cintikkavēṇṭiya pala karuttukkaḷ itil kuṟippiṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaiyē muṭinta muṭipeṉṟu aṟutiyiṭṭu uraittēṉillai. avaciyam ēṟpaṭil māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceytukoḷḷalām; ceytu koḷḷattāṉ vēṇṭum. tāymoḻiyaṟivu cemmaiyāka vāyttāltāṉ āṭci pīṭattil amarntiruppōrukkum nāṭṭu makkaḷukkum iṭaiyil nalla toṭarpu ēṟpaṭum mēlum araciyal pōkkai makkaḷ naṉkuṇaravum, taṅkaḷ viruppattai araciṉarukkup pulappaṭuttikkoḷḷavum iyalum. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, tāy moḻiyiṉ vaḷantāṉ araciṉarukkum makkaṭkum nēraṭiyāṉa toṭarpai uṇṭākka muṭiyum.
tāymoḻiyai ellōrum eḷitil kaṟṟukkoḷḷalāmē; piṟanta cila āṇṭukaḷil kuḻantai, tāṉ vāḻum cūḻnilaiyil peṟṟōr, uṟṟār, uṟaviṉar camūkam ākiyōriṉ mūlamākat tāymoḻiyai mika viraivākak kaṟṟukkoḷvatiliruntu ivvuṇmai teḷivākumē; ivvaḷavu eḷitākakak kaṟkakkūṭiya tāymoḻiyaik kaṟpittaṟku ivvaḷavu ārppāṭṭam tēvaiyillaiyē eṉṟu cilar karutalām; uṇmaiyākap palar avvāṟutāṉ karutukiṉṟaṉar. oru kuḻantaiyō allatu paṭikkātu vaḷarntavarō tam aṉṟāṭat tēvaikkākak kaṟkum nilaivēṟu; pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākap paṇpaṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa vaḷam paṭaitta moḻiyai aṟiviyal nilaiyil vaittuk kaṟpatu vēṟu; eṉpatai, eṇṇip pārkkum aṉaivarum eḷitil uṇarvar. uḷaviyal uṇmaikaḷ palavaṟṟaik kaṇṭaṟinta piṟaku iḵtu iṉṉum teḷivākap pulaṉākiyuḷḷatu.
nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷākavē navīṉa muṟaiyil tamiḻ kaṟpippataip paṟṟi oru puttakam eḻuti veḷiyiṭa eṇṇiyiruntēṉ. ōṉpatu āṇṭukaḷ uyarnilaip paḷḷiyil talaimaiyāciriyaṉāka iruntapoḻutu nēril kavaṉitta palvēṟu aṉupavaṅkaḷaik koṇṭum, eṭṭu āṇṭukaḷ pi. ṭi. māṇākkarkaṭkut tamiḻ kaṟpippataip paṟṟip pōtittuvanta aṉupavattaik koṇṭum ipputtakam eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷatu. eḻuti muṭikka iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ āyiṉa. ipputtakattil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa pala karuttukkaḷaiyum naṉku cintittu naṭaimuṟaiyil ceyalukkuk koṇṭuvantāl māṇākkarkaḷiṭam tamiḻmoḻiyaṟivu naṉmuṟaiyil amaivatu uṟuti eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ; nampikkaiyum koḷkiṉṟēṉ. muṟaikaḷaik kūṟuvatu eḷitu; avaṟṟai naṭaimuṟaikkuk koṇṭuvantu; veṟṟi kāṇpatu eṉpatu moḻi kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ mēṟkoḷḷum muyaṟciyaiyum, avarkaḷiṉ tiṟaṉaiyum, īṭupāṭṭaiyum poṟuttuḷḷatu.
ipputtakam patiṉāṟu attiyāyaṅkaḷākap pirikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. mutal attiyāyattil tamiḻ kaṟpippataṉ nōkkaṅkaḷ ōraḷavu virivāka ārāyappeṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭāvatu attiyāyattil innōkkaṅkaḷ naṭaimuṟaiyil niṟaivēṟuvataṟkāṉa pāṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyum; naṭai muṟaiyiluḷḷa toṭakka, iṭai, uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷil kalvi peṟum māṇākkarkaḷ payilavēṇṭiya moḻikaḷpaṟṟiyum viḷakkam koṭukkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu. mūṉṟiliruntu āṟu muṭiyavuḷḷa attiyāyaṅkaḷil payiṟṟuvataṟku mēṟkoḷḷap peṟum palvēṟu muṟaikaḷaippaṟṟiya vitikaḷum, vivaraṅkaḷum tarappeṟṟiruppatuṭaṉ; tamiḻmoḻi payiṟṟuṅkāl avaṟṟai evvevvāṟu kaiyāḷalām eṉpatum viḷakkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu. ēḻiliruntu paṉṉiraṇṭu muṭiyavuḷḷa attiyāyaṅkaḷil mēṟkūṟappeṟṟa vitikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟait tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ naṭaimuṟaiyil evvevvāṟu kaiyāḷalām eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷuṭaṉ viḷakkap paṟṟirukkiṉṟatu. ivai yāvum muṭinta muṭipukaḷ eṉṟu evarum niṉaittal vēṇṭā. ivaṟṟai naṭaimuṟaiyil koṇṭuvarumpoḻututāṉ uṇmaiyāṉa caṅkaṭaṅkaḷ teṉpaṭum. muṟaikaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṟkēṟpa māṟṟiyamaittuk koḷḷavum nēriṭum. patiṉmūṉṟāvatu attiyāyattil māṇākkarkaḷiṭam nalla kaiyeḻuttu amaivatupaṟṟiyum avarkaḷ eḻuttu vēlaikaḷil kāṇakkūṭiya piḻaikaḷpaṟṟiyum avaṟṟait tavirppatupaṟṟiyum cila karuttukkaḷ terivikkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. patiṉāṉkāvatu attiyāyattil tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ tam paṇiyait tiṟamaiyuṭaṉ āṟṟuvataṟku vakuttukkoḷḷavēṇṭiya tiṭṭaṅkaḷpaṟṟiyum pāṭak kuṟippukkaḷiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaipaṟṟiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷuṭaṉ viḷakkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu. patiṉaintāvatu attiyāyattil paḷḷiyil payaṉpaṭṭu varum puttakaṅkaḷiṉ vakaikaḷaiyum, nalla puttakaṅkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷaiyum paṟṟiya karuttukkaḷum, tuṇaippāṭap puttakaṅkaḷaip payiṟṟum muṟaikaḷum, nūlakap paṭippai mēṟkoḷḷum vaḻikaḷum ōraḷavu viḷakkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. navīṉa muṟaikaḷaik kaiyāṇṭu kaṟpippataṉāl tamiḻmoḻi kaṟpataṉ nōkkaṅkaḷaiyoṭṭi māṇākkar peṟum moḻiyaṟivaiyum ilakkiya aṟivaiyum navīṉa aḷaviyal muṟaikaḷaikkoṇṭē aḷappatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. cariyāṉa aḷavu kōlaikkoṇṭu aḷantāltāṉ uṇmaiyāṉa nilai pulaṉākum. uḷaviyaṟkalai vaḷarcciyiṉ viḷaivāka kalvittuṟai aḷaviyal kuṟittu virivāṉa nūloṉṟait tamiḻil eḻuta eṇṇiyuḷḷēṉ. aḷaviyalpaṟṟiya ellāk karuttukkaḷum atil tokuttuk kūṟappeṟum.
puttakattiṉ iṟutiyil  ip puttakattil mēṟkoṇṭa kalaiccoṟkaḷum puttakam eḻutap payaṉpeṟṟa nūlkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum poruṭ kuṟippu akarātiyum piṉṉiṇaippukkaḷākac cērkkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. kalaiccoṟkaḷ āṅkilattil paṭikkum payiṟṟu muṟaikaḷait tamiḻmoḻiyil amaittuk kōṭalukkum, mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷ ipputtakattaip paṭippōrai mēlum paṭippilkoṇṭu celuttuvataṟkum, poruṭ kuṟippu akarāti pala ceytikaḷai avaravar virumpukiṟavaṇṇam tokuttaṟivutaṟkum tuṇaiyāka irukkum eṉṟē avai cērkkappeṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa.
puttakam eḻuti muṭintu accuvākaṉam ēṟuvataṟkumuṉ eṉ ariya naṇparum aḻakappā payiṟcik kallūri mutalvarumāṉa tiru. pa. turaikkaṇṇu mutaliyār avarkaḷ pirati muḻuvataiyum mikap poṟumaiyuṭaṉ paṭittup pārttuc cila kuṟippukkaḷaik kūṟiṉārkaḷ. puttakam accākum   poḻutu purūp; pārttu palavita tiruttaṅkaḷaic ceytu puttakattiṉ akattōṟṟamum puṟattōṟṟamum aḻakāka amaivataṟku mutaṟ kāraṇamāka iruntavar eṉ naṇpar tiru. e. tiruvēṅkaṭāccāri avarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ iruvarukkum eṉ uḷaṅkaṉinta naṉṟi.
ip puttakam eḻutik koṇṭiruntapoḻutu nōyvāyppaṭṭēṉ. uṭkārntu eḻuta muṭiyāta nilaiyil iruntēṉ. ēṉ arumai māṇākkarāyirunta tiru. ci. irāmacāmi pi. ō.el.,pi.ṭi., (ippoḻutu teṉkāciyil tamiḻāciriyarākap paṇiyāṟṟupavar) yāṉ collac colla eḻuti utaviṉār. kiṭṭattaṭṭa kaiyeḻuttuppaṭiyil pāti avar kaiyeḻut tilē amaintatu. avarukkum atupōla poruṭ kuṟippu akarāti tokuppatu pōṉṟa piṟavakaikaḷil tuṇai ceyta piṟa māṇākkarkaḷukkum eṉ naṉṟi urittākum.
ip puttakam accu vaṭivam peṟuvataṟkup pallāṟṟāṉum utaviyaruḷiyavar kampaṉ pukaḻ pāṭik kaṉṉit tamiḻvaḷarppavarum; eṉmītu aḷavaṟṟa aṉpukāṭṭi varupavarumāṉa uyartiru. cā kaṇēcaṉ avarkaḷ. avarkaṭku ellāvaṟṟālum eṉṟeṉṟum kaṭamaippaṭṭirukkiṉṟēṉ. tiru. kaṇēcaṉ avarkaḷ ellaiyaṟṟa tamiḻppaṟṟuḷḷavar; tamiḻ moḻi ellāvita vaḷattaiyum peṟavēṇṭum eṉṟu tuṭittu niṟpavar; tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarcciyilum ataṉaik kaṟpittayilum aṭippaṭaiyāṉa māṟutalkaḷai avāvi niṟpavar; tamiḻmoḻi āṅkila moḻipōl viraivil vaḷampeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu ācai koḷpavar. ittakaiya uyar karuttukkaḷaikkoṇṭa oru pēraṉpar eṉṉai ūkkuvittu innūl veḷivaruvataṟku utaviṉār eṉṟāl ataṉai nāṉ peṟṟa pēṟu eṉṟē karutukiṉṟēṉ. aṉṉārukku eṉ naṉṟi kalanta vaṇakkam urittākum.
uyartiru. ti. cu. aviṉāciliṅkam avarkaḷai aṟiyāta tamiḻ makkaḷē irukka muṭiyātu; tamiḻ moḻikkum kalvikkum avar ceyta, ceytu varukiṉṟa toṇṭukaḷai tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟil poṉṉeḻuttukkaḷāl poṟikka vēṇṭum. ellaiyaṟṟa tamiḻk kātalu ṭaiya ap periyār innūlukku muṉṉurai vaḻaṅki āci kūṟiyatai yāṉpeṟṟa perumpēṟu eṉṟē karutukiṉṟēṉ. ap periyārukku eṉ uḷaṅkaṉinta naṉṟi eṉṟum uriyatu.
ip puttakattai acciṭuvatāl nēriṭum perum poruṭ celavaiyum poruṭpaṭuttātu itai maṉamuvantu ēṟṟu mikaviraivil acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭṭavarkaḷ tiruvāḷarkaḷāṉa e. ār. cuppiramaṇya piḷḷai patippakattār; kālattiṟku vēṇṭiya pala tamiḻ nūlkaḷai aḻakuṟa acciṭṭut tamiḻp paṇiyāṟṟukiṉṟavarkaḷ avarkaḷ. avarkaṭku eṉ maṉamuvanta naṉṟi urittākum.
mūṉṟāṇṭup paruvattilēyē tantaiyai iḻantavaṉ yāṉ. tantaiyōṭu kalvi pōm eṉṟu periyōr kūṟuvar. eṉṟālum, pala iṉṉalkaḷukkiṭaiyē ciṟitum taḷarātu  ammaiyāka iruntu eṉṉai vaḷarttum appaṉāka iruntu kalvi payiṟṟuvittum ammaiyum appaṉumāka iruntu eṉakku ellā nalaṉkaḷai aruḷiyum eṉṉai in nilaikkuk koṇṭuvantu kaṇṭu makiḻum eṉ arumai aṉṉaiyiṉ poṉṉār tiruvaṭikaṭku innūlaic camarppit tuḷḷēṉ. tāymoḻiyaic ciṟārkaḷiṭam vaḷamāka vaḷarkkum vaḻiyaik kūṟum tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟai yām in nūlait tāykkuc camarppippatu mikavum poruttam eṉṟu karutiyē avvāṟu ceytuḷḷēṉ.
ēṉakkuḷḷa palavakaiyāṉa kuṟaikaḷāl ip puttakattila palavita kuṟaipāṭukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruttal kūṭum. cila karuttu vēṟupāṭukaḷum irukkalām. āvaṟṟai aṉparkaḷ poṟutturuḷumāṟu vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ. puttakattai aṉparkaḷ ūṉṟip paṭittuk kuṟaipāṭukaḷaiyum karuttu vēṟupāṭukaḷaiyum terivippārkaḷāyiṉ aṭutta patippil avaṟṟait tiruttik koḷvēṉ. ip puttakattai eḻuti veḷiyiṭuvataṟku yāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa muyaṟciyai niṟai vēṟṟivaittai ellām valla tiruvaruḷai vāḻtti vaṇaṅkukiṉṟēṉ.
kuṟṟam kaḷaintu kuṟaipeytu vācittal
kaṟṟaṟinta māntar kaṭaṉ
na. cuppu reṭṭiyār,

aḻakappā payiṟcik kallūri kāraikkuṭi
akṭōpar 1, 1957
tiruntiya (iraṇṭām) patippu
nūliṉ aṭippaṭaik karuttukkaḷil atikamāṟṟam oṉṟum illai. kalaiccoṟkaḷilum iyal amaippukkaḷilum māṟṟam ceyyappeṟṟuḷḷatu. naṭaiyilum aṭikkuṟippukkaḷilum tiruttam ceyyappeṟṟuḷḷatu. nūliṉ uṭalil iṭaiyiṭaiyē cila āṅkilac coṟkaḷ viḷakkattiṟkāka nuḻaikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
	intappatippiṉai eṉ ariya naṇpar pērāciriyar e. tiruvēṅkaṭāc cāriyār avarkaḷiṉ āṅkila muṉṉurai aṇiceykiṉṟatu. pērāciriyar āccāriyār avarkaḷuṭaṉ patiṉāṉku āṇṭukaḷ neruṅkip paḻakiyuḷḷēṉ; pattu āṇṭukaḷ aḻakappā āciriyar payiṟcikkallūriyil avaruṭaṉ cērntup paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷēṉ. irupattaintu āṇṭukaṭkumēl kalvittuṟaiyil pala nilaikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟi aṉupavam peṟṟa eṉ naṇpar iṅkilāntu, amerikkā pōṉṟa mēṉāṭukaḷil pala kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷaip pārvaiyiṭṭuk kalvipaṟṟiya pala karuttukkaḷai aṟintavar. vaṭamoḻip pulamai mikkavar; tamiḻilum nalla payiṟciyuṭaiyavar. ēntap piracciṉaiyaiyum nuṇukiyaṟintu teḷivākappiṟarkku uṇarttum peṟṟiyar. āṅkilattilum aruntamiḻilum  kēṭṭār piṇikkum takaiyarāyk kēḷārum vēṭpa moḻiyum colvaṉmai vāyntavar. pala nūlkaḷiṉ āciriyar; ciṟanta āyvāḷar. ittakaiya eṉkeḻutakai naṇpar inta nūlukku āṅkila muṉṉurai vaḻaṅkiyamaikku eṉ uḷaṅkaṉinta naṉṟi eṉṟum uriyatu.
it tiruntiya patippu veḷivaruvataṟku eṉ muyaṟciyai niṟaivēṟṟi vaitta  paintamiḻp piṉ ceṉṟa paccaip pacuṅ koṇṭaliṉ tiruvaṭikaḷai niṉaintu pōṟṟukiṉṟēṉ.
na. cuppu reṭṭiyār, 
tiruppati 
15-3-64
tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟai
pirivu-1 : nōkkaṅkaḷ

oruvar piṟantatu mutal iṟakkum varai pēci varuvatu tāy moḻi.  kuḻantaiyiṉ iyalpāṉa moḻiyum atuvēyākum.  potuvāka nām namatu eṇṇaṅkaḷaip pēccu mūlamākavum eḻuttu mūlamākavum veḷiyiṭuvataṟkut tāymoḻi ciṟanta karuviyākap payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu.  atuvum viṭutalai peṟṟa nam nāṭṭil iṉṟu tāy moḻiyiṉ tāṇṭavattai emmaruṅkum kāṇalām. uyarnta maṉaveḻuccikaḷaiyum kaṟpaṉaiyāṟṟalaiyum koṇṭa tāymoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷaic cuvaippatupōl ayalmoḻi ilakkiyaṅkḷaic cuvaikka muṭiyātu.  tāymoḻi eḻiluṇar tiṟaṉaiyum, kaṟpaṉaiyāṟṟalaiyum naṉmuṟaiyil vaḷarkkiṉṟatu; vaḻi vaḻiyāka varum camūka iyalaiyum, paṇ pāṭṭaiyum, aṟiyum tiṟavukōlākavum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu; vāḻkkaiyai vaḷappaṭutti nītiyai iṉiya muṟaiyil pukaṭṭu kiṉṟatu.  tāymoḻi mūlam kalvi peṟuṅkāl kālac celavu. paṇac celavu. muyaṟci ākiyavai kuṟaikiṉṟaṉa.  tāy moḻi payilvatāl moḻip paṟṟum nāṭṭup paṟṟuṅkūṭa naṉmuṟaiyil amaikiṉṟaṉa.  ittakaiya nōkkaṅkaḷai inta iyal virivāka viḷakkukiṉṟatu.  ivaṟṟai niṟaivēṟṟa vēṇṭiya tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷiṉ poṟuppukaḷaiyum, avarkaḷ mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya muṟaikaḷaiyum, cuṭṭikkāṭṭuvatuṭaṉ tāymoḻik kalviyil piṟa pāṭa āciriyarkaḷiṉ paṅkiṉaiyum niṉaivūṭṭu kiṉṟatu ip pirivu.
1. tamiḻ moḻiyaip payiṟṟum nōkkaṅkaḷ
ivvulakil nuṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa moḻikaḷ pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. piṟantatu mutal iṟakkumvarai. toṭṭililiruntu cuṭukāṭuvarai, oruvar kēṭṭum, pēciyum, varum moḻiyē tāymoḻi eṉappaṭum.  kuḻantaikku iyalpāṉa moḻiyum atuvē.  oru tāy taṉ kuḻantaikkup pāluṭṭum poḻutum, tālāṭṭum poḻutum, cīrāṭṭip, pārāṭṭum poḻutum, pēcum moḻiyait tāṉē tāymoḻi eṉṟu collavēṇṭum?  ic ciṟappuk karutiyē namatu tāymoḻiyākiya tamiḻ moḻiyai āṉṟōr pālvāyp pacuntamiḻ eṉṟu ciṟappittuḷḷaṉar.
moḻi camūkattiltāṉ vaḷarccipeṟum:  moḻi iṉṟēl makkaḷ camūkamāka .iyaṅka muṭiyātu.  eṉavē oṉṟupaṭṭa potu vāḻkkaikku moḻi iṉṟiyamai yātatu eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil oruvarōṭu oruvar uṟavāṭuvataṟku moḻiyē ciṟanta karuviyāka amaikiṉṟatu.  cātāraṇa makkaḷ mutalil pēcat toṭaṅkiya tāy moḻiyaiyē oru ciṟanta karuviyākak koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.  potuvāka nām namatu eṇṇaṅkḷaip  pēccu mūlamākavum eḻuttu mūlamākavum veḷiyiṭuvataṟku namatu tāymoḻiyaiyē kaiyāḷukiṉṟōm.  palvēṟu vakaippaṭṭa koḷkaikaḷ, camayaṅkaḷ, poruḷātāra ēṟṟat tāḻvukaḷ, kulaṅkaḷ, nilaikaḷ, toḻilkaḷ, ākiyavaṟṟāl vēṟṟumaiyuṟṟa pala piriviṉarkaḷum pēcum tāymoḻiyāl oṉṟupaṭuvataik kāṇumpoḻutu tāymoḻiyiṉ pērāṟṟalai eṉṉaveṉṟu kūṟuvatu?!  ittakaiya ciṟappu vāynta tāymoḻi, kalvit tiṭṭattil mutaliṭam peṟuvatil viyappoṉṟum illai.  eṉavē, tāymoḻiyāciri yarkaḷ itaik kaṟpikkum muṉṉar, kaṟpikka vēṇṭiya nōkkaṅkaḷai, aṟintu kaṟpittal iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.  annōkkaṅkaḷ yāvai eṉpatai īṇṭu nōkkuvōm.
maṉattil uḷḷavaṟṟai veḷiyiṭal:  moḻiyiṉ mūlamākattāṉ namatu eṇṇaṅkaḷ, karuttukkaḷ, uṇrccikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟait terivikkiṉṟōm.  pēccālum eḻuttālum piṟar karuttukkaḷai aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu.  makkaḷiṉ potu vāḻkkaikku iṉṟiyamaiyāta tāymoḻiyaippayaṉ eytum muṟaiyil māṇakkarkaḷ tiṟamaiyuṭaṉ kaiyāḷak kūṭiyavāṟu kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.  mutalil vēṇṭappaṭuvatu teḷivē:  cariyākac colvatai aṭuttatākak koḷḷalām.  ivviṭattil jārj cāmcaṉ eṉṟa āṅkilak kalvi nipuṇar kūṟiyiruppatait tirumpak kūṟutal poruttamākum: iṅṅaṉam nuṇṇiya eṇṇaṅkaḷum teḷivāṉa karuttukkaḷum tōṉṟuvataṟku āciriyar māṇākkarkaḷiṭam (1)eḷiya muṟaiyil oru poruḷaik kuṟittut teḷivākap pēcavum. (2) teḷivāṉa muṟaiyil oruvar oru poruḷaippaṟṟik kēṭkavum kēṭṭup poruḷuṇaravum., (3) oru poruḷait teḷivāṉa muṟaiyil paṭikkavum, paṭittup poruḷuṇaravum (4) oru poruḷaippaṟṟit teḷivāṉa, eḷiya muṟaiyil, eḻutavumāṉa āṟṟalkaḷai uṇṭu paṇṇuvatākum.  piṟarukkuk kaṟpikkum āciriyariṭam ippaṇpukaḷ amaiya vēṇṭum1 tāymoḻiyil teḷivākap pēcavum, paṭikkavum, eḻutavumāṉa payiṟcikaḷaip paḷḷiyil aḷittu ivvāṟṟalkaḷai vaḷarttāl avarkaḷ vāḻkkait tuṟaiyil iṟaṅkic ceyalāṟṟum poḻutu avai peritum tuṇaiyāka irukkum.  makkaḷāṭci nilavum nam nāṭṭil oru ciṟanta kuṭimakaṉāka vāḻvataṟku ivvāṟṟalkaḷ mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātavai.  oru nalla kuṭimakaṉukkut teḷivāṉa cintaṉai, teḷivāṉa pēccu, teḷivāṉa eḻuttu ākiya tiṟaṉkaḷ vēṇṭappaṭupavai allavā?  vaḷḷuvap peruntakaiyum, 
	eṇporuḷ ākac celaccollit tāṉpiṟarvāy
 nuṇporuḷ kāṇpatu aṟivu. 2
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu īṇṭu cintittuṇaraṟpālatu.  tāymoḻi kaṟpikkum āciriyar ivvāṟṟalkaḷai vaḷarppatil takka kavaṉam celuttavēṇṭum.
tāymoḻiyil nalla pulamai peṟṟāltāṉ oruvar tam uṇarccikaḷaiyum, uyar karuttukkaḷaiyum teḷivāka veḷiyiṭa muṭiyum;   avaruṭaiya paṭaippāṟṟalilum oru vaḷarcciyaik kāṇa muṭiyum.  māṇākkarkaḷ muḻu vaḷarccikkum tāymoḻip payiṟciyē ciṟantatōr aṭippaṭaiyāka amaikiṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟalām.  tam uṇarccikaḷai veḷiyiṭuvatil avarkaḷukku oru taṉippaṭṭa makiḻcci ēṟpaṭuvatuṭaṉ, avarkaḷuṭaiya maṉam, oḻukkam, mutaliyavai paṇpaṭṭu āḷumai (Personality) ciṟakkavum iṭamuṇṭu.  tāymoḻiyil uḷḷa ciṟanta ceyyuṭkaḷ, kāviyaṅkaḷ, vēṟu pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷ maṉattai virivaṭaiyac ceytu avarkaḷuṭaiya taṉivīṟu ciṟakkavum vaḻikōlum.  ilakkiya āciriyarkaḷ  tam kāviya ulakil ulavac ceyyum kāviyamāntarkaḷiṭam māṇākkarkaḷ paḻaka vāyppukaḷ peṟumpoḻutu ammāntarkaḷiṉ ilaṭciya vāḻkkai avarkaḷ  vāḻvukku vaḻikāṭṭiyāka amaiyum.  ivvulaka  vāḻkkaiyil makkaḷ nāḷtōṟum kāṇa nēriṭum ēmāṟṟaṅkaḷ, ēkkaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum, avarkaḷiṭam aṭikkaṭi eḻum itayattuṭippukaḷ, iṉpakkaṉavukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum ilakkiya āciriyarkaḷ tām paṭaitta māntarkaḷiṭam niṟaivuceytu kāṭṭiyuḷḷataip paṭikkumpoḻutu avarkaḷ uḷḷam kaḷip paṭaikiṉtu:  maṉam amaitiyuṟu kiṉṟatu.  puṟanāṉūṟu pōṉṟa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil paṭaittiṟam vāynta peru vēntarkaḷaiyum, koṭaittiṟam mikka vaḷḷalkaḷaiyum, kaṟṟaṟintu aṭaṅkiya cāṉṟōr kaḷaiyum kāṇumpoḻutu iḷam māṇākkarkaḷiṉ etirkāla vāḻvu ciṟakkum vaḻikaḷ tōṉṟum; aṅkuk kāṇap peṟum nikaḻccikaḷ avarkaḷ vāḻvaic ciṟappikkavum kūṭum.
kaṭanta aimpatu āṇṭukaḷil makkaḷ vāḻkkaiyilum, karuttulakilum, aṉupavattilum ettaṉaiyō māṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.  avai makkaḷ maṉappāṉmaiyait talaikīḻāka māṟṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟalām.  putiya maṉappāṉmaiyiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil tōṉṟiyavaṟṟil mukkiya māṉatu  tāymoḻippaṟṟu.  viṭutalai peṟṟa nam nāṭṭil iḵtu emmaruṅkum kāṇappeṟum paṇpākak kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟatu.  iṉṟu eṅkum tamiḻ muḻakkam kēṭkappaṭukiṉṟatu; ellāt tuṟaikaḷilum tamiḻ maṇam kamaḻkiṉṟatu.  tēciya iyakkam tōṉṟiyatiliruntu tāymoḻi iyakkamum tōṉṟi vaḷarntu, nāṭu viṭutalai eytiya piṟaku aḵtu eṉṟumillāta putiya vēkattai iṉṟu peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu.  tāymoḻiyil eṇṇaṟṟa nāḷitaḻkaḷ, kiḻamai itaḻkaḷ, piṟaiitaḻkaḷ, tiṅkaḷitaḻkaḷ tōṉṟi vēkamākap paravi makkaḷ maṉataik kauvikkoṇṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa.  itukāṟum potumakkaḷ vāḻvuṭaṉ neruṅkic cērāmalirunta ilakkiya vāḻvu avarkaḷ vāḻvuṭaṉ cērat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu.  ilakkiyac cuvaiyum, kavitai iṉpamum pāmaramakkaḷukku illaiyeṉṟum, kaṟṟaṟinta pulavarkaḷukku maṭṭumē ilakkiyam uriya poruḷeṉṟum nāṭṭiṭaiyē vērūṉṟi nilaviyirunta karuttu māṟiviṭṭatu.  iṉṟu pāmaramakkaḷ maṉattiṉmītum ilakkiya vāṉam kavintu avarkaḷ cuvaittu aṉupavikkakkūṭiya muṟaiyil kavitai maḻai poḻiyavēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttu mella eḻuntu veṟṟiyum peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu.  ittuṟaiyil mutaṉmutalāka vaḻikōliya pāratiyiṉ aṭic cuvaṭṭil pala kaviñarkaḷ talaikāṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar.  tamiḻ urainaṭaiyum puttuṇarcci peṟṟup pāmararkaḷum tuykkum nilaiyil kataikaḷ, putiṉaṅkaḷ, nāṭakaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa putiya maṟumalarcci ilakkiya vakaikaḷ ērāḷamākat tōṉṟi varukiṉṟaṉa.
paṭakkāṭcikaḷ tāymoḻikku ēṟṟam aḷittirukkiṉṟaṉa.  kalaittuṟai yil tamiḻppaṭaṅkaḷ ciṟitu ciṟitāka muṉṉēṟi, eṇṇaṟṟa pēcum paṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi, eḻuttu maṇam aṟiyāta pāmaramakkaḷiṉ karuttulakattaic ciṟitu vaḷarttirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟē colla vēṇṭum.   ivai ciṟṟūrkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷuṭaiya naṭai uṭai pāvaṉaikaḷaiyum māṟṟiviṭṭaṉa.  nāḷitaḻkaḷilum piṟa veḷiyīṭukaḷilum veḷivarum viḷamparaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ nillātu tāymoḻi mūlam pala vaṇikaviḷamparaṅkaḷum pēcum paṭaṅkaḷ vaṭivil tōṉṟip pāmaramakkaḷiṉ karut tulakattaip paṉmukavirivaṭaiyac ceytuvarukiṉṟaṉa.  maruttuvam, cukātāram, kalvi, nāṭṭu naṭappukaḷ ākiya tuṟaikaḷilum ippēcum tamiḻpaṭaṅkaḷ peruntoṇṭāṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉa.
vāṉoli, tolaipēci, oliperukki, kirāmaḵpōṉ pōṉṟa pēcumpoṟikaḷ tōṉṟiya piṟaku makkaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavu neruṅki varukiṉṟatu.  tolaikkāṭciyaik kaṇṭaṟinta piṟaku ivvuṟavu mēlum neruṅkukiṉṟatu.  iṭattālum, kālattālum cēymaiyilirukkum makkaḷiṭamum nām ētō oru vitattil uṟavu koḷḷukiṉṟōm.  kālañceṉṟa pāṭakarkaḷiṉ kuralkaḷaiyum kirāmaḵpōṉ mūlam kēṭka muṭikiṉṟatu.  pala āyiram mailukku appālum irukkiṉṟa makkaḷiṭam tolaipēci mūlam pēcukiṉṟōm.  kampiyillāt tanti namakku nalkum vacatikaḷō collum taramaṉṟu.
intap putiya cātaṉaṅkaḷilellām iṉṟu tāy moḻiyiṉ tāṇṭavattaik kāṇkiṉṟōm.  vaṇikarkaḷum, piṟarum tolai pēcimūlam tāymoḻiyil pēcippala ceyalkaḷai niṟaivēṟṟik koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.  perumpālāṉa vāṉoli nikaḻccikaḷ tāymoḻiyil amaikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  oliperukki vanta piṟaku oruvar pēcuvatai orē kālattil ilaṭcakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ kēṭka muṭikiṉṟatu.  nāṭu viṭutalaip peṟuvataṟkuc cila āṇṭukaḷ muṉpiruntē potuppēccu mēṭaiyait tāymoḻi taṉakkē eṉṟu urimaiyākkik koṇṭuviṭṭatu.  vayatu vantavarkaḷukku vākkurimai vanta piṟaku potumēṭaiyil makkaḷ maṉattai orumukappaṭuttum palvēṟu potukkūṭṭaṅkaḷ, mānāṭukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ nikaḻccikaḷ tāymoḻiyilēyē naṭai peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  caṭṭacapai nikaḻccikaḷum tāy moḻiyāṉa tamiḻilēyē naṭaipeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam iṉṟu ceyaṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
potumēṭaiyait tāymoḻi kaippaṟṟiya kāraṇattāl iṉṟu nāṭṭil palvēṟu iyakkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi vaḷarntu varukiṉṟaṉa.  makkaḷiṉ etirkāla vāḻvai makkaḷē vakuttuk koḷḷavēṇṭu meṉkiṟa kural emmaruṅkum eḻuppappaṭukiṉṟatu.  moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil mānilap piriviṉai  ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ uṇarntuvarukiṉṟaṉar.  ataṟkeṉa ēṟpaṭṭup paṇiyāṟṟivarum kuḻuviṉ aṟikkaiyaiyum atupaṟṟiya palvēṟu tiṟaṉāyvukaḷaiyum pala aṟiñarkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷaiyum ceytittāḷkaḷil veḷivantataik kaṇṭōm.  moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭaiyiltāṉ āntira mānilap piriviṉaiyum ēṟpaṭṭatu;  pampāy mānilamum iraṇṭākap pirintatu.
ilakkiya nayaṅkaṇṭu iṉpuṟal: tamiḻmoḻiyil eṇṇaṟṟa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa;  namatu mūtātaiyar namakku kuṭivaḻic cottāka vaittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟa aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷ avai.  iḷamaiyiliruntē māṇākkarkaḷai ilakkiya nayamuṇarntu cuvaittalil īṭupaṭutta vēṇṭum.  ilakkiyac cuvaiyil tiḷaikkum āṟṟalai māṇākkarkaḷiṭam vaḷarttu viṭṭāl atuvē tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ tām kaṟpittalil kaṇṭa veṟṟiyākum.  aṟitōṟum aṟiyāmaiyaik kāṭṭum  ilakkiyac cuvaiyil tiḷaitta oruvar.
	iruntamiḻē uṉṉāl iruntēṉ; imaiyōr
 viruntu amiḻtam eṉṟālum vēṇṭēṉ3 
eṉṟallavō kūṟippōntōr? ilakkiya āciriyarkaḷiṉ paṭaippukkaḷ yāvum toṭṭaṉaittuṟum maṇaṟkēṇi pōl payiluntōṟum putupputup poruḷ curantu iṉpamūṭṭum teyva ūṟṟukkaḷākum.  ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ilaimaṟai kāykaḷākac ceṟintu kiṭakkum uḷḷuṟai uvamam iṟaiccip poruḷ pōṉṟa poruḷ nayaṅkaḷaiyum, avaṟṟiṉ veḷit tōṟṟaṅkaḷākak kāṭciyaḷikkum mōṉai etukai maṭakku yamakam tiripupōṉṟa col nayaṅkaḷaiyum kaṇṭuṇarntu cuvaikkumpoḻutu eṉṟumē kāṇāta iṉpam payattalai ilakkiyac cōlaikaḷil pukuntu pārppavarkaḷ eḷitil uṇarvar.
poruḷ kāraṇamāka, poṟi karuviyāka, nukarappaṭum iṉpam poruḷaṟṟa nilaiyil iḻakkappaṭukiṉṟatu.  poruḷāl peṟappaṭum iṉpam vaḷaravaḷara ataṟkuk kāraṇamākavuḷḷa poruḷ curuṅkik kaṭaiciyil atuvum illaiyākiviṭum.  āṉāl, ciṟupāṉmai poṟikaḷ tuṇainiṟpa, nuṇ māṇuḻaipulaṅ karuviyāka nukarappaṭum ilakkiya iṉpam, iṉpaṅkaḷ aṉaittilum talaiciṟantatu eṉpatu aṟiñar kaṇṭa uṇmai; aṟivāl paṭaikkappeṟum ivviṉpam vaḷaravaḷara ataṟkuk kāraṇamāya aṟivum mēṉmēlum vaḷarum.  nāṉum vaḷarntu taṉmūlattaiyum vaḷarkkum ilakkiya iṉpam makkaḷ naṉmaikku iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvatu. kampanāṭarum,  
kavippā amutam icaiyiṉ kaṟiyoṭu
	kaṇṇaṉ uṇṇak 
kuvippāṉ 4
eṉṟu kavippā iṉpaamiḻta māvataiyum, aḵtu icaiyuṭaṉ cērttu uṇṇappaṭumpoḻutu teviṭṭāta teḷḷamuta māvataiyum kūṟiyiruttal īṇṭu cintikkaṟpālatu.  tiruvācakat tēṉil tiḷaitta irāmaliṅka vaḷḷalum tām peṟṟa iṉpattai,
	vāṉkalanta māṇikka vācaka! niṉ vācakattai
nāṉkalantu pāṭuṅkāl naṟkaruppañ cāṟṟiṉilē 
tēṉkalantu pālkalantu ceḻuṅkaṉittīñ cuvaikalantu
ūṉkalantu uyirkalantu uvaṭṭāmal iṉippatuvē. 5
eṉṟu kāṭṭukiṉṟār.  ivviṉpattai aṭaiyum vaḻiyai naṉku aṟintu koṇṭāl, vēlaiyiliruntu nīṅki ōyvu peṟuṅ kālattilum, mutumai kāraṇamāka avalakkavalaiyālum taḷarcciyālum tuṉpappaṭum poḻutum ivviṉpam iḷamai yuṇarcciyaiyūṭṭi maṭṭillāta makiḻcciyaiyum aḷikkum.
aṉṟiyum, ivvulaka vāḻvil vayiṟṟup piḻaippukkākap pala tuṟaikaḷil kiṭantuḻaṉṟu uḻaittu aluttuppōkum poḻutu tōṉṟakkūṭiya veṟuppai ivvilakkiya iṉpam pōkkiviṭum.  kīḻmakkaḷ kūṭṭuṟavu, cōmpal, vīṇkālap pōkku mutaliya pala kuṟaikaḷai nīkkavum ilakkiyap payiṟci tuṇai purikiṉṟatu.  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil nēriṭak kūṭiya ēmāṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum iṭarp pāṭukaḷaiyum iṉpamāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇmaiyai ivvilakkiyaṅkaḷ namakkuk kaṟpikkum.
	naṉṟāmkāl nallavāk kāṇpavar aṉṟāmkāl
 allaṟ paṭuvatu  evaṉ? 6
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa poṉmoḻikaḷ maṉa amaitikkuc ciṟanta maruntupōl utavum.  paranta aṟivaiyum, virinta nōkkattaiyum nalki nammaip putiyatōr maṉitaṉākkavalla ivvilakkiya iṉpattai virumpātār yār? āṅkilat tiṟaṉāyvāḷar mātyū ārṉālṭu eṉpār kūṟiyapaṭi.  vāḻkkaiyiṉ tiṟaṉāyvutāṉē ilakkiyam? tāymoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷaip paṭittuc cuvaippatupōl ayal moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷaic cuvaikkamuṭiyātu.  eṉavē, iḷamaitoṭṭē tāymoḻip payiṟci aḻuttamāka vēṇṭap paṭuvatoṉṟu.
kaṟpaṉai yāṟṟalaiyum murukuṇarcciyaiyum vaḷarttal:   tāymoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷait tuyppatāl ivai iraṇṭum oruvarpāl tāmāka vaḷarak kūṭiyavai.  tāy moḻiyāciriyar ceyyuṭ pāṭaṅkaḷaik kaṟpikkumpoḻutu ivai naṉku vaḷarum cūḻnilaikaḷai uṇṭākkalām.  ilakkiyattiṟkuk karuvūlamākat tikaḻvatu kaṟpaṉaiyāṟṟal;  kavitaikku iṉṟiyamai yātatāka vēṇṭappaṭuvatum atuvē.  murukuṇarcci eṉpatu aḻakuḷḷavaikaḷait tuykkum oruvakai āṟṟal:  aḻakuḷḷa vaṟṟaip poṟi karuviyākak koṇṭum tuykkalām;  kaṟpaṉaic ciṟaku peṟṟa maṉattālum tuykkalām.  ilakkiya āciriyaṉ pulaṉkāḷālum kaṟpaṉaiyālum kaṇṭavaṟṟaiyē avaṉ paṭaitta ilakkiyaṅkaḷaip paṭikkumpoḻutu nammaiyum kāṇac ceykiṉṟāṉ.
caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷ, kamparāmāyaṇam, kaliṅkattupparaṇi pōṉṟa tīñcuvai mikka ilakkiyaṅkaḷaippaṭikkumpoḻutu eṇṇaṟṟa kaṟpaṉaik kāṭcikaḷaik kāṇkiṉṟōm;  ak kāṭcikaḷil tōyntu kiṭakkum murukuṇarcciyil nāmum īṭupaṭukiṉṟōm.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka kaṟpaṉaiyāṟṟalai nammiṭaiyē tōṟṟuvikkum oṉṟiraṇṭu kāṭcikaḷaik kāṇpōm. cāṭṭai koṇṭu pamparamviṭṭu viḷaiyāṭum ciṟuvarkaḷai  nām pārttirukkiṉṟōm.  alakilā viḷaiyāṭṭuṭai āṇṭavaṉ aṇṭaṅkaḷai āṭṭivaippatai iv viḷaiyāṭṭukku oppumai kāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ oru kaviñaṉ.
cāṭṭi niṟkum aṇṭam elām
	cāṭṭai ilāp pamparam pōl
āṭṭuvikkum kuṟṟālattu
	aṇṇalār. 7
eṉṟu kūṟuvatil kaṟpaṉait tiṟaṉaik kāṇka.  aṇṭaṅkaḷ cuḻalvataiyum pamparam cuḻalvataiyum oppiṭṭa murukuṇarcciyai eṇṇi makiḻka.  kālai iḷamparitiyiṉ eḻilaik kāṭṭum maṟṟoru kaviñaṉ.
	taṅkam urukkit taḻalkuṟaittut tēṉākki
eṅkum parappiyatōr iṅkitamō? 8
eṉṟu kūṟuvatil potintuḷḷa kaṟpaṉaiyāṟṟalaic cintittuk kaḷikka, itaiviṭa vēṟu evvāṟu kūṟamuṭiyum?
nīlat tiraikkaṭal arukē niṉṟukoṇṭu pāratiyār katiravaṉ maṟaivataik kāṇkiṉṟār;  avar appoḻutu uṇarnta uṇarcci vimmitaṅkoṇṭu virikiṉṟatu pāñcāli capatattil;  arccuṉaṉ vāymoḻiyāka avar kaṇṭa kāṭciyaik kūṟukiṉṟār.  pāṇṭavarkaḷ vituraṉ mūlam turiyōtaṉaṉ aṉuppiya aḻaippaip peṟṟu attiṉapurattiṟku varukiṉṟaṉar.  vaḻiyil mālaippoḻutil pārttaṉ pāñcālikkup paritiyiṉ eḻilai viḷakkukiṉṟāṉ.
aṭivāṉat tēaṅku paritik kōḷam
aḷappariya viraiviṉoṭu cuḻalak kāṇpāy;
iṭivāṉat toḷimiṉṉal pattuk kōṭi
eṭuttu avaṟṟai oṉṟupaṭa urukki vārttu
muṭivāṉa vaṭṭattaik kāḷi āṅkē
moykuḻalāy, cuṟṟuvataṉ moympu kāṇāy!
vaṭivāṉatu oṉṟākat takaṭu iraṇṭu
vaṭṭamuṟac cuḻaluvatai vaḷaintu kāṇpāy. 9 
parācakti kāḷitēvi pattukkōṭi miṉṉalaip piṭittu vārtta pantutāṉ katiravaṉ eṉṟu kaṟpaṉai ceytiruppatu kaviñaṉatu kaṟpaṉai ceyyum āṟṟalai viḷakkukiṉṟatu.  iṉi, pāratiyiṉ aṭiccuvaṭṭil varum pāratitācaṉ kāṭṭum muḻumatiyak kāṭciyaiyum pārppōm.
  nīlavāṉ āṭaikkuḷ uṭalma ṟaittu
nilāeṉṟu kāṭṭukiṉṟāy oḷimu kattaik
kōla muḻutuṅ kāṭṭiviṭṭāl kātaṟ
koḷḷaiyilē ivvulakam cāmō? vāṉac
cōlaiyilē pūttataṉip pūvō nītāṉ!
cokkaveḷḷip pāṟkuṭamō? amuta ūṟṟō?
kālaivanta cemparitik kaṭalil mūḻkik
kaṉalmāṟip kuḷiraṭainta oḷippi ḻampō! 10
iyaṟkaiyākiya peṇ taṉatu uṭalaiyellām nīlavāṉa mākiya āṭaiyil maṟaittukkoṇṭu mukattai maṭṭilum kāṭṭu kiṉṟāḷām;  muḻuvaṭivaiyum kāṭṭiviṭṭāl ivvulaka makkaḷ kātaṟ koḷḷaiyil iṟantu viṭuvārkaḷām;  maṟṟum,  kaviñaṉukkuc cantiraṉ cōlaiyilē pūtta taṉippūvākavum, cokkaveḷḷip pāṟkuṭamākavum, amuta ūṟṟākavum, kālaiyil tōṉṟiya katiravaṉ kaṭalil mūḻki eḻuntatāl kuḷiraṭainta oḷip piḻampākavum kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟāṉ.
caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu taṟkālattil tōṉṟi varum ilakkiyaṅkaḷvarai uḷḷa tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ittakaiya kāṭcikaḷait tammakattē koṇṭuḷḷa pāṭalkaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavai kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  tamiḻmoḻi kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkariṉ aṟivunilai, paṭṭaṟivu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu avaṟṟait tērnteṭuttuk kaṟpikkavēṇṭum.  avaṟṟaik kaṟpikkumpoḻutē kaṟpaṉaiyāṟṟalaiyum murukuṇarc -ciyaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭum; kaṟpaṉaiyāṟṟalukku aṭippaṭai uḷḷattil paṭintukiṭakkum uṇarcciyē.  uṇarcciyum kaṟpaṉaiyum oṉṟaiyoṉṟu piṉṉik kiṭakkum: oṉṟu kuṟaintālum  ataṟkēṟṟavāṟu piṟitoṉṟaṉ āṟṟal kuṟaintu viṭum. cintaṉaiyum kāṭcittiṟaṉum kalantu uṇarccikaḷai veḷippaṭuttum ikkaṟpaṉaiyāṟṟal uyarnta kaviñarkaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭum ōr aruḷāṟṟal.  ittakaiya āṟṟal potintuḷḷa pāṭalkaḷaik kaṟpikkum poḻutu māṇakkarkaḷiṭam meymaṟantu pāṭṭukkaḷait tuykkum āṟṟalai uṇṭākkiviṭṭāl, avarkaḷiṭam atu vaḷarvatuṭaṉ avarkaḷākavē pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷaip paṭittut tuykkum tiṟamaiyaiyum peṟuvar.  tāymoḻi kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ itai nōkkamākak koṇṭu paṇiyāṟṟa vēṇṭum.
vaḻivaḻi varum camūka iyalaiyum paṇpāṭṭaiyum aṟiyum tiṟavukōl:  ellā moḻikaḷilumē ammoḻi pēcukiṉṟavarkaḷiṉ paṇṭaic camutāya vāḻvaiyum paṇ pāṭṭaiyum aṟivataṟkuriya nūlkaḷ irukkum; perumpālum avai ilakkiyamākavum, varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷākavum tikaḻum.  āṉāl, tamiḻil vāḻvai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa, ilakkaṇa nūl iruppatu ammoḻiyiṉ taṉic ciṟappākum; vāḻkkaikkē ilakkaṇam vakuttuk kāṭṭiya perumai tamiḻaṉuṭaiyatu.  ivvulakil emmoḻiyilu mirāta perumai tamiḻ moḻiyil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.  puṟanāṉūṟu, akanāṉūṟu pōṉṟa caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu paṇṭait tamiḻarkaḷiṉ camūka vāḻkkaiyaiyum paṇpāṭṭaiyum aṟintukoḷḷalā meṉṟālum ippaṭittāṉ kuṟikkōḷ vāḻkkai amaiya vēṇṭum. eṉṟu tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattil tiṭṭamāṉa kuṟippukaḷ kāṇap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  tamiḻarkaḷ vāḻkkaiyai akavāḻkkai eṉṟum, puṟavāḻkkai eṉṟum iru kūṟiṭṭup pēcuvar: vāḻkkaiyiṉ kuṟikkōṭ poruḷākiya aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu eṉṟa nāṉku pakutikaḷil iṉpattai akattilum ēṉaiya mūṉṟaṉaip puṟattilum aṭakkiṉar.  kātalai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa vāḻkkaiyai akapporuḷ eṉṟum, pōr, koṭai, naṭpu pōṉṟavaṟṟai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa vāḻkkaiyaip puṟapporuḷ eṉṟum ilakkiyaṅkaḷil pirittup pēcappaṭum.  kātalaiyum pōraiyum kalaiyāka uyarttiya perumai tamiḻarkaḷuṭaiyatu. akapporuḷ, kuṭumpa vāḻk kaiyaip paṟṟiyatu:  uṇarcciyait talaimai yākak koṇṭu taṉivāḻkkaiyiṉ palvēṟu iyalpukaḷaip paṟṟip pakuttup pēcuvatu. puṟapporuḷ, ulaka vāḻkkaiyākiya potu vāḻkkaiyaip paṟṟiyatu; ceyalai aṭippaṭaiyākakkoṇṭu ulaka vāḻkkaiyiṉ palvēṟu tuṟaikaḷaip pakuttup pēcuvatu.
iṉpattai nalkum kāmattai mūṉṟu pirivākap pirittu ilakkaṇa nūlār vakaippaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. āṭavarilō makaḷirilō yārēṉum oruvar māttiram maṟṟoruvarmītu kātal koḷvatākac colvatu oru vakai; itu kaikkiḷai  eṉappaṭum.  valiya iṉpam nukartal maṟṟoru vakai;  itaṉaip peruntiṇai eṉṟu ilakkaṇa nulār kuṟippar.  mūṉṟāvatu vakaitāṉ aṉpuṭaik kāmam.  itu kuṟiñci, pālai, mullai, marutam, neytal eṉṟu aintu tiṇaikaḷākap pakuttup pēcap paṭuvatāl itaṉai aintiṇai neṟi eṉṟu kuṟippiṭum vaḻakkamum uṇṭu.  tiṇai eṉṟa col tamiḻil oḻukkattaik kuṟikkum.  aintu tiṇaiyilum nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷai muṟaiyē puṇartal, pirital, iruttal, ūṭal, iraṅkal eṉṟu paṇṭaiyōr kuṟippiṭṭaṉar.  ippaṭiaintu vakaiyākap pirittālum katai pōlat toṭarntu varum kātal vāḻkkaiyil pala kaṭṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  ovvoru kaṭṭattilum pala ciṟu nikaḻccikaḷ nikaḻak kūṭum.  avaṟṟait tuṟai eṉṟu kūṟuvar ilakkaṇa nūlār.  kiṭṭattaṭṭa nāṉūṟu tuṟaikaḷai akapporuḷ pāṭalkaḷil kāṇalām.  kātal vāḻkkaiyil paṭaittuk kāṭṭappeṟum talaivaṉum talaiviyum kuṟikōḷ vāḻkkai naṭattum puṉitamāṉavarkaḷ; avarkaḷ oru kālattil ōriṭattil irukkak kūṭiyavarkaḷ allar; eṉṟum ellā viṭattum nirantaramāka iruppavarkaḷ.  avarkaḷaip peyar cuṭṭip kūṟuvatu kūṭat tavaṟu eṉṟu karutiṉar tamiḻarkaḷ.  ātalāltāṉ akapporuḷ pāṭalkaḷil peyar cuṭṭik kūṟappeṟum māntarkaḷaik kāṇpatē illai.
puṟapporuḷil aṭaṅkiya kuṟikkōḷ poruḷkaḷ aṟam, poruḷ, vīṭu eṉṟa mūṉṟumākum.  puṟattavarkaḷukku veḷippaṭumpaṭiyāṉa nikaḻccikaḷ nikaḻvataṉāl itu puṟam eṉṟu cuṭṭappeṟṟatu.  akapporuḷiluḷḷa tiṇai tuṟaikaḷaip pōlavē puṟapporuḷilum tiṇai, tuṟaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  puṟap poruḷiṉ tiṇai, tuṟaikaḷai vakuttuk kūṟuvatil iraṇṭu vakaiyāṉa koḷkaikaḷ uḷa.  akattiyam, tolkāppiyam eṉṟa nūlkaḷ puṟapporuḷ kaḷukku ēḻu tiṇaikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.  avai veṭci, vañci, uḻiñai, tumpai, vākai, kāñci, pāṭāṇ eṉpavai, veṭcittiṇai pakaivaruṭaiya pacuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷai avar aṟiyāmal koṇarntu pātukāttalaic  ceyvatu. nāṭu piṭikkum ācaiyāl ōraracaṉ maṟṟōraracaṉmītu taṇṭeṭuttup pōrukkuc cellutal vañcittiṇai.  pakaivar araṇai muṟṟukaiyiṭuvatākac colvatu uḻiñaittiṇai.  vīracciṟappu veḷippaṭum poruṭṭu vēntar porutalai, eṭuttiyampuvatu tumpai.  pōril talaiyiṭum aracarkaḷ mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ toḻilaic ciṟappittu ōtutal vākai.  kāñci nilaiyāmaiyai pulappaṭuttuvatu.  pukaḻtal, vāḻttutal vakaiyāl palaraic ciṟappittup pāṭutal pāṭāṇ tiṇaiyākum.  paṉṉirupaṭalam, puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālai pōṉṟa nūlkaḷ tolkāppiyar kūṟiya tiṇaikaḷil cilavaṟṟai iraṇṭākap pirittum, akattaic cārtti vaitta kaikkiḷai peruntiṇai kaḷaik kūṭṭiyum, vēṟu cilavaṟṟaic cērttum puṟapporuḷaip paṉṉiraṇṭākap pakuttup pēcukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟiṉ virivukaḷai uriya nulkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷka.11  akapporuḷ, puṟapporuḷ tuṟaikaḷai vaittup pāṭip piṟkālap pulavarkaḷ tam kāviyaṅ kaḷaic ciṟappittirukkiṉṟaṉar. paṇṭiruntu iṉṟu varaiyil tōṉṟiyuḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷaip paṭikkumpoḻutu avai nērmukamākavum maṟaimukamākavum avvak kālaṅkaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ camūka iyalaiyum paṇpāṭṭaiyum viḷakkuvatai aṟiyalām. 
vāḻkkaiyai vaḷappaṭutti nītikaḷaip pukaṭṭutal:  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil moḻi payaṉpaṭāta tuṟaikaḷē illai. pēcutal, kēṭṭal, eḻututal ākiya mūṉṟu ceyalkaḷum payaṉpaṭāta vāḻkkait tuṟaikaḷē illai eṉalām.  kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷai viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiya māṇakkarkaḷ vāḻvil nuḻaiyumpoḻutu naṉṟākap pēcavum,  paṭikkavum, eḻutavum āṟṟal peṟṟirukkavēṇṭum.  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyilum immūṉṟu tiṟaṉkaḷum peritum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  irupattoru āṇṭu muṭinta yāvarum vākkurimai peṟṟu makkaḷālēyē aracāṅkam niṟuvappeṟum ikkālattil ivai payaṉpaṭāta nēramē illai.  iḷamaiyil tāymoḻip payiṟciyiṉṟēl ciṟanta maṉap payiṟci amaiyātu; nāṭṭu varalāṟṟaic cariyāka aṟintu koḷḷa iyalātu;  moḻippaṟṟum nāṭṭuppaṟṟum oruvariṭam amaiyā;  nalloḻukkamum iṟaiyaṉpum ēṟpaṭu vataṟkum tāymoḻip payiṟci mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.  vāḻvai vaḷappaṭuttum ip paṇpukaḷil ovvoṉṟaiyum kuṟittu oru ciṟitu ārāyvōm.  tāymoḻip payiṟciyiṉāl ivai  evvāṟu kaivarappeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpataiyum aṟivōm. 
eṇṇa vaḷarccikku moḻi iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.  eṇṇamum  moḻiyum oṉṟaiviṭṭu oṉṟupirikka muṭiyātapaṭi amaintirukkiṉṟaṉa.  oṉṟaiviṭṭup piṟitoṉṟai vaḷarkkavum iyalātu.  pēcappaṭum collukkum atu kuṟikkum poruḷukkum; pēcuvōṉukkum kēṭpōṉukkum nērāṉa toṭarpai ēṟpaṭuttuvatu moḻiyē.12  oru kuḻantai pacuvaip pārkkiṉṟatu; iḵtu ataṟku oru putiya paṭṭaṟivu (Experience). ovvoru  taṭavaiyilum ataip pārkkumpōtu putiya paṭṭaṟivu akkuḻantaikku ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. oru taṭavai pacu pullait tiṉpataiyum, maṟṟoru taṭavai akkuḻantaiyiṉ aṉṉai pacuviṟkut tīṉi vaittup pāl kaṟappataiyum, piṟitoru taṭavai pacu kaṉṟukkup pāl ūṭṭuvataiyum kuḻantai kāṇakkūṭum. intak kāṭcikaḷ yāvum akkuḻantai yiṉ maṉattil pativu peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  pacu eṉṟa collaik kēṭṭavuṭaṉēyē pacuviṉ uruvam akkuḻantaiyiṉ maṉattiraiyil amaikiṉṟatu; pacuvaip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ yāvum ataṉ maṉattāl cēkarikkap peṟṟu ataṉuṭaiya aṟivu mukaṭṭil vaikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  toṭarpāka eḻum karuttukkaḷ aṉaittaiyum orē kālattil maṉattiṟkuk koṇṭuvara muṭikiṉṟatu.  oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷyāvum ippaṭippaṭṭavaiyē.  moḻivaḷarcciyē nākarika vaḷarcciyākum.  tāymoḻiyiṉ vāyilāka eṇṇa vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭuvatu iyalpātalāl, atu mutalil kaṟpikkap peṟutal vēṇṭum.
maṉattiluḷḷa karuttait teḷivākak kāraṇakāriya muṟaiyil veḷippaṭuttuvataṟkuc ciṟanta maṉappayiṟci vēṇṭaṟpālatu.  itait tāymoḻi vāyilākattāṉ eḷitil peṟa iyalum.  tāymoḻip paṭippē kalvikku aṭippaṭaiyāka amaintu nilaittatum āḻamāṉatumāṉa uyar paṇpukaḷai oruvariṭam ēṟpaṭuttukiṉṟatu.  oru tiṭṭappaṭi vēlai ceytal, maṉattiluḷḷa karuttukkaḷaik kōvaiyāka veḷiyiṭal, kalaittuṟaikaḷil iṟaṅki uḻaittal mutaliya naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷ yāvum tāymoḻip paṭippāltāṉ kaivarappeṟum eṉṟu moḻimuṟai aṟinta mūtaṟiñarkaḷ moḻikiṉṟaṉar.  eṉavē, tāymoḻip paṭippu mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tāymoḻi ilakkiyattaip paṭikkumpoḻututāṉ annāṭṭu varalāṟṟaippaṟṟit teḷivāka aṟiyamuṭikiṉṟatu.  ilakkiyam vāḻkkaiyiṉ kaṇṇāṭi eṉṟum, vāḻkkaiyē moḻiyil ilakkiyamāka amaikiṉṟatu eṉṟum mēṉāṭṭut tiṟaṉāyvāḷarkaḷ kūṟuvar.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka puṟanāṉūṟu pōṉṟa caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷāltāṉ paṇṭait tamiḻakattiṉ varalāṟu, paṇṭait tamiḻarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaimuṟai avarkaḷiṉ āṭcimuṟai,  camayanilaikaḷ, kalaikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu.  varalāṟṟup paṭippu, kalveṭṭukkaḷ,  ceppup paṭṭayaṅkaḷ, paḻaṅ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ, kōyilkaḷ, nāṇayaṅkaḷ, ayalnāṭṭiṉar eḻutivaitta kuṟippukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl aṟiyamuṭiyāta uṇmaikaḷaiyellām ilakkiyaṅkaḷāl aṟiya muṭiyum.  kallilum cempilum eḻutivaikka muṭiyāta  itaya pāvaṅkaḷai yellām ilakkiyamtāṉ nuṭpamāka virittuk kāṭṭum.  etirkāla vāḻvai amaikkak kaṭantakāla vāḻvu peritum tuṇai puriyumātalāl, tāymoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam atait teḷivāka aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
moḻippaṟṟum nāṭṭuppaṟṟum uṇṭāvataṟkut tāy moḻippaṭippu mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.  ellā makkaḷum tattam tāymoḻiyaic ciṟappittup pēcutal iyalpeṉiṉum, emmoḻiyum peṟāta ciṟappiṉait tamiḻ moḻitāṉ peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu.  moḻiyai iṟaiyuṭaṉ iyaipupaṭuttip pēcum vaḻakkam nam nāṭṭiltāṉ uṇṭu.
	kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷum kaḻakamōṭu amarntu
 paṇṇu ṟatterintu āyntaip pacuntamiḻ13 
eṉṟu tamiḻmoḻi pōṟṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  civaperumāṉē naṉpāṭṭup pulavaṉāyc caṅkam ēṟi tamiḻ āynta perumaiyait tēvāram eṭuttiyampukiṉṟatu.  pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷai tamiḻ moḻikkup paramporuḷai uvamai kūṟiyuḷḷār.  ōr ellaiyaṟṟa paramporuḷ pal uyirkaḷaiyum pala ulakaṅkaḷaiyum paṭaittu, aḷittu, aḻittālum; tāṉ yātoru māṟupāṭiṉṟi, aṉṟum, iṉṟum, eṉṟum, iruppatupōlavē, namatu tamiḻ moḻiyum muṉṉaip paḻamoḻikkum muṉṉaip paḻamoḻiyāyum; piṉṉaip putumoḻikkum piṉṉaip putu moḻiyāyum polikiṉṟatu `eṉṟu pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār,  vaṭamoḻiyum, teṉmoḻiyum, civaperumāṉiṉ irukaṇkaḷ eṉṟu oru pulavar kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.  iṉṉoru pulavar,
	ōṅkal iṭaivantu uyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
ēṅkolinīr ñālattu iruḷkaṭiyum-āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatir oṉṟu; ēṉaiyatu
taṉṉēr ilāta tamiḻ. 14
eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.  ic ceyyuḷ pakalavaṉukkum tamiḻukkum cilēṭaiyāy amaintirukkiṉṟatu.  piṟitoru pulavar,
	poruppilē piṟantu, teṉṉaṉ
	pukaḻilē kiṭantu, caṅkattu
iruppilē iruntu, vaiyai 
	ēṭṭilē tavaḻnta pētai
neruppilē niṉṟu, kaṟṟōr
	niṉaivilē naṭantuōr ēṉa
maruppilē payiṉṟa pāvai 
	maruṅkilē vaḷaru kiṉṟāḷ, 15
eṉṟu pāṭukiṉṟār.  ic ceyyuḷil tamiḻai oru kuḻantaiyāka uruvakittu ataṉ vaḷarcciyai muṟaiyē kāṭṭiyiruppatu aṟintu iṉpuṟattakkatu.  tamiḻ viṭu tūtu eṉṉum nūlil tamiḻiṉ ciṟappu virivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  annūlāciriyar,
tittikkum teḷḷamutāyt teḷḷamutiṉ mēlāṉa
muttik kaṉiyē!eṉ muttamiḻē!-  puttikkuḷ
uṇṇap paṭuntēṉē! 16
eṉṟu tamiḻaik kuḻaintu pōṟṟuvataik kāṇka.
nāṭṭup paṟṟiṉ mutircciyāl viṭutalaiyaṭainta makkaḷ moḻippaṟṟāl nikaḻvataik kāṇkiṉṟōm.
yāmaṟinta moḻikaḷilē tamiḻmoḻipōl
iṉitāva teṅkum kāṇōm
eṉṟu pāratiyiṉ kural tamiḻarkaḷiṭam tamiḻppaṟṟiṉait tūṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu.  tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷai naṉku cuvaitta pāratitācaṉ kaṉṉal poruḷtarum tamiḻē nī ōr, pūkkāṭu; nāṉōr tumpi! eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
tāymoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷ nāṭṭuppaṟṟaiyum uṇṭākkum.  tamiḻiṉ āṇai nilaviya pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟku vaṇakkam terivikkum pāṭal oṉṟil innāṭṭup paṟṟu veḷiyākiṉṟatu.
āviyan teṉṟal veṟpiṉ
	akattiyaṉ virumpum teṉpāl
nāvalan tīvam pōṟṟi
	nāvalan tīvan taṉṉuḷ 
mūvarkkum ariyāṉ niṟpa
	muttamiḻ teyvac caṅkap
pāvalar vīṟṟi rukkum
	pāṇṭinaṉ ṉāṭu pōṟṟi. 17
eṉpatu kaviñariṉ vākku.  tarumiyiṉ poruṭṭu vātāṭa vantapoḻutu civaperumāṉ niṟkavum, caṅkap pulavarkaḷellām vīṟṟirukkavum ceytatu tamiḻallavā? kampar, cēkkiḻār pōṉṟa peruṅ kaviñarkaḷiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ittakaiya pāṭalkaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa.  kaṉinta nāṭṭuppaṟṟākiya amutak kaṭalil tōṉṟiya pāratiyāriṉ pāṭṭukkaḷ nāṭṭuppaṟṟai eṉṟum vaṟṟātu curantu niṟkum ūṟṟukkaḷākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. 
	centamiḻ nāṭeṉṉum pōtiṉilē-iṉpat
tēṉvantu pāyutu kātiṉilē
eṉṟa aṭikaḷukku īṭāṉavai enta moḻiyilāvatu irukka muṭiyumā?
aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu aṭaital nūṟpayaṉē18
eṉṟu ilakkaṇam vakutta perumai tamiḻmoḻikkut tāṉ uṇṭu.  nalloḻukkamum kaṭavuḷ aṉpumē kalviyiṉ kaṉinta payaṉkaḷ eṉṟu koṇṭa perumai tamiḻmaṟaikkuttāṉ uṇṭu.
  kaṟka kacaṭaṟak kaṟpavai; kaṟṟapiṉ
niṟka ataṟkut taka.19
kaṟṟataṉāl āya payaṉeṉkol, vālaṟivaṉ
naltāḷ toḻāreṉiṉ?20
eṉpaṉapōṉṟa maṟaimoḻikaḷ māṇākkarkaṭkuk kalviyiṉ muṭinta nōkkattai uṇartti avarkaḷ vāḻvaic ceḻikkac ceykiṉṟaṉa.   tamiḻ moḻiyil aṟanūlkaḷukkum iṟai vaḻipāṭṭu nūlkaḷukkum kuṟaivē illai; vāḻkkaiyiṉ palvēṟu uṇmaikaḷaik kataivaṭivil vaittuk kāṭṭakkūṭiya kāviyaṅkaḷum pala uḷḷaṉa.  nēraṭiyāka nītiyaip pukaṭṭum pākkaḷait tavira maṟaimukamāka nīti kūṟum kavitaikaḷaiyum ērāḷamākak kāṇalām.
	nallatu ceytal āṟṟīr āyiṉum
allatu ceytal ōmpumiṉ; atutāṉ
ellārum uvappatu aṉṟiyum
nallāṟṟup paṭūu neṟiyumā ratuvē. 21
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa aṭikaḷ makkaḷākap piṟanta aṉaivarukkumē ciṟantatōr oḻukkattaip pukaṭṭum aṟavuraiyāka miḷirkiṉṟaṉa.  caiva camaya kuravarkaḷ nalkiya pāṭalkaḷum āḻvār maṇikaḷ aruḷiyuḷḷa pācuraṅkaḷum, irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ pāṭiyuḷḷa tiruvaruṭpākkaḷum, kalneñcattaiyum karaikkavallavai:  ūṉiṉaic curukki uḷḷoḷi perukki uvappilā āṉantattai aḷikkak kūṭiyavai.
taṭittaōr makaṉait tantaikaṇṭu aṭittāl
	tāyuṭaṉ aṇaippaḷ:  tāy aṭittāl
piṭittuoru tantai aṇaippaṉ; iṅkueṉakkup
	pēciya tantaiyum tāyum
poṭittiru mēṉi ampalattu āṭum
	puṉita! nī ātalāl eṉṉai
aṭittatu pōtum aṇaittiṭal vēṇṭum
ammaiyap pāiṉi āṟṟēṉ.22
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa iṟai vaḻipāṭṭaik kūṟum pākkaḷ nantamiḻ moḻiyil eṇṇiṟantavai uḷḷaṉa.  tamiḻmoḻi payilvōr attakaiya pāṭalkaḷaip paṭittu iṉpuṟṟup payaṉaṭaiyalām.
payiṟṟumoḻi:  tamiḻ moḻi ēṉaiya pāṭaṅkaḷaip payiṟṟum moḻiyāka (Medium) vantapiṟaku ammoḻiyaik kaṟpikkum nōkkamum virivaṭain tirukkiṉṟatu.  ettaṉaiyō kalait tuṟaikaḷait tamiḻil kaṟpikkum nilai ēṟpaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu; kallūrikaḷilum tamiḻ payiṟṟu moḻiyāka amaiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar; atupaṟṟi ārāyntum varukiṉṟaṉar.  tāymoḻiyil poruḷuṇarntu aṟivai vaḷarttuk koḷvatil kālaccelavu, paṇaccelavu muyaṟci ākiyavai curuṅkum eṉpatu kūṟāmalē pōtarum.  puṭṭippālai viṭat tāyppāl ciṟantataṉṟō?  eṉavē, tāymoḻip payiṟciyiṉṟi paḷḷikaḷilum kallūrikaḷilum uṇmaiyāṉa aṟivu vaḷarcciyē illaiyeṉṟu kūṟiviṭalām.
ovvoruvaruṭaiya uyarvukkum tāḻvukkum avaravaruṭaiya aṟivē kāraṇamākum.  aṟivuṭaiyār ellām uṭaiyār eṉṟa vaḷḷuvar vākkai aṟika.  āṟaṟivuṭaiya makkaḷiṭam iyalpāka amainta aṟivu ceyaṟkai muṟaiyāl vaḷarcciyaṭai kiṉṟatu; oruvaruṭaiya paṭṭaṟivālum piṟar paṭṭaṟintavaṟṟaik kēṭpatālum avaṟṟaippaṟṟiya nūlkaḷaip paṭippatālum vaḷarvataiyē ceyaṟkai muṟaiyāl vaḷarvatu eṉṟu colla vēṇṭum.  āḻntu cila nūlkaḷaip paṭittu aṟivai vaḷarkkum vāyppukaḷ ikkālattil atikamāka uḷḷaṉa; kalvi paṉmuka virivai eyti yirukkiṉṟatu.  eṉavē, piṉṉar uyirkku uṟuti payappataṟku maṭṭilum nōkkamākak koṇṭa paṇṭaik kālattu tāymoḻip paṭippaip pōlaṉṟi ikkālat tāy moḻip paṭippu, makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, uṭalnalam, poruḷ nilai, aṟiviyalkaḷ mutaliya pala tuṟaikaḷaippaṟṟiyum aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa nōkkat til amaintirukkiṉṟatu.  ikkālat tēvaikaḷaiyoṭṭi kaṇṇaiyum karuttaiyum kavarum ellāk kalaikaḷaiyum kaṟṟu aṟintu koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉṟa iyalpāṉa uṇarcci māṇākkarkaḷiṭam ēṟpaṭakkūṭum.  tāy moḻiyil uḷḷa puttakaṅkaḷai mēmpōkkākap paṭikkum paḻakkatālēyē iḵtu eḷitil niṟaivēṟum. eṉavē, ikkālattiṉ aṟivuttuṟaikku ēṟṟavāṟu coṟ celvamum naṭaip poruttamum uṭaiya tamiḻ moḻiyai vaḷarkkum muṟaiyil  tamiḻk kaṟpittal amaiyavēṇṭum.  ilakkiya naṭaiyum ilakkiyak karuttukkaḷai uṇarttum tamiḻum piṟa pāṭak karuttukkaḷai uṇarttuvataṟku ēṟṟatāka irā.  tēvaik kēṟṟavāṟu putu naṭaiyaiyum putiya kalaic coṟkaḷaiyum ākkac coṟkaḷaiyum mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyirukkum.  tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ innōkkattaiyum naṉku aṟiya vēṇṭiyatu iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.
tamiḻmoḻi payiṟṟu moḻiyāka vantapiṟaku paḷḷiyil perumpālāṉa nikaḻccikaḷ tamiḻilēyē naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ivviṭattil `jārj cāmcaṉ eṉpār āṅkila moḻikkuk kūṟiyuḷḷatu tamiḻukkum poruntum.  avar kūṟukiṉṟār:  tāymoḻip paḷḷi vāḻkkaikkē iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.  avvāḻkkaiyiliruntu ataip pirikka iyalātu; atu paḷḷiyākiya uṭalukku uyirāka iruppatu.  ovvoruvaruṭaiya vāḻvilum atu toṭṭililiruntu cuṭukāṭu maṭṭum puṟakkaṇikka muṭiyāta oru nilaiyaip peṟṟiruppatu.  aṟivai vaḷarttuk koḷḷum oru karuviyāka iruppatu maṭṭumaṉṟik kuḻantaiyai vāḻkkaiyil īṭupaṭuttum iṉṟiyamaiyāta uṟuppākavum irukkiṉṟatu.23  eṉavē, tamiḻmoḻip payiṟci ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷukkum aṭippaṭaiyāṉa potup payiṟci eṉṟum, tamiḻmoḻi, kalvit tiṭṭattil (Curriculum) kāṇappeṟum ēṉaiya pāṭaṅkaḷaip pōl oru pāṭam maṭṭilum aṉṟu eṉṟum, aḵtu aṅku iṅku eṉātapaṭi eṅkum pirakācamāyp paḷḷi vāḻkkaiyiṉ cūḻnilaiyilellām ūṭuruvic cellum cātaṉam eṉṟum tamiḻāciriyar maṭṭumaṉṟi ellā āciriyarkaḷum talaimaiyāciriyar kaḷum araciṉarum uṇartal vēṇṭum.  uṇarntu, viraivil kuṟaikaḷaik kaḷaintu ākkavēlaikkuriya aṉaittaiyum niṟaivēṟṟat tuṇiyavēṇṭum.
tāymoḻi āciriyarkaḷ:  iṉṟu uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟi varum tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷil perumpālōr āṅkila aṟivu illātavarkaḷ: perumpālōr paḷḷiyiṟutit tērvuvarai kūṭap potukkalvi peṟātavarkaḷ.*  ēṉaiya pāṭaṅkaḷākiya kaṇitam, camūka iyal, aṟiviyal, āṅkilam ākiyavaṟṟaik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷaippōl ivarkaḷum paṭṭatārikaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  avarkaḷaippōla ivarkaḷum payiṟcik kalluriyil āciriyar payiṟciyaip peṟṟu appayiṟciyilum paṭṭatārikaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  iṉṟuḷḷa tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ paṭṭatārikaḷaiviṭa moḻiyaṟivu peṟṟiruntālum, paṭṭatārikaḷukkirukkum vāyppukaḷ ivarkaḷukkillai.  paṭṭatārikaḷ peṟum payiṟci muṟaiyai ivarkaḷ peṟa muṭivatillai.  kalvittuṟaipaṟṟiya pala putiya karuttukkaḷai āṅkila nūlkaḷiliruntu terintu koḷḷum vāyppukkaḷum ivarkaḷuk killai.  kaṟpikkum muṟaipaṟṟiyum, kalvittuṟaiyil pal vēṟu poruḷ kaḷaip paṟṟiyum tāymoḻiyil nūlkaḷ mikak kuṟaivu; nalla nūlkaḷ illai yeṉṟē tuṇintu kūṟi viṭalām.  tāymoḻiyil ittakaiya nūlkaḷ perukumvarai tāy moḻiyaik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ āṅkila aṟivu peṟṟup paṭṭatārikaḷāka iruttal iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum. paṭṭatārikaḷāka illāviṭṭāl āṅkilam, kaṇitam, aṟiviyal, camūka iyal pōṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷaik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷaippōl tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ pala putiya karuttukkaḷai aṟintukoḷvataṟku vāyppukaḷ irā; ēṉaiyōr putiya karuttukkaḷait terintu koḷvataip pōl ivarkaḷ terintu koḷḷa iyalātu.
ēṉaiya pāṭaṅkaḷaik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ peṟṟuḷḷa kalviyaṟivu, payiṟṟum muṟai ākiyavaṟṟaip pōl tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷum peṟṟiruntāl ivarkaḷiṭam vērūṉṟiyirukkum tāḻvuṇarcci ōraḷavu naṉṟākavē nīṅkiviṭum.  avvāciriyarkaḷaip pōlavē ivarkaḷum paḷḷiyil naṭaipeṟum ellā nikaḻccikaḷilum kalantu koḷḷavēṇṭum.  ivarkaḷ kuṭimaip payiṟci, cāraṇarpaṭai, ceñciluvaic caṅkam, cuṟṟulā ākiyavaṟṟilellām paṅku koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  palatiṟappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟipēcavum, uraiyāṭavum vallavarkaḷākat tikaḻa vēṇṭum.  tāvara iyal, nila iyal, pirāṇi iyal, araciyal nūl, pautika iyal, vētiyal, vāṉiyal pōṉṟa tuṟaikaḷpaṟṟi yellām māṇavarkaḷiṭam uraiyāṭum aṟivuṭaiyavarkaḷāka iruntāl, māṇākkarkaḷiṭam nalla celvākkuṭaṉ tikaḻalām;  tām kaṟpikkum nūlkaḷil avvakaik karuttukkaḷ varumpaṭi pāṭaṅkaḷ tokukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉar: akaṉṟa paṭippukkuriya nūlkaḷ palvēṟu poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟiyaṉavāka veḷi varukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟaik kaṟpikka vēṇṭiya nilaiyiluḷḷavarkaḷ ōraḷavu paḷḷiyiṟutit tērvu varaiyilāvatu potuk kalviyum palkalaik kaḻakap paṭippum paṭṭamum peṟṟiruntāltāṉ ciṟappuṭaṉum tiṟamai yuṭaṉum paṇiyāṟṟa muṭiyum.
tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ itukāṟum ilakkiya iṉpam aṭaivatē kalviyiṉ nōkkam eṉṟu eṇṇiyiruntaṉar.  anta nōkkattai avarkaḷ ciṟitu māṟṟik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  moḻiyaik kaṟpatu ilakkiyam paṭittu nukarvataṟkāka maṭṭum aṉṟu eṉpatai uṇara vēṇṭum.  kalvi maṉitaṉai muḻu vaḷarcci aṭaiyac ceyyum cātaṉam eṉpataiyum, ciṟanta kuṭimakaṉākac ceyvataṟkut tuṇaipurivatum atuvē eṉpatu pōṉṟa nōkkaṅkaḷaiyum avarkaḷ niṉaivil iruttavēṇṭum.  uḷaviyal kaṟṟu, kuḻantaiyiṉ maṉattaip paṟṟiya navīṉak karuttuk kaḷai aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  pala kalvi aṟiñarkaḷ tam aṉupavattil kaṇṭaṟinta muṟaikaḷaiyellām terintu avaṟṟait tam moḻip payiṟṟalil evvevvāṟu kaiyāḷalām eṉpataic cintittal vēṇṭum, ceviyiṉ vāyilākap payiṟṟu vatuṭaṉ ēṉaiya pulaṉkaḷ vāyilākavum uyarttiṉāl māṇākkarkaḷ pāṭattai naṉku uṇarvar eṉpatai aṟintu ataṟkēṟṟa muṟaikaḷaik kaiyāḷavēṇṭum.  paṭaṅkaḷ, nāṭṭup paṭaṅkaḷ, ōviyaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum. ēṉaiya āciriyarkaḷaippōl māṇākkarkaḷukkup pāṭattil evvevvāṟu cuvaiyūṭṭi ārvattai eḻuppalām? entat tuṇaikkaruvikaḷai mēṟ koṇṭāl avarkaḷ kavaṉattaip pāṭattil nilainiṟuttalām? uṭaṟcōrvaip pōlavē maṉaccōrvum uṇṭu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavaṟṟait tam uḷḷattil amaintu avaṟṟiṟkēṟṟavāṟu kaṟpittalai māṟṟiyamaittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
tāymoḻiyaik kaṟpataṟkum vāḻkkaikkum uḷḷa toṭarpai aṭikkaṭi māṇākkarkaḷ uṇarumpaṭi ceytal vēṇṭum.  ēṉaiya āciriyarkaḷaip pōlavē ceyalkaḷ mūlamum tām kaṟpittalai mēṟkoḷḷalām.  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyaiyum kuḻantaikaḷ vāḻum cūḻnilaiyaiyum naṉku kavaṉittāl ettaṉaiyō nikaḻccikaḷ itaṟkut tuṇai ceyyum; avaṟṟaip payaṉuḷḷa vāṟu amaittuk koḷvatiltāṉ āciriyarkaḷiṉ tiṟaṉ  irukkiṉṟatu.  paḷḷi nikaḻccikaḷilellām paṅkeṭuttukkoḷḷum āciriyarkaḷukku ivai yāvum eḷitil tōṉṟupavaiyē.  iṉṟu ettaṉaiyō tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ karum palakaiyaikkūṭac cariyākap payaṉpaṭuttuvatillai.  kaṭiṉac coṟkaḷukkup poruḷ eḻututal, ilakkaṇa vitikaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷ tarutal, cila poruḷkaḷaip paṭampōṭṭu viḷakkutal ākiyavaṟṟiṟkuk karumpalakaiyaip payaṉpaṭuttalām.  kaṟpittalil karumpalakaiyaiviṭac ciṟantatōr tuṇaik karuvi illai eṉṟu kūṭac collumpaṭi atu payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu.  kavaṉak kuṟaivuḷḷa māṇākkarkaḷ, kēḷvi uṇarcci kuṉṟiyuḷḷa māṇākkarkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷukkuk karumpalakaic cātaṉam ciṟanta tuṇaiyāka irukkum eṉpatai aṉupavattāl aṟiyalām.
tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷum ēṉaiya āciriyarkaḷaip pōlavē niṉṟu koṇṭu kaṟpittal vēṇṭum.  niṉṟukoṇṭu kaṟpittal māṇākkarkaḷukkum āciriyarkaḷukkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa kaṇṇāl kāṇamuṭiyāta māṉacīka uṟavai naṉku vaḷarkkum. amarntu kaṟpittāl aṭikkaṭikkarum palakaiyaip payaṉpaṭuttutal, paṭaṅkaḷaiyum ēṉaiya tuṇaikkaruvikaḷaiyum tiṟaṉuṭaṉ kaiyāḷutal  pōṉṟavaṟṟai naṉku niṟaivēṟṟa muṭiyātu.  aṭikkaṭi rakara-ṟakara vēṟupāṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷ, lakara-ḷakara-ḻakara vēṟupāṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟaik karumpalakaiyil eḻutikkāṭṭiṉāl, māṇākkarkaḷiṉ eḻuttup piḻaikaḷ kuṟaiyum.  kaṭpulap payiṟciyiṉālaṉṟi vēṟu muṟaikaḷiṉāl eḻuttup piḻaikaḷaik kuṟaikka muṭiyātu.
tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ viṉākkaḷiṉ iṉṟiyamai yāmaiyai  naṉku uṇartal avaciyam; kalvi payiṟṟalil viṉākkaḷiṉṟi nallaveṟṟiyaik kāṇamuṭiyātu.  pāṭattait toṭaṅkuvataṟkumuṉ tērnteṭuttuḷḷa cila viṉākkaḷai viṭuttu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ muṉṉaṟivaic cōtittal vēṇṭum; muṉṉaṟivai aṟiyātu pāṭattait toṭaṅkip payaṉillai.  pāṭa vaḷarcciyiṉ poḻutu aṭikkaṭi viṉākkaḷai viṭuttu viṭaiyaḷikkac coṉṉāl tāṉ māṇākkarkaḷ āciriyaraip piṉpaṟṟuvatu teriyum; pāṭattaip purintukoḷḷukiṉṟaṉarā eṉpatai aṟiyavum muṭiyum.  pāṭattai muṭitta piṉṉarum viṉākkaḷai viṭuttāl tāṉ pāṭattil evvaḷavu kaṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷaṉar  eṉpatai  aṟintukoḷḷa iyalum.  aṉṟiyum, viṉākkaḷai aṭikkaṭik kaiyāṇṭāltāṉ māṇākkar tam karuttait teḷivāka uṇarttalil nalla payiṟciyaip peṟavum muṭiyum; kavaṉak kuṟaivuṭaṉiruppavarkaḷaik kavaṉikkac ceyyavum,  tūṅkum cilarai viḻippuṭaṉirukkac ceyyavum ivviṉākkaḷ peruntuṇai puriyum.
nalla muṟaiyil viṉākkaḷ viṭuttal eḷitaṉṟu.  nallapaṭṭaṟivuḷḷa āciriyarkaḷtām avaṟṟai naṉku kaiyāḷa muṭiyum.  nalla viṉākkaḷ nalla cintaṉaiyiṉiṉṟu tāṉ tōṉṟa muṭiyum.  nalla coṟkaḷāl teḷivāṉa muṟaiyil viṉākkaḷai uruvākkic ciṟanta muṟaiyil kaiyāḷa vēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷiṭam viṉākkaḷai viṭukkumpoḻutu avaṟṟai aṭikkaṭi māṟṟiyamaittāl avarkaḷiṭam mayakka uṇarcciyai uṇṭākkum; cintaṉaiyiṉ viḷaivākat tōṉṟiya viṉākkaḷai māṟṟi yamaikka vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamaiyāmai irātu. nalla muṟaiyil viṉākkaḷait tayārittaṟkuk kālac celavum cintaṉaiyum vēṇṭum; māṇākkarkaḷiṉ viṭaikaḷai ēṟṟamuṟaiyil payaṉpaṭutta aṉupavam vēṇṭum.  muṉ āyattamiṉṟi vakuppiṟkuc cellum moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ ittuṟaiyil veṟṟikāṇa muṭiyātu.  viṉākkaḷait tayārittal ēṉaiya āciriyarkaḷaiviṭa moḻiyāciriyar kaḷukku mikavum mukkiyamāṉatu. moḻittuṟaiyil viṉākkaḷai āyattam  ceytal eḷitaṉṟu.
itiliruntu tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ vakuppiṟkuc cellu vataṟkumuṉ muṉ-āyattam ceyyavēṇṭiyataṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmai teḷivākiṉṟatu.  piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷaip payiṟṟum āciriyarkaḷ muṉ-āyattam ceykiṉṟaṉarā eṉpatu vēṟu viṣayam; āṉāl avarkaḷ muṉ-āyattattiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyai maṟuppatillai.  āṉāl, tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷukku muṉ-āyattam ceytal eṉpatu putiya tuṟai.  avarkaḷ pāṭaputtaṅkaḷukkuttāṉ mukkiyattuvattai aḷikkiṉṟaṉar; puttakattil uḷḷavaṟṟaittāṉ māṇākkar maṉattil tiṇippatu eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar.  kaṟpikkum nērattai yellām antak kaiṅkariyattiltāṉ celaviṭu kiṉṟaṉar.  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil moḻi evvevvāṟu payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatait tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ  cintittup pārttāl, tām kaṟpikkum muṟaiyai māṟṟikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya taṉ avaciyattai uṇarvar; māṇākkarkaḷiṉ maṉa nilaik kēṟṟavāṟu ev vevvāṟu muṉ-āyattam ceyyappeṟutal vēṇṭum eṉpataiyum aṟivar.
ivaṟṟait tavira moḻipayiṟṟutaṟkeṉac ciṟappāṉa cila vitikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟait tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ naṉku aṟintirukkavēṇṭum.  avarkaḷ moḻikaṟpippataṉ ciṟappāṉa nōkkaṅkaḷaip paricīlaṉai ceyya vēṇṭum. pēccu, oppuvittal, vāyviṭṭup paṭittal. kaṇṇōṭṭamākap paṭittal, poruḷuṇartal, (Comprehension), coṟkaḷañ ciyappēṟu,  eḻututal, paṭaippāṟṟal pōṉṟa moḻit tiṟaṉkaḷaip paricīlaṉai ceyya vēṇṭum.  ceyyuḷ , urainaṭai eṉṟa iruvēṟu moḻi vaṭivaṅkaḷaip pākupāṭu ceytu avaṟṟai moḻi kaṟpippataṉ nōkkattiṟkēṟpa evvevvāṟu kaṟpikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa muṟaikaḷait tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.
uyarnilaippaḷḷikaḷil moḻi kaṟpittalai mēṟkoṇṭu paṇiyāṟṟi varum tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ toṭakka nilaip paḷḷikaḷil mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya vitikaḷaiyum muṟaikaḷaiyum kaiyāḷavēṇṭiya vāyppukaḷaip peṟuvatillai.  māṇākkarkaḷ toṭakkanilaip paḷḷikaḷil aintāṇṭu moḻippayiṟci peṟṟu mutaṟpaṭivattil vantu cērkiṉṟaṉar.  immāṇākkarkaḷ urai naṭaiyai vāyviṭṭup paṭikkavum, cātāraṇa ceyyuṭkaḷait taṭaiyiṉṟi viraivākap paṭikkavum payiṟci peṟṟiruppatuṭaṉ kaṇṇōṭṭamākap paṭippatilum ōraḷavu payiṟci peṟṟiruppar.  ippayiṟci kuḻantaikaḷiṉ taṉippaṭṭa tiṟamai, avarkaḷ vāḻum cūḻnilai, peṟṟa payiṟci mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkēṟpa māṟupaṭum.  kuḻantai ilakkiyaṅkaḷai eḷitil peṟṟup paṭittup payiṟciyaṭainta nakarppuṟac ciṟuvarkaḷ avvita ilakkiya vacatikaḷaip peṟamuṭiyāta nāṭṭuppuṟac ciṟuvarkaḷaiviṭa atikattiṟamaiyuṭaṉiruppar.  ivaṟṟaiyellām tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ naṉku aṟintu tam payiṟṟum muṟaiyai ēṟṟavāṟu māṟṟiyamaittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum,  aṅṅaṉam māṟṟiyamaittuk koḷḷāviṭil ellā māṇākkarkaḷum tām etirpārkkum aḷavu moḻiyaṟivu peṟa muṭiyātu.
iṉṟaiya nilaiyil paṭṭatārikaḷ tamiḻai naṉku payiṉṟu tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟa muṉvantāl, tamiḻp payiṟṟum muṟai pala tuṟaikaḷil virivaṭaiyum.  āṉāl, paṭṭatārikaḷ iṉṟu tamiḻ payiṟṟu muṟaiyai viruppappāṭamāka eṭuttukkoṇṭāl taṅkaḷ nilaikkuk kuṟaivu ēṟpaṭum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar.  innilai viraivil māṟavēṇṭum.  āṅkilam paṭittup paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷ tāymoḻiyaip payiṟṟal perumai eṉṟa karuttai makkaḷiṭaiyē kāl koḷḷac ceyyavēṇṭum.  āṅkila aṟivuṭaṉ paṭṭam peṟṟa tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷukku ūtiya aḷavilum piṟa tuṟaikaḷilum ciṟitu atikac calukaikaḷ aḷikkap peṟṟāl, pala paṭṭatārikaḷait tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟac ceyyamuṭiyum.  āṅkilam payiṟṟu moḻiyāka irunta poḻutu āciriyar payiṟci peṟṟa ovvoruvarum kaṭṭāyamāka āṅkila moḻiyaik kaṟpikkum muṟaikaḷilum payiṟci peṟṟaṉar.  atupōlavē tāymoḻi payiṟṟu moḻiyāka vantirukkum ik kālattil aṉaivarum tāy moḻippayiṟṟalil kaṭṭāyamākap payiṟci peṟa vaḻiceyya vēṇṭum.  āṉāl, iṉṟaiya nilai eṉṉa? piṟapāṭaṅkaḷaip payiṟṟum āciriyarkaḷ tāymoḻiyaip paṟṟic ciṟitum kavalai koḷḷuvatillai.  tām tāymoḻiyil cātāraṇamākac ceyyum piḻaikaḷaippaṟṟiyum kavalaip paṭuvatillai. tāymoḻiyil eppaṭi vēṇṭumāṉālum eḻutalām eṉṟa maṉappāṉmai mēlum kēṭu payakkiṉṟatu.  innilaiyai iṉi vaḷaraviṭakkūṭātu.  araciṉar ittuṟaiyil ciṟitu taṉikkavaṉam celuttiṉāl viraivil ciṟanta tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷait tōṟṟuvikka muṭiyum.
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ avaciyattai naṉkuṇarnta uyartiru.ti. cu. aviṉāciliṅkam avarkaḷ tām kalviyamaiccarāka irunta kālattil uyarnilaippaḷḷik kalvit tiṭṭattil tāymoḻikku mutaliṭam aḷittārkaḷ.  tāymoḻi kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷiṉ nilaiyiṉaiyum ōraḷavu uyarttiṉārkaḷ.  itukāṟum kalvit tuṟaiyiṉ varalāṟṟilēyē irāta perumaiyait tāymoḻi āciriyarukku aḷittārkaḷ.  kallūrikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟivarum tāymoḻiyillāta piṟapāṭa āciriyarkaḷukku iruntuvarum aracitaḻil pativu peṟum nilaiyait (Gazetted rank) tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷukkum mutaṉmutalāka vaḻaṅkiya perumai ivaraic cārum. 
tamiḻum piṟapāṭa āciriyarkaḷum: iṉṟu āṅkilattait tavira uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷiṉ kalvit tiṭṭattiluḷḷa pāṭaṅkaḷ yāvum tāymoḻiyilēyē kaṟpikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. innilaiyil tamiḻai oru taṉippāṭam eṉṟu karututal tavaṟu. piṟapāṭaṅkaḷaik kaṟpikkumpoḻutum cariyāṉa moḻiyaik kaiyāḷa vēṇṭum; appāṭaṅkaḷaik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ moḻiyuṇarcciyuṭaṉ tam viḻumiya paṇiyai āṟṟavēṇṭum. āṅkilam payiṟṟu moḻiyāka iruntapoḻutu eṅṅaṉam ellā āciriyarkaḷum pāṭa āciriyarkaḷum avvappōtu māṇakkarkaḷ āṅkila moḻiyil ceyyum piḻaikaḷaik kaḷaintaṉarō, atu pōlavē iṉṟu ellā āciriyarkaḷum tamiḻmoḻiyil māṇākkar ceyyum piḻaikaḷaik kaḷaiya muyalavēṇṭum. curuṅkak kūṟiṉāl ellā āciriyarkaḷum, pāṭa āciriyarkaḷāka iruppatuṭaṉ moḻiyāciriyar kaḷākavum paṇiyāṟṟavēṇṭum. māṇākkar vaḻaṅkum tāy moḻiyil kāṇum piḻaikaḷukku tamiḻāciriyar maṭṭilum poṟuppāḷikaḷ ākār; piṟapāṭa āciriyarkaḷum ōraḷavu poṟuppāḷikaḷāka irukkattāṉ vēṇṭum. innilai ēṟpaṭa vēṇṭumāṉāl paṭṭatārikaḷ tamiḻaik kaṟpikkum nilai varavēṇṭum. piṟapāṭa āciriyarkaṭku oru vakuppiṟkāvatu tāymoḻiyaik kaṟpikkum vāyppiṉai nalka vēṇṭum; avarkaḷai ataṟkut takutiyāḷarkaḷākavum ākkavēṇṭum.
uṭaṉaṭiyākac ceyyappeṟa vēṇṭiyavai: tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarcci karutic cila cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ uṭaṉaṭiyākac ceyyap peṟal vēṇṭum. kallūrit tamiḻp pāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷai māṟṟiyamaittup paṭṭatārikaḷiṭam nalla tamiḻaṟivu ēṟpaṭum vaḻivakaikaḷai vakukkavēṇṭum. tāymoḻi payilātu paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷ āciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyēṟka varumpoḻutu avarkaḷai appaṭṭattiṟkuriya tamiḻt tērvil tammait takutiyākkik koṇṭutāṉ varavēṇṭum eṉṟa kaṭṭāya viti ceyyavēṇṭum. iyaṉṟavarai, paṭṭat tērvuvarai tamiḻ payilātavarkaḷait tamiḻil piṟapāṭaṅkaḷaik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟum poṟuppukaḷaik kuṟaittal vēṇṭum. pi.ē. varaiyilāvatu tamiḻ payilātavarkaḷ piṟapāṭaṅ kaḷait tamiḻil kaṟpittal poruttamaṉṟu; pi. eci. paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷ* vittuvāṉtoṭakkanilait tērvu varaiyilāvatu tamiḻ payiṉṟāltāṉ nalla tamiḻmoḻi aṟivaippeṟa muṭiyum. uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷil talaimaiyāciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟu vōr tāy moḻip paṟṟuḷḷavarkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum. tāmum koñcam tamiḻ payiṉṟu tamiḻil aṟivu peṟātavarai tamiḻmoḻiyiṉmītu eṅṅaṉam ārvaṅ kāṭṭamuṭiyum? eṉavē, talaimaiyāciriyarkaḷāka niyamaṉam peṟupavarkaḷ ēṉaiya tiṟamaikaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻmoḻip pulamaiyum ōraḷavu peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. talaimaiyāciriyarkaḷ tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarcciyil akkaṟai kāṭṭātavarai paḷḷikaḷil nalla tamiḻai etirpārkka muṭiyātu. iṉṟu pi. ṭi. vakuppil tamiḻ kaṟpittalai viruppap pāṭamāka eṭuttup payiṉṟavarkaṭkut tamiḻāciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟum vāyppiṉai nalkavēṇṭum. pi.ṭi. tērvil tamiḻk kaṟpittal viruppap pāṭamāka iruppatai nīkkik kaṭṭāyap pāṭamākac ceyyavēṇṭum.+ tāymoḻiyil nalla aṟivu peṟātavarai, tāymoḻiyil piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷait tiṟamaiyākak kaṟpittal iyalātu. tāymoḻiyil piṟapāṭaṅ kaḷaik kaṟpippavarkaḷ tāymoḻiyaiyum kaṟpittal arumaiyaṉṟu. eṉavē, pi. ṭi. tērvukkuc cellum aṉaivarukkum tāymoḻi (maṇṭala moḻi) kaṟpikkum payiṟcikaḷai aḷittal porut tamāṉatē. nāḷaṭaivil uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷil tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ eṉṟa taṉi vakuppiṉarē illātu ceytalvēṇṭum. pi. ō. el. paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷ piṟapāṭaṅkaḷaip payiṉṟu paṭṭam peṟavum, piṟapāṭaṅkaḷil paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷ tāymoḻiyil paṭṭam peṟavum vāyppukkaḷai nalka vēṇṭum. iṉip paṭṭam peṟṟu uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷil āciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟupavarkaḷ tāymoḻiyilum piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷilum nalla pulamai aṭaiyum vāyppukkaḷaip peṟuvataṟkēṟṟavāṟu ovvoru paṭṭaṅkaḷuk kuriya pāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷil māṟṟam ceyyap palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷ muṉvarutal vēṇṭum. kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ, palkalaik kaḻakattiṉar, araciṉar ākiya mūvakaiyiṉarum oruṅku ārāyntu ceyyappeṟāta entak kalvit tiṭṭaṅkaḷum naṟpayaṉai viḷaivikkā.

pirivu-2: tiṭṭaṅkaḷum payiṟṟu muṟaikaḷum
kalvit tiṭṭam eṉpatu, toṭakka nilai, uyar nilai, kallūri nilaip paṭippukaḷil kaṟka vēṇṭiya pāṭaṅkaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟum oru tiṭṭam. pāṭat tiṭṭam eṉpatu, attiṭṭattil uḷḷa pāṭap poruḷ oṉṟil amaiya vēṇṭiya pāṭa vivaraṅkaḷaik kuṟippatu. muṉṉatu kalvit tuṟaikaḷaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu; piṉṉatu attakaiya tuṟaikaḷ ovvoṉṟilumuḷḷa pāṭaṅkaḷpaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷait terivikkiṉṟatu. tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ kalvit tiṭṭam, moḻippāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷait teḷivāka aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷaik karuvikaḷākak koṇṭu payiṟṟuṅkāl tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ payiṟṟaliṉ cila aṭip paṭaiyāṉa vitikaḷai aṟintu koḷḷavēṇṭum. ūkkuvittal, ceyalmūlam kaṟṟal, kaṟpaṉavaṟṟai vāḻkkaiyuṭaṉ iṇaittuk kāṭṭal, kaṟṟalil akkaṟaiyai uṇṭākkal, kaṟpittalil mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya aintu paṭikaḷ ākiyavai avaṟṟuḷ cila. paṇṭiruntu iṉṟu varai ettaiṉaiyō payiṟṟu muṟaikaḷai aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ māṇakkarkaḷ kuḻuvāka niṉṟu payilvataṟkum, taṉittaṉiyāka iruntu payilvataṟkum eṉat taṉittaṉi muṟaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. immuṟaikaḷ palvēṟu nāṭukaḷaic cērnta kalvi nipuṇarkaḷāl vakukkap peṟṟavai. āciriyar vakuppaṟaiyiliruntu kaṟpikkumpoḻutu cila upāyaṅ kaḷaikkaiyāṇṭu kaṟpittāl payiṟṟuvikkap peṟum poruḷ māṇākkar maṉattil pacumarat tāṇipōl patiyum; māṇākkar kaṭkuk kaṟpatilum ūkkammikum; uṟcākam kiḷarnteḻum. āciriyarkaḷ mēṟkoḷḷum upāyaṅkaḷuḷ vāy moḻiviṉākkaḷ, viḷakkan tarutal, tuṇaik karuvikaḷ, vīṭṭu vēlai ākiyavai kuṟippiṭat takkavai; ellāvaṟṟiṟkum iṉṟiyamaiyātavai. icceytikaḷ ip pirivil aintu iyalkaḷil viḷakkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
2. kalvittiṭṭamum moḻippāṭattiṭṭamum
tamiḻp payiṟṟuvataṉ nōkkaṅkaḷai aṟinta āciriyarkaḷ annōkkaṅkaḷai niṟaivēṟṟuvatu eṅṅaṉam eṉpataic cintikkavēṇṭiyatu avaciyamākum. ataṟkēṟṟa oru kalvit tiṭṭamum antat tiṭṭattil moḻip pāṭattiṭṭamum eṅṅaṉam amaikkappeṟal vēṇṭum eṉpataippaṟṟiya oru cila karuttukkaḷai īṇṭu ārāyvōm.
kalvit tiṭṭattait takkavāṟu vakuttuk koṇṭutāṉ kaṟpittalait toṭaṅkavēṇṭum. toṭakkanilai, naṭunilai, uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷukkēṟṟa kalvit tiṭṭaṅkaḷ nalla muṟaiyil uruvākkap peṟutal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟai vakuppatil mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya aṭippaṭaik karuttukkaḷai ēṉaiya āciriyarkaḷaip pōlavē tāymoḻi āciriyarkaḷum naṉku terintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. caṟṟuc cintittup pārttāl, tāymoḻi payiṟṟumoḻiyāka amainta piṟaku avarkaḷtāṉ avaciyam aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpatu teriya varum. ovvoru nilaikkuriya kalvittiṭṭattil tāy moḻippāṭattiṉ iṭam eṉṉa? tāymoḻip pāṭattiṭṭam evvāṟu vakukkappeṟa vēṇṭum?  eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vivaraṅkaḷaiyum avarkaḷ aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ikkaruttukkaḷai aṟiyātu tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ kuḻantaikaṭkuk kaṟpittāl atil payaṉ oṉṟum irātu; ivai talaiyamainta yāṉaikku viṉaiyamaitta pākaṉ pōlavum, aḷappariya ākāyattiṟku viḷakkamākiya tiṅkaḷum ñāyiṟum pōlavum avarkaḷkaruttil amaintu avartam paṇiyaip payaṉuṟum vaḻiyil ciṟappikkum.  ivaṟṟai aṟiyātu kaṟpippavar kuṉṟu muṭṭiya kuruvipōlavum, kuṟicci pukka māṉpōlavum iṭarppaṭuvatuṭaṉ kaṟpikkappeṟum māṇāk karkaḷum virumpiya payaṉai eytamuṭiyātu.  iṉi, kalvittiṭṭattai vakuppatil tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ tam maṉattil irutta vēṇṭiya orucila karuttukkaḷaik kāṇpōm.
