	ivvāṟu uṇarccivākkiyam ākkalām.
	(7)	tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟukiṉṟār : ellā vuyirkaṭkum aruḷpuripavar yāṇṭum tuṉpuṟutal ilar.5 
	ivvāṟu nēr kūṟṟu vākkiyam ākkalām.
iṅṅaṉam oru vākkiyattiṉ karuttaip palavita vākkiyaṅ kaḷil amaikkum paḻakkam kaivarappeṟṟāl moḻi naṭai tiruntum.
ippayiṟcikaḷil cilavaṟṟiṉ tuṇaiyāl moḻi nayam, naṭai nuṭpam mutaliyavaṟṟai māṇākkarkaḷ uṇarumpaṭi ceyyalām;  uṇarccikaḷaik kāṇumpaṭiyum ceyyalām.  immātiriyāṉa palavakai moḻip payiṟcikaḷiṉāl māṇākkarkaḷ tām eḻutum kaṭitaṅkaḷilum kaṭṭuraikaḷilum, tammuṭaiya uḷḷakkaruttukkaḷaiyum uṇarccikaḷaiyum teḷivākavum aḻuttamākavum veḷiyiṭum tiṟaṉai aṭaivar eṉpataṟ kaiyamillai.
11. kaṭṭuraip payiṟci
kd¤âš njh‹W« fU¤J¡fis¡ nfhit¥gLkhW fhuz fhÇa¤ bjhl®ãš mHfhd bkhÊÆš mik¥gJ f£LiuahF«.; iyaṟkaiyil maṉattil tōṉṟi eḻum eṇṇaṅkaḷ, moḻiyaip poṟuttavaraiyil, iraṇṭu vitamāka veḷippaṭum.  oṉṟu, vāymoḻi: iraṇṭu, eḻuttu moḻi.  pēccil veḷippaṭum eṇṇaṅkaḷ eḻuttu vaṭivu peṟātu nilaitta taṉmaiyai aṭaiya iyalātu.  avai ciṟantaṉavāy, kēṭka vāyppirāta piṟarum paṭikkat takkaṉavāy. eṉṟum nilaitta taṉmaiyai aṭaiyat takkaṉavāy irukka vēṇṭumāṉāl, avai eḻuttu nilaiyaip peṟavēṇṭum.  eḻuti vaikkum paḻakkam illaiyēl, maṉita nākarikam iṉṟiruppatu pōla ciṟantu vaḷarntōṅki irukka muṭiyātu.  eḻuttu moḻiyāl tāṉ orutalaimuṟaiyiṉ aṟivuc celvam iṉṉoru talai muṟaikkum payaṉpaṭa vāyppu ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.
vāymoḻik kaṭṭuraiyum eḻuttuk kaṭṭuraiyum: oru poruḷaippaṟṟiya karuttukkaḷai niralpaṭap pēccu mūlam veḷiyiṭuvatu vāymoḻik kaṭṭuraiyākum; ataiyē eḻuttu mūlam veḷiyiṭuvatu eḻuttuk kaṭṭuraiyākum.  vāymoḻik kaṭṭurai eḻuttuk kaṭṭuraikku aṭippaṭaiyāka amaiyum.  paḷḷikaḷil iruvitak kaṭṭuraikaḷilum māṇākkarkaḷ nalla payiṟciyiṉaip peṟutal vēṇṭum.  toṭakkanilaip paḷḷikaḷil vāymoḻikkaṭṭuraikaḷ mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātavai; mēl vakuppukkaḷil eḻuttuk kaṭṭuraikaḷukku atikamāṉa iṭam tarappeṟum.  vāymoḻik kaṭṭuraip payiṟci nalla muṟaiyil amaintāl eḻuttuk kaṭṭuraikaḷ ciṟanta muṟaiyil vaḷarcci peṟum.
pēccu naṭaikkum eḻuttu naṭaikkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu.  pēccu naṭaiyil atikak kaṭṭuppāṭu irātu.  muḻu vākkiyaṅkaḷāka irukkavēṇṭumeṉṟa kaṭṭāyam illai; pala coṟkaḷiṉ tuṇaiyiṉṟiyē apinayaṅkaḷālum mukakkuṟikaḷālum karuttukkaḷ viḷakkap peṟum.  āṉāl, eḻuttu naṭaiyil oḻuṅkum kaṭṭuppāṭum tēvai.  ilakkaṇa varampukku uṭpaṭṭu, karuttukkaḷ kāraṇakāriya muṟaippaṭi oruvitat toṭarpuṭaṉ muḻu vākkiyaṅ kaḷāka amaivatu  eḻuttu naṭaiyiṉ ciṟappākum. pēccu naṭaiyil kāṇappeṟum uyirōṭṭamum uṇarccip perukkum eḻuttu naṭaiyilum amaiyavēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  tiru. vi. ka. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, tiruppātirippuliyūr ñāṉiyār aṭikaḷ pōṉṟa periyārkaḷiṉ pēccum eḻuttum kiṭṭattaṭṭa orē vitamāka irukkum eṉpatai aṟiñarkaḷ aṭikkaṭic colli makiḻvatuṇṭu.
payiṟṟum muṟai:  kaṭṭuraip payiṟcikaḷait tarutalil āciriyar iyaṉṟavarai vitivaruvittal muṟaiyai mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.  ellāvitamāṉa kaṭṭuraip payiṟcikaḷum eḷimai yiliruntu arumaikkum, terintavaṟṟiliruntu teriyātavaṟṟiṟkum, teḷiviliruntu cikkalukkum, kāṭcip poruḷiliruntu karuttup poruḷukkum ceṉṟāl māṇākkar kaṭṭuraip payiṟciyil perum payaṉ eytuvar.  muṟai vallār kaṭṭuraip payiṟciyiṉ vaḷarcciyai mūṉṟu nilaikaḷākap pakuttup pēcuvar.
mutal nilai:  innilaiyil payiṟcikaḷai māṇākkar piṟar utavi koṇṭē eḻutuvar.  toṭakkanilaip paḷḷikaḷil
mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu vakuppukaḷil pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷil kāṇappeṟum poruḷkaḷpaṟṟiyē eḻuttup payiṟcikaḷ naṭaipeṟum.  ēṟakkuṟaiya pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkap peṟum moḻi naṭaiyilēyē eḻuttup payiṟcikaḷ amaiyum.  innilai māṇākkarkaḷ oru poruḷiṉ iyalpukaḷai viḷakkiyum varuṇittum eḻutuvateṉpatu iyalātatoṉṟu.  pāṭapputtakaṅkaḷiliruntu tarappeṟum payiṟcikaḷ paṭitta pāṭap poruḷai mīṇṭum cintikka vāyppiṉai nalkukiṉṟaṉa.  tamiḻp pāṭa nūlkaḷil kāṇappeṟum poruḷkaḷōṭu camūkappāṭam, iyaṟkaip poruṭpāṭam, kaittoḻil pāṭam pōṉṟa piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷilum kāṇappeṟum kataip poruḷkaḷ, vēṟu eḷiya poruḷkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaiyum kaṭṭuraip payiṟcikaḷākat taralām.  innilaiyil kīḻ vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷukkum pārttu eḻutum payiṟcikaḷum, collu vateḻutum payiṟcikaḷum eḻutum payiṟcikaḷāka amaiyum.
iraṇṭām nilai:  innilaiyil māṇākkarkaḷ piṟar utavikaḷaik koṇṭu eḻutum nilaiyiliruntu paṭippaṭiyākat tāmē eḻutum nilaikkuc celvar.  aintām vakuppilum, mutaṟpaṭivattilum pāṭapputtakattilirukkum poruḷkaḷ, avaṟṟaic ciṟitu māṟṟiyamaitta poruḷkaḷ, avaṟṟiliruntu tōṉṟi eḻakkūṭiya poruḷkaḷ ākiyavai paṟṟikkuṟippukaḷ koṭuttup paṭippaṭiyākap payiṟcikaḷai eḻutac ceyyalām.  pāṭapputtakaṅkaḷil kāṇappeṟāta, āṉāl tam pulaṉkaḷāl kaṇṭu kēṭṭu uṇṭu uyirttu uṟṟu aṟiyum poruḷkaḷai eḻuttup payiṟcip poruḷkaḷākak koḷḷalām.  innilai māṇākkarkaḷukkup paṭṭaṟiviṟku appāṟpaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟi eḻutac collutal miṉkaṭattiyiṉ oru kōṭiyai maṭṭum iṇaittu miṉ āṟṟalai varuvikka muyalvatai okkum eṉṟu pērāciriyar pālārṭu karutukiṉṟār.  eṉṉatāṉ muyaṉṟālum oru kōṭiyaik koṇṭu miṉ āṟṟalai veḷippaṭutta muṭiyātu eṉpataip pōlavē, paṭṭaṟiviṟkappāṟpaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟi varaiyac ceyvatilum āciriyar evvaḷavu muyaṟci eṭuppiṉum ataṉāl māṇākkarkkuc ciṟitum payaṉ viḷaiyātu.  moḻip payiṟcikaḷ, kataik kaṭṭuraikaḷ, paṭak kaṭṭuraikaḷ pōṉṟa payiṟcikaḷ innilai māṇākkarkaḷukku ēṟṟavai.
mūṉṟām nilai:  innilaiyil māṇākkarkaḷ tāmākavē tārāḷamāka eḻutum tiṟaṉai eytuvar.  naṭaimuṟaiyil iraṇṭu mūṉṟu paṭivaṅkaḷil payilum  māṇākkarkaḷ ittakaiya moḻiyaṟivaip peṟuvar.  varalāṟṟuk kaṭṭurai, varuṇaṉaik kaṭṭurai, viḷakkak kaṭṭurai, cintaṉaik kaṭṭurai, kaṟpaṉaik kaṭṭurai, viḷakkak kaṭṭurai,  tarukkak kaṭṭurai pōṉṟa kaṭṭurai vakaikaḷum, palvēṟu kaṭita vakaikaḷum, eḷiya moḻipeyarppup payiṟcikaḷum, palavakaiyāṉa karuttuṇar payiṟcikaḷum ippaṭiva māṇākkarkaṭku ēṟṟavai.  mēṟ paṭivaṅkaḷil ivvakaip payiṟcikaḷiṉ tarattai uyartti eḻutac ceyvatuṭaṉ ārāyccik kaṭṭurai, aṅkatak kaṭṭurai, pukaḻccik kaṭṭurai, nōṭṭak kaṭṭurai pōṉṟa  kaṭṭurai vakaikaḷum perukki eḻututal, curukki eḻututal, poḻippurai varaital, kaṭiṉamāṉa moḻipeyarppukaḷ, paṭaippāṟṟalai vaḷarkkum ciṟukataikaḷ, kavitaikaḷ, ciṟu nāṭakaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa payiṟcikaḷaiyum paṭippaṭiyākattaralām.
māṇākkar aṟiyavēṇṭiya kuṟippukkaḷ:  kaṭṭuraikaḷai eḻutat toṭaṅkuvataṟkumuṉ āciriyar māṇākkarkaḷaic cila kuṟippukaḷaippaṟṟi aṟiyac ceytal vēṇṭum.
mutalāvatu :  kaṭṭuraiyiṉ talaippukkēṟṟapaṭi poruḷ cēkarittal.  poruḷ tiraṭṭum vaḻikaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ mūṉṟu mikavum mukkiyamāṉavai: (i) māṇākkarkaḷ tām muṉṉarē aṟintuḷḷa poruḷkaḷai niṉaivupaṭuttit tiraṭṭik koḷḷutal; (ii) poruḷkaḷait takka nūlkaḷiliruntum, poruḷkaḷai nōkki ārāyntum, vayatilum aṟivilum mūtta aṟiñarkaḷai aṭuttuk kēṭṭum tiraṭṭutal; (iii) iruvaḻikaḷilum aṟiyamuṭiyātaṉavaṟṟai māṇākkarkaḷ tam aṟivu nilaik kēṟṟavāṟu puṟavulakaiyum akavulakaiyum kavaṉittut tiraṭṭutal.
iraṇṭāvatu:  tiraṭṭiya poruḷkaḷai oḻuṅku paṭuttutal.  perumpālāṉa māṇākkarkaḷ poruḷkaḷai varaiyaṟuttu ōḻuṅkupaṭuttum vakaikaḷai aṟiyātatāl avarkaḷatu kaṭṭuraikaḷ cariyāka  amaivatillai.  kaṭṭurai amaippaiyum pattiyiṉ amaippaiyum oṭṭik karuttukkaḷaik kāraṇa kāriya muṟaippaṭi oḻuṅkupaṭuttic caṭṭakam (Skeleton) varaiyum paḻakkattait toṭakkattiliruntē vaṟpuṟutta vēṇṭum.  caṭṭakam amaippatil uyarnilaip paḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷ pattu maṇittuḷikaṭku (Minutes) mēlum, kallūri māṇākkarkaḷ kālmaṇikku mēlum kālattaic celaviṭalākātu.
mūṉṟāvatu : caṭṭakattai amaittāṉavuṭaṉ kaṭṭurai vēlaiyait toṭaṅkalām.  poruḷukkēṟṟa naṭaiyil aḷavāṉa vākkiyaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu kōvaipaṭat teḷivākavum, tiruttamākavum kuṟippiṭṭa nērattiṟkuḷ kaṭṭuraiyai eḻuti muṭikkavēṇṭum.  eḻutumpoḻutu kaṭṭurai amaippupaṟṟiya vitikaḷ, patti amaippuppaṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ, vākkiya amaippup paṟṟiya karuttukkaḷ, niṟuttaṟ kuṟikaḷiṭum muṟaikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟai niṉaivilirutti nalla kaiyeḻuttil niṟutti eḻuti, eḻutiya vaṟṟait tirumpap paṭittāl kaṭṭurai piḻaikaḷiṉṟi nalla muṟaiyil amaiyum.
kīḻ vakuppukaḷil eḻuttup payiṟcikkumuṉ āciriyar māṇākkaruṭaṉ kalantu pēci naṉmuṟaiyil takka vāy moḻip payiṟciyai aḷikkavēṇṭum.  iṅku ip payiṟci virivākavum viḷakkamākavum irukkum.  mēl vakuppukkaḷil paṭippaṭiyāka itaṉ aḷavu kuṟaintu koṇṭē ceṉṟu iṟutiyil eḻutuvataṉ muyaṟci muṟṟilum māṇākkaruṭaiyatākavē amaintu viṭum.  kīḻ vakuppukkaḷil vāymoḻik kaṭṭuraikkup piṉṉarē eḻuttukkaṭṭuraip payiṟci varavēṇṭum.  ivvāṟu amaintāl tirutta vēṇṭiya piḻaikaḷ atikamākā.  eḻuttukkaṭṭuraip payiṟcikaḷallāta vāymoḻik kaṭṭuraikaḷ atikamāka naṭattap peṟṟāl mēl nilaiyil eḻutappaṭupavai mikac ciṟappāka amaitalkūṭum.  cātāraṇamākap paḷḷikaḷilum kallūrikaḷilum payiṟcikaḷākat tarappeṟum eḻuttup payiṟcikaḷiṉ vakaikaḷaip paṟṟi oru ciṟitu aṟintu koḷvōm.
eḻuttup payiṟcikaḷ
1. kataik kaṭṭurai:  kataikaḷai virumpātavarē illai.  kataikaḷ kaṟṟōrkkum maṟṟōrkkum iṉpam aḷikkiṉṟaṉa; ciṟanta uṇmaikaḷaiyum nītikaḷaiyum kēṭpōr maṉattil patikkat tuṇai ceykiṉṟaṉa; kataikaḷ pētaiyaraiyum mētaiyarākkum peṟṟi vāyntavai.  pālar mutal mutiyōr varai kataikaḷai ellōrum virumpi ēṟkiṉṟaṉar.  ittakaiya pērāṟṟal vāynta kataikaḷai eḻutuvatil ciṟu vayatiliruntē māṇākkarkaṭkup payiṟci aḷikka vēṇṭum.  ciṟuvarkaḷukkuk karuttai īrkkum kataikaḷ eḻutuvataṉ mūlam ciṟanta moḻip payiṟciyait tanta avarkaḷiṭam moḻivaḷattai eytu vikkalām.  naṭunilaip paḷḷikaḷil kīḻ vakuppukku ēṟṟa payiṟciyāka itaik koḷḷalām.  kataikkaṭṭuraiyil mūṉṟupaṭikaḷaik koṇṭa oru vaḷarcci muṟaiyaik kāṇalām.
mutaṟpaṭiyil āciriyar oru kataiyaic curukkamākak kūṟuvār; allatu ōriraṇṭu muṟai paṭittuk kāṭṭuvār.  ataik kēṭṭuk koṇṭirukkum māṇākkarkaḷait tirumpavum toṭarcciyākac collumpaṭi āciriyar ēvuvār. itaip pala viṉākkaḷ viṭuttup palarum cērntu toṭarcciyākak kūṟumāṟum ceyyalām.  kataiyiṉaik kōvaiyākak kūṟi muṭittapiṉ karumpalakaiyil kuṟippukaḷai eḻuti oruvāṟu māṇākkar uḷḷattil kataiyai amaiyumpaṭi ceyvār.  piṉṉar avaṟṟai aḻittuviṭṭu māṇākkarkaḷai mēṟpaṭi kataiyai eḻutumpaṭi kūṟuvār.
iraṇṭāvatu paṭiyil katai muḻuvataiyum āciriyar kūṟuvatillai.  iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭum kuṟippukkaḷai maṭṭilum caṭṭakamākak karumpalakaiyil eḻutik kataiyiṉ paranta pōkkai oruvāṟu viḷaṅkumpaṭi colluvār.  piṟaku muṉpōlavē vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷaik kataiyiṉaic collac ceytu, piṟaku avarkaḷai taṉit taṉiyāka eḻutumpaṭi ēvuvār.
mūṉṟāmpaṭiyil kataiyiṉ kuṟippukaḷ toṭarcciyākavum mikutiyākavum irā.  kataiyiṉ curukkamē ciṟutoṭar moḻikaḷāṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟirukkum.  ovvoru toṭar moḻiyaiyum māṇākkarkaḷ virittuk kataiyait toṭarntu collumpaṭi ceyvār.  piṟaku muṉpōlavē aṉaivaraiyum kataiyait taṉittaṉiyāka eḻutumpaṭi ēvuvār.  pulappaṭāta iṭaṅkaḷil maṭṭilum āciriyar kūṟit tuṇai ceyvār.
āciriyar koṭukkum caṭṭakam nāṉku vakaippaṭum.  kataiyai evarum aṟintu koḷḷumāṟu ellā vivaraṅkaḷaiyum kuṟippatu niṟai caṭṭakam; mukkiyamāṉa nikaḻccikaḷai maṭṭilum taruvatu veṟuñ caṭṭakam; kataiyiṉ muṟpakutikkumaṭṭilum kuṟippukaḷ taruvatu kuṟai caṭṭakam; kataiyiṉ muṟpakutiyai maṭṭilum varaintu viṭṭuviṭuvatu kuṟaikatai.  niṟai caṭṭakam muṟṟum teriyāk kataikkum, veṟuñ caṭṭakam ciṟitē terinta kataikkum, kuṟai caṭṭakam arai kuṟaiyāyt terinta kataikkum, kuṟai katai kataiyiṉ piṟpakutiyai ūkittu aṟiyak kūṭiya kataikkum ēṟṟavai.  māṇākkariṉ paṭṭaṟivu, moḻiyaṟivu, paṭikkum vakuppu ākiyavaṟṟiṟku ēṟṟavāṟu ivai tarappeṟum.
2. paṭak kaṭṭurai: moḻip pāṭattil paṭaṅkaḷiṉ payaṉkaḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷil kaṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  kaṭṭuraip payiṟciyilum pāṭaṅkaḷ ciṟantatōr iṭattaip peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  paṭaṅkaḷ vāymoḻik kaṭṭuraikkēṟṟa poruḷkaḷaiyum kuṟippukaḷaiyum nalkukiṉṟaṉa.  paṭak kaṭṭuraik kataik kaṭṭuraikku aṭutta nilaiyil arumai nōkki amaikiṉṟatu.  kataik kaṭṭuraiyil māṇākkarkaḷ paṭitta allatu kēṭṭa kataikaḷaic curukki oḻuṅkupaṭutti eḻutuvar; allatu caṭṭakak kuṟippukaḷaip perukki eḻutuvar.  āṉāl, paṭakkaṭṭuraiyil paṭattil kāṇum kāṭciyaiyō allatu potintu kiṭakkum kuṟippukaḷaiyum vivaraṅkaḷaiyumō kuṟippāl aṟintu oḻuṅku ceytu vakaippaṭutti eḻutavēṇṭum.  itil piṟar utavi kuṟaivu; māṇākkarkaḷ tāmāka eḻutum muyaṟciyē atikam.
paṭak kaṭṭuraip pāṭam naṭattuvatil mūṉṟu paṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  mutalāvatu: āciriyariṉ āyattam.  āciriyar paṭattiṉ uyirnilai, ataṟku utavum ciṟu vivaraṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṭattai uṟṟu nōkki eḻutivaittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ paṭṭaṟivu, paṭa aṟivu, parivu, coṟkaḷañciya aṟivu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkēṟṟavāṟu viṉākkaḷai āyattam ceytu koḷḷavēṇṭum.  ivai teḷivāṉa viḷakkattiliruntu toṭaṅkip paṭippaṭiyākap paṭattaip paṟṟiya muḻu vivaraṅkaḷai aṟiyum nōkkattuṭaṉ vaḷarntu koṇṭu cellum nilaiyil amaiya vēṇṭum.
iraṇṭāvatu:  āciriyar māṇākkarkaṭkuppaṭattaik kāṭṭutal, paṭaṅkaḷaik kāṭṭumpoḻutu māṇākkarkaḷ avaṟṟaip pārttu uṟṟu nōkkip pala vivaraṅkaḷaiaṟivar.  paṭaṅkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ kaṇṇaiyum karuttaiyum īrkkum muṟaiyil iruntāl, avarkaḷ ataippaṟṟiya pala viṉākkaḷai viṭuppar; tamakkuḷḷēyē viṉavavum ceyvar.  āciriyar itaṟkup pōtiya aḷavu kālam koṭukkavēṇṭum: ek kāraṇattaik koṇṭum āciriyar kuṟukkiṭṭu paṭattai māṇākkarkaḷ nukartalaik kulaittuviṭāmal atai vaḷarkka vēṇṭum.  vakuppiṉ amaiti kulaiyum eṉṟu eṇṇi atait taṭuttuviṭavum kūṭātu.
mūṉṟāvatu: oḻuṅkāṉa pēccu, cātāraṇamākap paṭattaip paṟṟi nikaḻum pēccai ōr oḻuṅkāṉa muṟaiyil koṇṭu celuttuvatē āciriyariṉ poṟuppākum.  mutalil paṭattai oru muḻup poruḷākap pārkkac ceyta piṟaku, paṭattiṉ pakutikaḷ paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷai nōkkutal vēṇṭum.  piṟaku appaṭattaippaṟṟik kōvaiyākavum toṭarcciyākavum vāymoḻiyil pala viṉākkaḷiṉ tuṇai koṇṭu collac ceyyavēṇṭum; itaik karumpalakaiyilum eḻutavēṇṭum.  pattup patiṉaintu varikaḷ eḻutum aḷavu kaṭṭurai vaḷarntāl pōtumāṉatu piṟaku karumpalakaiyiluḷḷavaṟṟai aḻittu viṭṭuk kaṭṭuraippayiṟci ēṭukaḷil avaṟṟai eḻutac ceyya vēṇṭum.
paṭaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu pala nuṭpamāṉa payiṟcikaḷaiyum āyattam ceyya iyalum; avaṟṟai mēl vakuppu māṇākkar kaṭkum koṭukkalām.  tamiḻppāṭa mallāta tamiḻ moḻiyil payilum piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷilum ivvita payiṟcikaḷai amaikkalām; ippāṭaṅkaḷai eḷitākavum kavarcci tarum muṟaiyilu kaṟpip pataṟku ivai peritum tuṇai ceyyum eṉpataṟku aiyamillai.
3. kaṭitam varaital: kaṭitam varaital paḷḷi vāḻvilum, piṟkāla vāḻvilum ōr iṉṟiyamaiyāta iṭattaip peṟṟuḷḷatu.  putiya pāṭattiṭṭattil itu mikavum valiyuṟuttap peṟukiṉṟatu.  vēlai tēṭutaṟkētuvāṉa viṇṇappamum parinturaiyum, pērūtiyattiṟ kētuvāṉa vaṇika eḻuttup pōkkuvaravum, peru naṉmaiyai viḷaikkum muṟaiyīṭum vēṇṭukōḷum kaṭita vakaikaḷē, eṇṇip pārttāl kaṭitam eṉpatu eḻutappeṟum pēccē eṉpatu pulaṉākum.  oruvarukku muṉpirunta, tiṟamaiyākavum teḷivākavum muṟaiyākavum iṉimaiyākavum pēcuvatāl peṟum payaṉkaḷai ellām tolaiviliruntē aṅṅaṉam eḻutuvatālum peṟakkūṭum.  āṅkilam pōṉṟa ayal moḻiyil kaṭitam varaital oru kalaiyāka vaḷarntuḷḷatu; ilakkiyat taṉyaiyum peṟṟuḷḷatu.  javaharlāl nēru taṉ makaḷukku eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷum1; ālivar kōlṭumit eṉpār cīṉat tattuva aṟiñar nilaiyiliruntu eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷum2, ṭākṭar mu. varatarācaṉār eḻutiyuḷḷa kaṭitaṅkaḷum3, centil vēlaṉukkut tiruvēṅkaṭattāṉ eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷum4 pōṉṟavai ilakkiya nilaikku uyarntavai.  tamiḻil cīṭṭuk kavikaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkum kavitaikaḷ yāvum pāvaṭiviluḷḷa kaṭitaṅkaḷē; avai ciṟanta ilakkiyap paṇpukaḷuṭaṉ miḷirpavai.  ātalāl, avai ilakkiyampōṟ pōṟṟappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  eṉavē toṭakka nilai, naṭu nilai, uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ tām peṟum eḻuttu vēlaiyil kaṭitam eḻutalaiyum mukkiyamāṉa uṟuppākakkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
ivviṭattil 1926-ām āṇṭil iṅkilāntu nāṭṭil veḷiyiṭappeṟṟa kalvi ārāyccik kuḻaviṉarāl tayārikkap peṟṟa hāṭō aṟikkai kūṟuvatu eṇṇip pārttaṟkuriyatu; kaṭitam eḻutum kalaiyil ikkālattup paḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷait tiṟamai peṟap payiṟṟuvittaliṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyai mikavum vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟōm.  ak kaṭitaṅkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷ uṇarn taṟiyum vāḻkkai nilaimaikaḷiṉiṉṟum, avarkaḷuṭaiya paṭṭaṟiviṉiṉṟum viḷaivaṉavākavum, kuṟitta nōkkam koṇṭaṉavākavum irukkavēṇṭum....... iṉṉoru aṟiñar kūṟuvatāvatu: māṇākkarkaḷ tammuṭaiya paḷḷi vāḻvil virivāṉa kaṭṭuraikaḷ eḻutip payiluvatil evvaḷavō kālam celuttip pāṭupaṭukiṉṟārkaḷeṉiṉum, piṟkālattil mikac cilarē avvakaik kaṭṭuraikaḷ eḻuta nēriṭukiṉṟatu; āṉāl, ellōrukkumē eppoḻutum kaṭitam eḻutum tēvai ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.  palar paṟpala kaṭitaṅkaḷ eḻutukiṉṟaṉar.  avarkaḷ eḻutum akkaṭitaṅkaḷaic cevvaiyāka eḻutap payiṟṟuvittal mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum.5  inta iraṇṭu kūṟṟukkaḷaiyum cintittup pārttāl kaṭitam varaital paḷḷikaḷil kaṟpikkavēṇṭiyataṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyai  ōraḷavu naṉku aṟiyalām.
kaṭitaṅkaḷ palavakaippaṭum.  avaṟṟait taṉippaṭṭavar kaṭitaṅkaḷ, uṟavāṭaṟ kaṭitaṅkaḷ, toḻil muṟaik kaṭitaṅkaḷ eṉṟu mūṉṟu vitamākap pirikkalām.  kaṇavaṉ maṉaivikku eḻutuvatu, maṉaivi kaṇavaṉukku eḻutuvatu, tantai makaṉukku eḻutuvatu, makaṉ tantaikku eḻutuvatu pōṉṟa neruṅkiya uṟavumuṟaippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷaik koṇṭa kaṭitaṅkaḷ mutalvakaiyuḷ aṭaṅkum.  uṟaviṉarukkuḷ eḻutap peṟum kaṭitaṅkaḷ, naṇparkaḷukku eḻutappeṟum kaṭitaṅkaḷ, aḻaippitaḻkaḷ, pārāṭṭuk kaṭitaṅkaḷ, vāḻttuk kaṭitaṅkaḷ ākiyavai iraṇṭāvatu pakutiyil aṭaṅkum.  aluvaṟ kaṭitaṅkaḷ,. vaṇikak kaṭitaṅkaḷ, ceytittāḷukku eḻutap peṟum kaṭitaṅkaḷ, viṭumuṟaik kaṭitaṅkaḷ, viṇṇappak kaṭitaṅkaḷ, muṟaiyīṭṭuk kaṭitaṅkaḷ (Written complaint) parinturaik kaṭitaṅkaḷ (Letters of recommendation) ākiyavai mūṉṟāvatu vakaiyuḷ aṭaṅkum.
ovvoruvakaik kaṭitattiṟkum ataṟkeṉa amainta taṉivaṭivamuṇṭu.  āyiṉum, ellāvakaik kaṭitaṅkaḷukkum potuvāṉa cila kaṭita uṟuppukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  avai talaippu, koḷu, viḷi, ceyti, uṟavut toṭar moḻi, kainnāṭṭu, mukavari eṉpavai.  ivaṟṟuḷ viḷiyum uṟavut toṭar moḻiyum eḻutappeṟum kaṭita vakaikkēṟpa ciṟcila marapuc collākavum, coṟṟoṭarākavum amaiyum.  piṟavuṟuppukkaḷ vevvēṟu coṟkaḷālum coṟṟoṭarkaḷālum  amaiyiṉum ovvoru potu vāypāṭṭait taḻuviyavaiyākavē irukkum.  avai ovvoṉṟaiyumpaṟṟi īṇṭu ciṟitu nōkkuvōm.
talaippu: evvakaippaṭṭa kaṭitamāka iruntālum aḵtu eḻutappeṟṟa iṭamum nāḷum kuṟikkappeṟal vēṇṭum.  uṟavu, naṭpu, vaṇikam mutaliyavaipaṟṟi eḻutum kaṭitaṅkaḷil kaṭitattiṉ mutaṟpakka valappuṟamēl mūlaiyil iṭam mēlum, tēti kīḻumāka eḻutappeṟal vēṇṭum.  viṇṇappak kaṭitaṅkaḷilum, muṟaiyīṭukaḷilum naṟcāṉṟitaḻkaḷilum, avai iṟutippakka iṭappuṟak kīḻ mūlaiyiltāṉ kuṟikkappeṟum.  koḷu:  talaippiṟkuc caṟṟu kīḻāka iṉṉāriṭamiruntu iṉṉārukku eṉṟō, iṉṉār iṉṉārukku eḻutuvatu eṉṟō ceyti toṭaṅka muṉ eḻutappeṟum mukavariyē itu.  uṟavāṭaṟ kaṭitaṅkaḷilum vaṇikak kaṭitaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa koḷu curukkam paṟṟi kaṭitam viṭuppōraik kuṟiyātu kaṭitam viṭukkap peṟuvōrai maṭṭum kuṟippatuṇṭu, naṇparukkum aṟimukamākātavarukkum eḻutum uṟavāṭaṟ kaṭitaṅkaḷ peṟumpālum koḷuviṉṟiyē amaiyum.  viḷi: koḷuviṟkak kīḻāka iṭappuṟattil taṉiyāyk kaṭitam peṟuvōrai ceyti eḻutat toṭaṅkamuṉ viḷippatē viḷi eṉappaṭum.  aḵtu avarkaḷiṉ takutiyum paḻamaiyum urimaiyum karuti viḷiyai iṭattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu orumaiyākavō uyarvup paṉmaiyākavō amaikkalām.  peṟuvōr oru kuḻuvākavō palarākavō iruppiṉ paṉmaiyākkalām.  ivviḷikkumuṉ avaravar takutikkēṟṟa aṉpu vaṇakkaṅkaḷaik kuṟikkum aṭaimoḻikaḷaiyum cērkkalām.  ceyti: itu viḷikkuk kīḻāka varaiyappaṭuvatu.  ataṉ aḷavum poruḷum avvak kaṭitavakaiyaiyum eḻutuvōriṉ karuttaiyum poṟuttavai.  ceyti nīṇṭatākavum pala  karuttupaṟṟiyatākavum iruntāl, ataip pala pattikaḷākap pirittu eḻutavēṇṭum.  iṉiya eḷiya teḷivāṉa ciṟu vākkiya naṭaiyil ceytiyai amaikka vēṇṭum.  kaṭitavakaiyai yoṭṭi naṭai vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟalām.  orē muṟaiyil eḻutavēṇṭiya aṉaittaiyum muṟṟup peṟum paṭi eḻutiviṭal vēṇṭum.  oru kaṭitattil piṉvaraivāka (Post script) varaivatum, toṭarntu iṉṉoru kaṭitam varaivatum eṇṇāmaṟ ceyyum iyalpaiyum oḻuṅkīṉattaiyum tiṟamaiyiṉmaiyayum kāṭṭum.  aṭittaṭittu eḻutātu, iyaṉṟavarai nērākavum nērttiyākavum cevvai yākavum varaiyuṅ kaṭitaṅkaḷ pakaivarālum virumpi ēṟṟuk koḷḷappeṟum; avarkaḷ maṉattaiyum māṟṟak kūṭum.  niṟuttaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷaic cariyāka iṭṭu eḻututal vēṇṭum.  uṟavut toṭarmoḻi: kaṭitam viṭuppōrukkum, peṟuvōrukkum uḷḷa uṟavaik kāṭṭuvatu itu; kīḻāka valappuṟattil varaiyappaṭuvatu.  kaṭitavakaik kēṟṟavāṟu itu māṟupaṭum .  kainnāṭṭu: uṟavut toṭar moḻikkuk kīḻāka, kaṭitam viṭuppōriṉ peyar tantai peyariṉ mutaleḻuttuṭaṉ teḷivāka varaiyappaṭutal vēṇṭum.  aṟimukam ākāta varukku eḻutum kaṭitaṅkaḷil teḷivaṟṟa kainnāṭṭāka iruntāl kainnāṭṭiṟkuk kīḻ teḷivākap peyarai eḻutalām.  mukavari: kaṭita uṟaiyiṉ piṉpuṟattil allatu maṭitta kaṭitattiṉ maṟupuṟattil kaṭitam peṟu vōriṉ muḻu mukavariyum teḷivāka eḻutappaṭal vēṇṭum.  añcaṟ kaṭitamāyiṉum āḷ vāyilāy viṭukkum kaṭitamāyiṉum valappuṟa mūlaiyil muttirai yoṭṭaṟ kuriya iṭattai viṭṭuviṭṭu mukavariyai eḻuta vēṇṭum, āṅkila muṟaippaṭi āḷ peyar, aluval, kataveṇ, teru, ūr, añcal nilaiyam, kūṟṟam, kōṭṭam, mānilam, nāṭu eṉṟa varicaiyil iḵtu amaiyum.
4. uraiyāṭal: iruvar oruvarōṭoruvar pēcik koḷvatupōl varaiyappeṟuvatai uraiyāṭal eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar.  ivvuraiyāṭal uṇmaiyāy naṭanta tākavum irukkalām; kaṟpaṉaiyākavum irukkalām.  uraiyāṭṭu varaivu kaṭṭurai varaiviṉum kaṭiṉap payiṟciyākum.  maṉattil iruvaraip paṭaittuk koṇṭu avarkaḷ uraiyāṭuvatupōl nāṭakappāṇiyil uraiyāṭalai varaivataṟkuk kaṟpaṉai yāṟṟalum, kāraṇa kāriya aṟivut tiṟaṉum, nāṭakat tiṟamaiyum mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātavai.  uraiyāṭal varaivatil tērcci peṟum māṇākkar piṟkālattil ciṟanta putiṉa āciriyarākavō nāṭaka nūlāciriyarākavō tikaḻak kūṭum. 
māṇākkarkaḷ varaiyum uraiyāṭalkaḷiṉ naṭai eḷitākavum, iṉitākavum, iruttal vēṇṭum.  ilakkaṇap piḻaikaḷ talaikāṭṭalākātu. koccai moḻikaḷaiyum, iṭakkarc coṟkaḷaiyum, iḻi vaḻakkukaḷaiyum akaṟṟutal vēṇṭum.  makkaḷ aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil pēcum pēccukkaḷai ūṉṟik kavaṉittāl naṭai iyalpāka amaiyum.  aṟiñarkaḷ varainta nāṭakaṅkaḷaiyum puttakaṅkaḷaiyum paṭittāl, vaḻukkaḷaṟṟa naṭai kaivarap peṟum iraṇṭu aḵṟiṇaip poruḷkaḷiṭaiyē uraiyāṭal nikaḻvatupōl kaṟpaṉaiyākavum uraiyāṭalai amaikkalām.
uraiyāṭal varaivatil iṉṟiyamaiyāta kuṟippukkaḷaik kavaṉittal vēṇṭum.  uraiyāṭaliṟkuriya poruḷkaḷai-māṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷai-iru varicaikaḷil kuṟittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.  piṟaku avaṟṟaik kāraṇa kāriya muṟaippaṭi oḻuṅku paṭutta vēṇṭum.  uraiyāṭalai nikaḻttuvatākak kuṟikkap peṟum ipporuḷ māntarkku iṭappaṭum peyar avvak karuttiṟkēṟpap poruttamāyiruttal vēṇṭum.  uraiyāṭal iyalpākat toṭaṅki, iyalpākat toṭarntu,  iyalpāka muṭital naṉṟu; tiṭumeṉat toṭaṅkutalum muṭitalum kūṭā.  uraiyāṭu vōriṉ kūṟṟukkaḷ curuṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum.  iyaṉṟa varai uraiyāṭal paṭippōrkkum kēṭpōrkkum iṉimaiyākavum iṟutiyil oru muṭinta muṭivai uṇarttu vatākavum iruttal nalam.  
uraiyāṭal paṭaippuraiyāka irukkalām (e-ṭu) ōr āciriyarukkum araciyaṟkaṇakkarukkum nikaḻvatu; oru mutalāḷikkum toḻilāḷikkum nikaḻvatu; ōr uḻavaṉukkum oru kait toḻilāḷikkum nikaḻvatu;  iru vaḻippōkkariṭaiyē nikaḻvatu; ākiyavai pōṉṟavai.  uraiyāṭalaik kaṟpaṉaiyākavum nikaḻumpaṭi amaittu eḻutalām6. (e-ṭu) . ōr erutiṟkum oru kutiraikkum nikaḻvatu; oru vīṭṭelikkum oru kāṭṭelikkum nikaḻvatu; oru kaḻutaikkum oru nāykkum nikaḻvatu; ōr uralukkum mattaḷattiṟkum nikaḻvatu; oru puttakattiṟkum ceytit tāḷukkum nikaḻvatu-ākiyavai pōṉṟavai.  coṟ pōrukku ēṟṟa poruḷkaḷaippaṟṟiyum karuttu māṟu pāṭuṭaiya cila poruḷkaḷpaṟṟiyum uraiyāṭalai amaikkalām.  
5. kaṭṭuraikaḷ: kaṭṭuraiyaik kōppurai eṉṟār maṟaimalai aṭikaḷ.  oru poruḷaip paṟṟiyap pala karuttukkaḷai icaittuk kōvaippaṭa varaiyappaṭum coṟ perukkē kaṭṭuraiyākum.  kaṭṭurai pala pattikaḷaik koṇṭa oru piṇṭam; pattikaḷ pala vākkiyaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa ciṟu ciṟu tokutikaḷ.  oru kaṭṭuraiyai eḻutum muṟaiyait terintu koḷḷa vēṇṭumāṉāl vākkiya amaippu, kaṭṭurai amaippu, pattiyamaippu ākiya mūṉṟaiyum paṟṟi ōraḷavu terintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
vākkiya amaippu:  ovvoru vākkiyattilum orumaip pāṭu amaintirukka vēṇṭum.  ovvoru vākkiyamum pattikkup putiya karuttukkaḷait tarum pāṅkil amaiya vēṇṭum.  ovvoru vākkiyattilum eṇṇattiṟku appāṟpaṭṭa oṉṟum cērntiruttal kūṭātu.  nīṇṭa vākkiyaṅkaḷait tavirkkak kūṭātāyiṉum, ciṟu vākkiyaṅkaḷāl eḷiya iṉiya naṭaiyai amaittuk karuttai nēraṭiyāka viḷakkalām eṉpatai māṇākkar aṟiyumpaṭi ceytal vēṇṭum.  orē vitamāṉa vākkiyaṅkaḷāka iruntālum naṭai naṉṟāka irātu.  taṉi vākkiyam, kalavai vākkiyam pōṉṟa palvēṟu vākkiya vakaikaḷ oru kaṭṭuraiyil amaintāl naṭaiyaḻaku, poruḷ teḷivu, karuttōṭṭam mutaliya paṇpukaḷ kaṭṭuraiyil amaintu kaṭṭuraiyaik ciṟappikkum.  palvēṟu vākkiya vakaikaḷaippaṟṟi moḻip payiṟcikaḷ eṉpataṉ kīḻ kūṟap paṭṭiruppaṉavaṟṟai īṇṭu niṉaivu kūrka.  ivaṟṟiṉ virivaiyum vākkiya amaippaippaṟṟiya coṉ muṟai, colliṭaiyīṭu, mutaṉmai nilai, aṇmai nilai, teḷivu, poruḷ valimai, tiṭṭam, iṉimai, curukkam, tūymai, icaivu, vākkiyattiṉ muṉpiṉ muṭippukaḷ, vākkiyattiṉ aḷavu, oru pōku amaippu, ummait toṭar, viṉā, marapukaḷ mutaliya ceytikaḷaip paṟṟiyum uriya nūlkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷka.
pattiyamaippu: pattiyamaippu karuttaṭaivupaṟṟiya kāraṇa kāriyat toṭarpuṭaiyatēyaṉṟi avaravar viruppam pōla ceyaṟkai muṟaiyil pirittukkoḷvataṉṟu; aḵtu oru muṟaiyai yoṭṭi pirikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.  oru kataiyō kaṭṭuraiyō ivvāṟu pattikaḷākap pirikkap paṭāviṭiṉ, pala pakkaṅkaḷ toṭarcciyāka irukku mātaliṉ, paṭippavarukkum veṟuppum kaḷaippum uṇṭākum; poruḷai uḷaṅkoḷḷutalum aritākum.  paṭippavarukku eḷimaiyāyirukka vēṇṭu meṉṟu karutiyē patti pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉpatai āciriyar māṇākkariṉ kavaṉattiṟkuk koṇṭuvara vēṇṭum.
oru pattiyil pala vākkiyaṅkaḷ irukkum.  vākkiyaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟum poruḷ toṭarpu uṭaiyaṉavāyirukkum.  av vellā vākkiyaṅkaḷum oru karuttiṉai allatu oru ceytiyaik kūṟuvaṉavāka virukkum.  ātaliṉ, oru karuttiṉaik kuṟitta pala vākkiyaṅkaḷiṉ tokutiyē patti eṉappaṭum.  oru katai allatu kaṭṭuraiyiṉ iyalpiṟkēṟpap patti pirikkappaṭum.  katai allatu varalāṟu āyiṉ, oru nikaḻcci muṭintatum putiya patti toṭaṅkappaṭum.  oru pirāṇiyaippaṟṟiya kaṭṭuraiyāyiṉ ataṉ potuvamaippu, uṟuppukkaḷ, vāḻumiṭam, paḻakkam-iyalpu, uṇavu, payaṉ ākiyavaṟṟaip paṟṟip pala pattikaḷ pirikkappaṭum.  ātaliṉ, oru patti ittaṉai vākkiyaṅkaḷai uṭaiyatāyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟāvatu, iṉṉa aḷaviṉatāyirukka vēṇṭu meṉṟāvatu niyati illai.  poruḷiṉ iyalpiṟkut takkavāṟu pattikaḷai vakuttu eḻutuvatē muṟai.
oru pattiyai maṟṟoru pattiyiliruntu vēṟu pirittuk kāṭṭaṟku atait toṭaṅkumpoḻutu iṭappakkattil ciṟitu iṭam viṭṭup putitākat toṭaṅka vēṇṭum.  inta iṭam ituvarai eḻutiya pattikaḷaip paṟṟi niṉaittu, iṉi varap pōkum pattiyaippaṟṟi niṉaittu ivaiyiraṇṭaṟkum oru toṭarpai eṅṅaṉam uṇṭākkuvatu eṉpataik karutuvataṟkāṉa kālattaiyum iṭattaiyum kēṭpōrukkum paṭippōrukkum taruvataṉ aṟikuṟiyāka iṭappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  eṉavē, oru nalla paṭippōraiyum kēṭpōraiyum avarkaḷaṟiyāmalē aṭutta pattikku aḻaittup pōka vēṇṭum.  oru varalāṟṟil nikaḻum ovvoru putu nikaḻcci allatu viḷakkam oru putiya pattiyil toṭaṅkappeṟuvatāl kaṭṭurai vaḷarcciyiṉ pōkku eṅṅaṉam celkiṉṟatu eṉpatu teḷivākap pulaṉākum.
pattiyamaippuppaṟṟiya cila tiṭṭamāṉa neṟi muṟaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟai īṇṭu nōkkuvōm: oru patti orē poruḷai allatu poruṭkūṟākiya karuttaippaṟṟi iruttal vēṇṭum. itu patti yorumaippāṭu eṉap peyar peṟum.  epporuḷāka iruntālum atu pala karuttukkaḷait taṉṉuṭ koṇṭirukkum.  avaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟaiyumpaṟṟi oru patti varaital iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.  oru karuttu pala uṭkaruttukkaḷaik koṇṭatāka iruntāl, avai ovvoṉṟukkum ovvoru patti amaittal avaciyam.
pattiyamaippup paṟṟiya iraṇṭāvatu neṟimuṟai karuttoḻuṅkākum.  itaip pattiyoḻuṅku  eṉpar.  iḵtu akavoḻuṅku, puṟavoḻuṅku eṉa iruvakaippaṭum.  oru pattikkuḷḷē amaintirukkum karuttoḻuṅkai akavoḻuṅ keṉṟum, ataṟkup puṟamāka muṉṉum piṉṉum amaintirukkum karuttoḻuṅkaip puṟavoḻuṅkeṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar.  pattiyiṉ karuttiṉai viḷakkikkūṟum tiṟattil vākkiyaṅkaḷ amaintiruttal pattiyoḻuṅkākum.  potuvāka oru pattiyiṉ mutal vākkiyam pattiyiṉ karuttait toṭaṅka vēṇṭum.  iṭaiyiluḷḷa vākkiyaṅkaḷ poruṭṭoṭarpu kulaiyātu pattiyiṉ karuttai vaḷarkkalām; allatu viḷakkalām; allatu vaṟpuṟuttalām.  iṟuti vākkiyam appattiyiṉ karuttai muṭittu niṟkavēṇṭum.  pattiyiṉ karuttaik koṇṭa vākkiyam karuttu vākkiyam allatu tiṟavu vākkiyam eṉa vaḻaṅkum. itu pattiyiṉ mutal, iṭai, kaṭai, ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil eṅkāvatu irukkakkūṭum.  mutaliliruppatu ciṟappākum.  piñcu tōṉṟik kāyāyp paruttuk kaṉiyāyp paḻuppatu pōlavē, pattiyiṉ karuttum muṉpu tōṉṟi muṟaippaṭa vaḷarntu muṭivil mutirntāl patti ciṟappāka amaiyum.
pattiyamaippiṉ mūṉṟāvatu neṟimuṟai aḷavu vēṟu pāṭu ākum.  aḷavu vēṟupāṭu eṉpatu ovvoru pattiyum kūṟum poruḷiṉ taṉmaikkēṟpa aḷavil curuṅkiyum virintum iruppatu.  itaṉaip pattiyiṉ aḷavu vēṟupāṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar.  oru patti oru vākkiyattiṉālum amaiyalām; pala vākkiyaṅkaḷālum amaiyalām.  pattiyiṉ pērellaiyai varaiyaṟaippaṭuttuvatu iyalā teṉiṉum, oru pakkattiṟku mēl nīḷum pattiyaip pala ciṟu pattikaḷākap pirittukkoḷvatu naṉṟu; varaivu kaṭantu nīṭṭutal poruḷ mayakkattaip payakkam.  ōru pattiyiṉ iṭaiyil mēṟkōṭ ceyyuḷ varumāyiṉ atait taṉittu varaital naṉṟu.  ceyyuḷ vaṭivu keṭātu varaital iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.  mēṟkōḷ ceyyuḷiṉ orē aṭi allatu aṭippakutiyāka viruntāl pattiyōṭu cērttu varaivatāl kuṟṟamillai.
oru kaṭṭuraiyiṉ pattikaḷai mukavuraippatti, māṟu nilaippatti, uṭaṟpatti, muṭivup patti eṉṟu aṟiñar vakaippaṭuttip pēcuvar. mukavuraippattik kaṭṭuraiyait toṭaṅkuvatu. mukavuraiyiliruntu uṭalukkum, uṭaliliruntu muṭiviṟkum aḻaittuc cellum patti māṟunilaip pattiyākum.  uṭaṟpatti kaṭṭuraiyiṉ uyirnilaik karuttai viḷakkuvatu.  muṭivup pattiyil kaṭṭurai muṭivu peṟum.
oru pattiyiṉ aḷavai varaiyaṟai ceyya iyalā teṉiṉum, naṭunilai uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷil payilum māṇākkarkaḷ perumpālum neṭumpattikaḷ eḻutuvatai viṭak kuṟumpattikaḷ eḻutuvatē muṟaiyākum; nalamumākum.  araimaṇi nērattil oṉṟaraip pakka aḷavil piṟar tuṇaiyiṉṟi māṇākkarkaḷē eḻutum ciṟu kaṭṭuraikaḷil allatu kaṭitaṅkaḷil neṭumpattikaḷ amaivatu muṟaiyaṉṟu.  ovvoru putuk karuttukkum putuppatti vēṇṭiyiruppatāl ovvoṉṟum neṭumpattiyāyiruppatu iyalpaṉṟu eṉiṉum, pattikaḷ ellām kuṟukiṉavāyiruntāl kaṭṭurai pārppataṟku aḻakāyirātu.  ākaiyāl vēṟupāṭu karuti orōvaḻi caṟṟu nīṇṭa neṭumpatti oṉṟai āṅkāṅku iṭaiyiṟ cerukuvatāl vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟik kaṭṭuraiyil paṭippōrukkuk kavarcci tōṉṟalām; tōṉṟum.
kaṭṭurai amaippu:  potuvāka ovvoru kaṭṭuraiyum mukavurai, uṭal, muṭivu eṉṟa muppakutikaḷaik koṇṭirukkum.
mukavurai porutta muḷḷatāyum, paṭippōriṉ kavaṉattai īrkkak kūṭiyatāyum, ciṟiyatāyum, kaṭṭuraiyiṉ nōkkattaik kāṭṭuvatāyum amaiyavēṇṭum.  atu paṭippōraik kaṭṭuraiyiṉ uṭalukkuḷ aḻaittuk koṇṭu pōvatāyum irukkavēṇṭum.  eṉavē, kaṭṭuraiyiṉ uṭalukkuc caṭṭakam amaittuk koṇṭu ataṟkēṟṟa tōṟṟuvāy amaippatu ēṟṟatākum.  entak kaṭṭuraiyaiyum mukavuraiyiṉṟit toṭaṅkuvatu mukappu illāta maṇṭapattait toṭaṅkuvatu pōlākum.  varalāṟṟuk kaṭṭurai, varuṇaṉaik kaṭṭurai pōṉṟavaṟṟai mukavuraiyiṉṟiyum toṭaṅkalām.  āyiṉum, aṅkum oru toṭaraḷavākavāvatu mukavurai amaintāltāṉ kaṭṭurai ciṟakkum.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka kamparaip paṟṟi varaiyumpoḻutu ulaka makā kavikaḷuḷ oruvarāṉa kampar eṉṉum toṭar mukavuraiyāka amaiyalām. javaharaip paṟṟi eḻutum kaṭṭuraiyil namatu nāṭṭiṉ tilakamum maṉitaruḷ māṇikkamumāṉa eṉṟa toṭarai mukavuraiyākak koḷḷalām.  cila camayam āḷiṉ piṟappu, poruḷiṉ tōṟṟam, nikaḻcciyiṉ amayam, poruḷiṉ varaiyaṟai ākiyavaṟṟuḷ yātēṉum oṉṟu mukavuraiyāka amaiyavum kūṭum.
uṭalē kaṭṭuraiyiṉ ciṟanta pakutiyākum.  mukavuraikkum muṭivuraikkum iṭaiyil iḵtu irukkum.  eḻuta eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷpaṟṟiya iṉṟiyamaiyāta karuttukkaḷum, viḷakkaṅkaḷum, kuṟippukkaḷum, mēṟkōḷkaḷum,  eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷum ip pakutiyilēyē aṭaṅkiyirukkum. kuṟippiṭṭa kāla aḷavil, iṭa aḷavil aṉaittaiyum eḻuta iyalātatāl, iṉṟiyamai yātaṉa vaṟṟai maṭṭilum tērnteṭuttuk kōvaip paṭa amaikka vēṇṭum.  kaṭṭuraiyiṉ uṭal eṭuttuk koṇṭa poruḷukkēṟpa nīṇṭum, curuṅkiyum pala karuttukkaḷait taḻuviṉatāyum irukkum; innilaiyil uṭal pala pattikaḷaik koṇṭum irukkum.
kaṭṭuraiyiṉ muṭivu paṭippōr maṉattil pacumarattāṇi yeṉap patiyumāṟu amaital vēṇṭum.  oru ceyaliṉ viḷaivu evvaḷavu mukkiyamāṉatō, avvaḷavu mukkiyamāṉatu oru kaṭṭuraiyiṉ muṭivum.  muṭiviṉ vaṭivu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟiṉ curukkattokuppu, mēṟkōḷ, paḻamoḻi.  kaṭṭuraiyāḷariṉ karuttuvākkiyam ivaṟṟuḷ yātāṉum oṉṟāka irukkalām.
oru kaṭṭuraiyiṉ mukavurai, uṭal, muṭivu ākiya mūṉṟum talaiyum uṭalum kālum pōṉṟiruttalāl, ataṟkut takuntavāṟu mukavurai muṭivuraikaḷaik curukkamākavum uṭaluraiyaip perukkamākavum amaittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  kaṭṭuraiyiṉ mutal vākkiyam paṭip pōriṉ vēṭkaiyait tūṇṭak kūṭiyatākavum, iṟuti vākkiyam atait tīrkkak kūṭiyatākavum iruntāl kaṭṭurai ciṟappāka amaiyum.
ciṟanta kaṭṭuraiyiṉ kūṟukaḷ: oru kaṭṭurai ciṟappāka amaiyavēṇṭumāṉāl cilavaṟṟaik kavaṉikka vēṇṭum.  avaiyāvaṉa: orumaippāṭu: orē poruḷaip paṟṟiyatāy maṟṟoṉṟu virikkāmaliruppatē orumaip pāṭākum.  poruṭ polivu: curuṅkac colli viḷaṅka vaikkum muṟaiyil ciṟanta karuttukkaḷ ceṟintiruppataittāṉ poruṭpolivu eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar.  kaṭṭuraip poruḷ cariyāka varaiyaṟukkap peṟutal vēṇṭum.  oḻaṅku: oru poruḷaippaṟṟiya karuttukkaḷai muṉpiṉ toṭarpu keṭātavāṟu muṟaippaṭuttik kōvaippaṭa eḻutuvatai oḻuṅku eṉpar.  pattiyamaippu: ovvoru karuttaiyum taṉippattiyilum, atil pala uṭkaruttukkaḷ irukkumāyiṉ avai ovvoṉṟaiyum taṉittaṉi pattikaḷilum amaikkavēṇṭum.  naṭai: eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷukkēṟpa naṭai amaital ciṟappākum.  eḻutuvōr tam iyalpāṉa naṭaiyil eḻuttuppiḻai, coṟ piḻai, puṇarccip piḻai, vākkiyap piḻai, poruṭpiḻai, marapup piḻai mutaliya piḻaikaḷiṉṟit tūymaiyāṉa moḻiyil eḻutavēṇṭum.  potuvāka urai naṭaiyil teḷivu, ceṟivu, iṉimai ākiya mūṉṟu paṇpukaḷ amaital vēṇṭum naṭaiyil teḷivu iruntāltāṉ paṭippavar atil kūṟappaṭṭirukkum poruḷai aṟiya muṭiyum.  eṉavē, itu mutaṉmaiyāka vēṇṭappaṭuvatu. ceṟivu eṉṉum kuṇam kaṟpōrait kaṭṭuraiyil īṭupaṭac ceyyum. nūlkaḷil veṟupput tōṉṟāmal viruppuṟac ceyvatu iṉimai eṉṉum kuṇamākum,7 takucol, iṉcol, iṇaimoḻi, maraputtoṭar, paḻamoḻi, aṇi moḻi mutaliyavai naṭaiyaic ciṟappikkum, tamiḻccollirukka ayaṟcollai vaḻaṅkutal kūṭātu.  teḷivum tuppuravum: kaṭṭuraiyai iruṇṭa maiyil teḷivāka aṭittaṭittu eḻutāmal eḻututal naṉṟu. kūṭṭeḻuttukkaḷaip payaṉ paṭuttutal .kūṭātu. porutta vītam: ovvoru poruṭ pakutikkum karuttiṟkum ēṟpa nīṭṭiyum curukkiyum eḻututal avaciyam. aḷavu mikāmai: ennak kaṭṭuraiyum kuṟitta aḷavukku mēl pōkakkūṭātu.  araittāḷ pakkattil naṭunilaippaḷḷi māṇākkar oru pakkamum, uyarnilaippaḷḷi māṇākkar oṉṟaraip pakkamum,   iṭai nilai vakuppu (Intermediate) māṇākkar iru pakkaṅkaḷum kalaiyiḷaiñar vakuppu (B.A) māṇākkar mūṉṟu pakkaṅkaḷum eḻututal muṟaiyākum.  muṭipu: itu kaṭṭuraiyāḷariṉ karuttai valiyuṟutta vēṇṭum.  kaṭṭuraip poruḷ karuttuc ciṟappu, ētuvum cāṉṟum kūṟal, piṟaṉkōḷ maṟuppu, mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭal, eṭuttuk kāṭṭuṭaimai mutaliyavaṟṟāl ciṟantu valiyuṟum.
kaṭṭurai vakaikaḷ:  kaṭṭuraikaḷaip pattu vakaiyāka aṟiñarkaḷ pakuttup pēcuvar.  oru poruḷiṉ varalāṟṟaik kūṟuvatu varalāṟṟuk kaṭṭurai.  .itu varalāṟu, vāḻkkai varalāṟu, katai, nikaḻcci eṉṉum nāṉkiṉuḷ oṉṟupaṟṟi varum. (e-ṭu) iraṇṭām ulakap perum pōr, kāntiyaṭikaḷ, puliyum iṭaic ciṟuvaṉum, kācumīrat tollai eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai.  oru poruḷai varuṇittuk kūṟuvatu varuṇaṉaik kaṭṭurai.  itil nāṭu, nakaram, malai, āṟu, kaṭal, maḻai, paruva kālaṅkaḷ, iyaṟkaiyiṉ aticayam mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṟṟiyaṉavum, pukaivaṇṭip pirayāṇam pōṉṟa pirayāṇampaṟṟiyaṉavum, kaittoḻil naṭaipeṟum muṟai mutaliyaṉavaṟṟaip paṟṟiyaṉavum, putaiyuṇṭa  nakaraṅkaḷ, kōvilkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṟṟiyaṉavum aṭaṅkum. (e-ṭu) imayam, oru maḻaināḷ, oru tērtal kāṭci, makāpalipurac ciṟpam, kappal pirayāṇam, muttuk kuḷippu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai.  oru poruḷai viḷakkik kūṟuvatu viḷakkiyaṟ kaṭṭurai.  oru paḻamoḻi yaikkoṇṭu atai viḷakki eḻutuvatu pōṉṟa kaṭṭuraiyākum itu. (e-ṭu). vāṉoli, maṟumalarcci, kāṟṟuḷḷa pōtē tūṟṟikkoḷ , taṟkāla aṭimaittaṉam eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai. oru poruḷaippaṟṟic cintaṉaiyil amiḻn turaippatu cintaṉaik kaṭṭurai.  vāymai, vīram, nāṭṭup paṟṟu,  muyaṟci ivaipōṉṟa paṇpukaḷaiyum, camūkam, araciyal, kuṭumpam ivaṟṟait taḻuviya poruḷkaḷākiya celvam, vaṟumai, kuṭiyāṭciyurimai, kalvi, vāṇikam mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum, vētāntam, camayam ivaṟṟait taḻuviya poruḷkaḷaippaṟṟiyum eḻutappeṟum kaṭṭuraikaḷaic cinkaṉaik kaṭṭurai eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. (e-ṭu) paḻaiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum, nalvāḻkkaiyē naṟcamayam, peruntakai yār? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai.  illāta nilaimaiyai iruppatākap pāvittu eḻutuvatu pāṇippuk kaṭṭurai. (e-ṭu) nī mutamaiccaṉāṉāl nāṭṭiṟku eṉṉa naṉmai ceyvāy? maṉitaṉ cirañcīviyāyiruntāl eṉṉeṉṉa nērntirukkum? pulavarellām peruñ celvarā yiruntiruppiṉ ilakkiyam tōṉṟiyirukkumā? kaṇavaṉ kolaiyuṇṭataiyaṟinta kaṇṇaki maṉanilai evvāṟu iruntirukkum? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai.  oru poruḷait tarukkamuṟaiyiṟ kūṟuvatu tarukkiyaṟ kaṭṭuraiyākum.  (e-ṭu). aṟiviyalāl naṉmaiyā? tīmaiyā? cōtiṭam pārppatu naṉmaiyā tīmaiyā? marakkaṟiyūṇ aruḷuṭaimaiyiṉ aṟikuṟiyā? iṉa uṇarcci nallatā? irappōrkkītalellām aṟamākumā? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai. oru poruḷai ārāyntu kūṟuvatu ārāyccik kaṭṭuraiyākum. (e-ṭu). akattiyar varalāṟu, vaḷḷuvar camayam, māṇikka vācakar kālam, tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai.  oru poruḷaik kaṇṭittu allatu paḻittuk kūṟuvatu aṅkatak kaṭṭuraiyākum.  atu cemporuḷ aṅkatam, paḻikarappu aṅkatam eṉa iruvakaippaṭum.  veḷip paṭaiyāyp paḻippatu cemporuḷaṅkatam: maṟaivāyp paḻippatu paḻikarappaṅkatam,  (e-ṭu) maṟumalarcci, kuṭiyiṉ perumai, cila kaṭaṉkārar ceyyum koṭumai, kaiyūṭṭu vāṅkal, celvac cerukku eṉpavaṟṟaippaṟṟi ittakaiya kaṭṭuraikaḷ varaiyalām.  oru poruḷaip pukaḻntu kūṟuvatu pukaḻccik kaṭṭuraiyākum.  atu cemporuṭ pukaḻcci, vañcakap pukaḻcci eṉa iruvakaippaṭum.  veḷippaṭaiyāyp pukaḻvatu cemporuṭ pukaḻcci; maṟaivāyp pukaḻvatu vañcakap pukaḻcci. (e-ṭu) āṅkilar paṇpāṭu, tiruvaḷḷuvar, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, erutu, nāy eṉpaṉavaṟṟai ivvāṟu varaiyalām.  oru poruḷiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷaiyum kuṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum naṭunilaiyāy eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu nōṭṭak kaṭṭuraiyākum. (e-ṭu). kamparāmāyaṇam, purāṇak kalvi, tamiḻilakkiyam, periya purāṇam eṉṟa poruḷpaṟṟi ivvakaik kaṭṭuraikaḷ varaiyalām.  īṇṭup pirittuk kāṭṭappeṟṟavai oṉṟaiyoṉṟu taḻuvāta paṭi muṟṟum vēṟupaṭṭavaiyalla.  ovvōr iyalpiṉ perum pāṉmaipaṟṟi avai aṅṅaṉam pirikkappeṟṟaṉa.  iṉṉum cilar vēṟu vitamākavum pakuttuk kūṟuvar. 
6. curukki varaital: virivāṉa ōr urainaṭaip pakutiyaip poruḷ keṭātavāṟu curukkamāka eḻutuvataic curukki varaital eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar. oru pakutiyai evvaḷavu vēṇṭu māṉālum curukki eḻutalām; itaṟ keṉat taṉippaṭṭa viti oṉṟumillai.  eṉiṉum, poruḷ keṭātavāṟu curukka vēṇṭiyiruppatāl, koṭukkappaṭṭa pakutiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷil mūṉṟil oru pakutikkumēl curukkam koṇṭiruttalākātu eṉṟu koḷḷalām. 
curukki varaitaṟ payiṟci vāḻkkaikku mikavum iṉṟiyamai yātatu; vāḻkkaiyil pala vakaiyil payaṉpaṭuvatu.  virivurai yāḷarum, coṟpoḻivāḷarum urai nikaḻttum poḻutu kuṟippeṭuttuk koḷḷa ip payiṟci mikavum tuṇai ceykiṉṟatu; nūlakaṅkaḷil paṭippōr mukkiyap poruḷkaḷaic curukki varaintukoḷḷap payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu.  ceytittāḷkaḷil paṇiyāṟṟum nirūparkaḷ, āciriyarkaḷ, tuṇaiyāciriyarkaḷ, patippāḷarkaḷ, eḻuttāḷarkaḷ mutaliyōrkku ippayiṟci mikavum tuṇai ceyyum eṉpatil aiyamillē.  varuṅkāla āṭci muṟai, vaṇika aluvalakaṅkaḷ, nāṭu ūr āḷum kaḻakaṅkaḷ, aṟaṅkūṟu avaiyaṅkaḷ mutaliya ellāt tuṟaikaḷum tāymoḻiyil naṭaipeṟum eṉṟa aṟikuṟikaḷ tōṉṟi varukiṉṟaṉa;* moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil mānilap pirivu naṭaipeṟavēṇṭum eṉṟa avāvum niṟaivēṟiviṭṭatu.  etirkālattil ittakaiya aluvalakaṅkaḷil paṇiyāṟṟap pōkum. iḷaiñarkaḷukku ippayiṟci peruntuṇaiyāka irukkum.  paḷḷiyilēyē ippayiṟci kalvi kaṟṟalukkut tuṇaiyāka uḷḷatu.  tāymoḻi vāyilākak kalvi kaṟṟal naṭaipeṟum ikkālattil oru pakutiyaik karuttūṉṟip paṭittaṟkum, collaiyum poruḷaiyum kuṟitta aḷaviṟ kaṇittaṟivataṟkum, varaivup payiṟci taruvataṟkum ip payiṟci payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu. 
curukki varaitalil ceṟivum teḷivum mikavum iṉṟiya maiyāta paṇpukaḷākum.  collavēṇṭiyavaṟṟai nērāka aiyam tiripukaḷiṉṟi eṭuttuk kūṟavēṇṭum.kūṟuvatu paṭippōrukkum kēṭpōrukkum eḷitil viḷaṅkumpaṭi amaiyavēṇṭum.  ittakaiya nalliyalpukaḷ amaiya vēṇṭumāṉāl, curukki varaitalil cila vitikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭum.  avaṟṟaik curukkamāka īṇṭu taruvōm: koṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷa urai naṭaip pakutiyaip poruḷ viḷaṅkumvarai mīṇṭum mīṇṭum karuttūṉṟip paṭikkavēṇṭum.  parup poruḷai aṟintapiṉ nuṇporuḷai nōkki uṇartalvēṇṭum.  poruḷ teriyāta oru collaikkūṭa viṭṭuviṭātu akarātiyiṉ tuṇaik koṇṭu poruḷ teriyavēṇṭum.  poruḷaiyaṟintavuṭaṉ ataṟkēṟṟa talaippaic curukkamāṉa coṟṟoṭarāl amaittal vēṇṭum. mūlap pakutiyiliruntu mukkiyamāṉa kuṟippukkaḷaip piritteṭuttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum; ivaṟṟait taṉittāḷil kuṟittukkoḷḷal nalam mūlappakutiyil aṭaiyāḷamiṭutal cariyaṉṟu.  piṟaku mūlappakutiyiṉ aḷavaiyum col- leṇṇikkaiyaiyum kavaṉittu atil mūṉṟiloru paṅku amaiyumāṟu curukki eḻutat toṭaṅka vēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷ mutaṉ muṟaiyilēyē tiruttamāka eḻutuvatu kaṭiṉamātaliṉ mutalil karaṭṭu varaivu (Rough draft) varaintukoṇṭu piṟaku atiliruntu cevvaip paṭi (Fair copy) eṭuttuk koḷḷalām curukki varaiyum poḻutu conta naṭaiyil eḻutavēṇṭum; orē toṭarpuṭaṉum mūlattai nōkkāmalēyē poruḷ muḻuvataiyum teḷivāka aṟiyakkūṭiyatākavum irukka vēṇṭum; atil em muṟaiyilum contak karuttai ēṟṟutalō mūlak karuttai māṟṟutalō kūṭātu.  curukki varaiyumpoḻutu mukkiyamallāta vivaraṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷaiyum nīkkiviṭalām; kūṟiyatu kūṟalai vilakkalām; neṭu vākkiyaṅkaḷaik kuṟu vākkiyaṅkaḷākavum, kuṟu vākkiyaṅkaḷait toṭar moḻikaḷākavum, toṭar moḻikaḷaic coṟkaḷākavum curukkalām.  ivaṟṟil iṉṉiṉṉavaitām mukkiyam eṉṟu varaiyaṟai ceyya iyalātu; avai curukkavēṇṭiya aḷavai yoṭṭi māṟupaṭum. 
7. perukki varaital:- oru vākkiyattai allatu kūṟṟai oru pakutiyākavum, oru pattiyaip pala pattikaḷuḷḷa ciṟu kaṭṭuraiyākavum viritteḻutal perukki varaital eṉappaṭum.  itu curukki varaitalukku nēr etirāṉa payiṟciyākum.  puttaka āciriyarukkum, coṟpoḻivāḷarukkum ippayiṟci peritu payaṉ tarum.  oru vākkiyattai allatu paḻamoḻiyai oru pattiyāka virikkum payiṟci kīḻ vakuppu māṇākkarkaṭku ēṟṟatu; oru pattiyaik kaṭṭuraiyāka virikkum payiṟci mēl vakuppu māṇākkarkaṭku ēṟṟatu.
perukki varaitalilum kavaṉattiṟkuriya cila vitikaḷ uḷa.  avaṟṟaip piṉpaṟṟiṉāl perukki varaital cemmaiyāka amaiyum.  perukki varaiya vēṇṭum poruḷai naṉku cintittu ēṟṟa talaipput tarutal vēṇṭum.  ataṟkup piṟaku viḷakkam, ētu, eṭuttukkāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟāl virikka vēṇṭum.  cērkkum ceytikaḷaṉaittum, mūlap poruḷai yoṭṭiyē yiruttal vēṇṭum,  mūlappakuti aṇiyāyiruppiṉ atait teḷivāṉa naṭaiyil viḷakki varaital vēṇṭum.  perumpālum oru kūṟṟāka irukkum mūlappakuti oru paṭippiṉai pōṉṟa muṭipāka irukkumātalāl, am muṭipiṟ kēṟṟa karuttukkaḷai niralpaṭa amaikka vēṇṭum.  mūlappakuti oru pakutiyāka iruppiṉ atiluḷḷa ovvōr uṭkaruttiṟkum ovvoru pattiyai amaittal vēṇṭum.  perukki varaitalilum mutalil karaṭṭu varaivu eḻuti, piṟaku cevvaippaṭi eṭukkum muṟaiyaik kaiyāḷalām.
8. poḻippurai varaital:- oru ceyyuḷiṉ poruḷai yellām urainaṭaiyil tiraṭṭi varaivataip poḻippurai varaivu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar.  tamiḻil urainaṭaip pakutiyai viḷakkivaraiyum payiṟciyai viḷakka naṭai  varaivu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭalām.  tamiḻk kaṭṭuraip payiṟciyil ivvita poḻippuraip payiṟcikaḷ naṭaimuṟaiyil illai.  pulavar pukumukat tērviltāṉ ittakaiya payiṟci tarappaṭukiṉṟatu.  tāymoḻi vaḷarcciyil ūkkam celuttuvārkku ittakaiya payiṟcikaḷum peruntuṇaiyāka irukkum.  ellā nilai vakuppukkaḷilum ippayiṟcikaḷaittara āciriyarkaḷ muyaṟci eṭukkavēṇṭum.  poḻippurai varaiviṉāl pala naṉmaikaḷ uṇṭu.  itu oru ceyyuḷaik karuttūṉṟip paṭikkum paḻakkattai māṇākkarkaḷiṭam uṇṭākkum; maṟaintum aritākavum uḷḷa poruḷai nuṉittaṟiyum āṟṟalai eytuvikkum.  uḷḷak karuttaic ciṟanta muṟaiyil nalla moḻiyil veḷiyiṭum vāyppiṉai nalkum. māṇākkarkaḷ ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ poruḷai viḷakkum tiṟaṉaiyum itaṉāl peṟuvar.
poḻippurai naṭaimāṟi eḻutappeṟal vēṇṭum. mūlattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭavai aṉaittum poḻippuraiyil amaiya vēṇṭum.  oru ceyyuḷai muṟṟum nōkki ataṉ muḻup poruḷai uṇarnta piṟakutāṉ poḻippuraiyai varaiyavēṇṭum.  poḻippurai mūlattiṉ tuṇaiyiṉṟi taṉṉil tāṉē niṟaiyuḷḷatāka iruttal vēṇṭum; atu taṉi ilakkiyap pakutiyāka amaiyumāṟu iruntāl nalam.  mūlattaiyaṉṟi vēṟoṉṟaiyum poḻippuraiyil cērttal ākātu.  ceyyuḷil kāṇappeṟum ceyyuṭkē uriya coṟkaḷaiyum coṟṟoṭar kaḷaiyum ulaka naṭaivaṭivattiṟku māṟṟi eḻuta vēṇṭum; muṟaimāṟiyiruntāl avaṟṟai urainaṭai  muṟaiyilum,  aṇi naṭaiyiliruntāl avaṟṟai iyalpāṉa muṟaiyilum māṟṟiyamaikka vēṇṭum.  
poḻippurai varaivatil ikkuṟippukkaḷ ōraḷavu tuṇai ceyyum: poḻippuraiyai varaiyum muṉ mūlappakutiyai oru muṟaikkup palamuṟai naṉku paṭittu parupporuḷait teḷivāka aṟitalvēṇṭum.  parupporuḷai aṟinta piṟaku appakutiyil kāṇappeṟum ciṟu ceytikaḷ, aṭai moḻit toṭarporuḷ aruñcoṟ aṇipporuḷ poruḷ pōṉṟa nuṇporuḷkaḷai uṇaravēṇṭum.  akarātiyai itaṟkut tuṇaiyāka vaittukkoḷḷalām.  muṟṟum poru-ḷuṇarnta piṟaku aḷavāṉa vākkiyaṅkaḷil poḻippuraiyai varaiya vēṇṭum.  poruḷ teḷivāka-vuḷḷa coṟkaḷai māṟṟavēṇṭiyatillai; ceyyuṭkē ciṟappākavuḷḷa coṟkaḷaiyum, col vaṭivaṅkaḷaiyum maṭṭilum māṟṟiṉāl pōtumāṉatu.  poḻippuraikkup puṟampāka evvita viḷakkaṅkaḷum iruttal kūṭātu; aṉaittum poḻippuraiyiṉ kūṟākavē amaintuviṭal vēṇṭum.  ceyyuṭkaḷil kāṇappeṟum nēr kūṟṟukkaḷai yellām appaṭiyē poḻippuraiyil amaittal naṉṟu.  oru muṟaiyil tiruttamāka eḻuta iyalātavar karaṭṭu varaivu varaintukoṇṭu piṟaku cevvaippaṭi eṭukka vēṇṭum.
9. karuttuṇar payiṟcikaḷ : ellā nilaikaḷilum karuttuṇar payiṟcikaḷ nalla kaṭṭuraip payiṟcikaḷāka amaiyum. ceyyuṭ pakutikaḷ, urainaṭaip pakutikaḷ, paṭaṅkaḷ, varai pāṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟaik kaiyāṇṭu ippayiṟcikaḷait taralām.  ippayiṟcikaḷai iraṇṭu vitamāka eḻutac ceyyalām.  appakutikaḷaiyō paṭaṅkaḷaiyō māṇākkarkaḷiṭam koṭuttu cila viṉākkaḷukku viṭai kaḷeḻutac ceyyalām; allatu avai kuṟikkum tiraṇṭa karuttai oru kuṟippiṭṭa aḷavil eḻutat tūṇṭalām.  ip payiṟcikaḷai vāymoḻi-yākavum naṭattalām; eḻuttup payiṟcikaḷākavum taralām.  eḷiya ceyyuṭ pakutikaḷ, pērāciriyarkaḷiṉ urainaṭaip pakutikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiliruntu ittakaiya payiṟcikaḷait tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.  iṉṟu ceytit tāḷkaḷil veḷivarum palvēṟu vakai viḷamparaṅkaḷ ciṟanta moḻip payiṟcikaḷāka payaṉpaṭuttat takkavai; āciriyarē payiṟcikaḷai āyattam ceyvataṟkum ivai muṉmātiriyāka irukkat takkavai. 
10. moḻi peyarppu: oru moḻiyai vaḷappaṭutti uyarac ceyvataṟkuriya vaḻikaḷuḷ moḻi peyarttalum oṉṟākum.  ayal moḻiyiluḷḷa ciṟanta nūlkaḷait tam moḻiyil peyartteḻuti ilakkiya vaḷattaip perukka moḻi peyarppu tuṇaiceyyum.  paṇṭaikkālattiliruntu moḻi peyarppu kaiyāḷappaṭṭu vantateṉpataṟkut tolkāppiyam cāṉṟāka uḷḷatu.  vaḻi nūlkaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟavanta iṭattil vaḻi nūlkaḷ nāṉku vakaippaṭum eṉṟu kūṟi moḻi peyarttu ataṟpaṭayāttal8. eṉpataiyum vaḻi nūlkaḷil oṉṟākkukiṉṟatu.  puraṭcikkavi pāratiyārum.  
piṟanāṭṭu nallaṟiñar cāttiraṅkaḷ
tamiḻmoḻiyiṟ peyarttal vēṇṭum
eṉṟu moḻipeyarppiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyai uṇarttukiṉṟār.  iṉṟaiya nilaiyil araciyalaṟivu peṟṟu araciyalil kalantukoḷḷavum, ulakac ceytikaḷaiyum nāṭṭu naṭappaiyum aṟivataṟkum, caṭṭa nuṭpaṅkaḷaiyuṇarntu aṟaṅ kūṟu avaiyaṅkaḷilum, aluval nilaiyaṅkaḷilum, vaṇika nilaiyaṅkaḷilum vaḻakkāṭuvataṟkum, āṅkilam aṟiyātārum aṟivu peṟuvataṟkum moḻipeyarppu mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyāta cātaṉamākiṉṟatu.  ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷum tāymoḻi vāyilākak kaṟpikkap peṟuvatāl, moḻi peyarppāl pala ceytikaḷait tāy moḻikku koṇṭuvara vāyppu uṇṭākum.
iṉṟum āṅkilap paṭippil moḻipeyarppai oru kuṟaiyākak kaiyāṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉār.  atil kuṟaikaḷ uṇṭu eṉṟu evvaḷavu valiyuṟuttik kūṟappaṭiṉum, ammuṟai iṉṉum vaḻakkattilirukkattāṉ ceykiṉṟatu.  ayal moḻiyil viḷaṅkāta karuttup poruḷai coṟkaḷaiyum, coṟṟoṭarkaḷaiyum, moḻi peyarttalāl kūṭiyavarai eḷitil viḷakkiṭa muṭikiṉṟatu; kālam, muyaṟci mutaliyavai vīṇākāmal māṇākkarkaḷ poruḷai uṇarntu koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar eṉpataṟku aiyam ciṟitu illai.  moḻi peyarkkappaṭumpoḻutu ayal moḻic coṟkaḷ maṉattil naṉku patintu ammoḻiyilum aṟivu kaivarappeṟukiṉṟatu eṉṟu karutuvatil uṇmai illāmal illai.
moḻipeyarppil iruvakai uṇṭu.  oṉṟu, ayal moḻiyiliruntu tāymoḻiyil peyarppatu; maṟṟoṉṟu, tāymoḻiyiliruntu ayal moḻiyil peyarppatu.  muṉṉataṟkut tāymoḻiyaṟivu atikamum, piṉṉataṟku ayal moḻiyaṟivu atikamum iruttal vēṇṭum.  muṉṉatāl piṟamoḻic celvaṅkaḷ tamiḻ moḻikku varavum, piṉṉatāl tāy moḻiyiluḷḷa tirukkuṟaḷ, tiruvācakam, kamparāmāyaṇam pōṉṟa kalaik celvaṅkaḷ veḷināṭukaḷil paravum vāyppukkaḷ ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  iṉṟu āṅkila moḻi pōṉṟa veḷināṭṭu moḻikaḷiliruntu ettaṉaiyō aṟiviyaltuṟaic celvaṅkaḷum, vaṅkāḷam, kucarātti, marātti, inti mutaliya uḷnāṭṭu moḻikaḷiliruntu ettaṉaiyō putiṉam pōṉṟa putiya ilakkiya vakaikaḷum moḻipeyarkkappeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.  muṉṉataik kīḻ, mēl vakuppukkaḷilum, piṉṉatai mēl vakuppukkaḷilum kaṟpikkalām.  tamiḻiliruntu āṅkilattukkup peyarkkum payiṟcikaḷ naṭaimuṟaiyil tarappeṟuvatillai.  avaciyam avai tarappeṟutal vēṇṭum.
moḻi peyarppil nāṉku paṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  mutaṟpaṭiyil ayal moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum karuttai yaṟivataṟku maṭṭilum moḻi peyarkkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  iraṇṭāvatu paṭiyil ivvāṟu moḻi peyarkkum paḻakkam kuṟaintu pakutikaḷaip paṭikkum poḻutē cantarppaṅkaḷai yoṭṭic coṟkaḷiṉ allatu coṟṟoṭarkaḷiṉ poruḷ kaḷai aṟintu koḷḷa muṭikiṉṟatu.  mūṉṟāvatu paṭiyil iru moḻiyiṉ vākkiya amaippukkaḷaiyum, coṟṟoṭar maraput toṭar nuṭpaṅkaḷaiyum aṟintu koḷḷukiṉṟōm.  nāṉkāvatu paṭiyil iru moḻikaḷiṉ poḻutuppōkku, naṭaippōkku, uyirōṭṭam mutaliya paṇpukaḷai aṟiyum tiṟaṉ kaivarap peṟukiṉṟatu.  
moḻipeyarppil kavaṉikka vēṇṭiya cila kuṟippukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟai īṇṭu nōkkuvōm: collukkuc col moḻipeyarttal cariyāṉa moḻi peyarppākātu.  piṟamoḻic coṟṟoṭarkaḷ, maraput toṭarkaḷ, paḻamoḻikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ poruḷkaḷaic collukkuc col kūṟutal eṉpatu iyalātatoṉṟu. appaṭip peyarppiṉum appakuti uyiraṟṟa cavampōl tōṉṟum.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, āṅkila moḻiyiliruntu tamiḻ moḻikku oru pakutiyaip peyarttuk kūṟumpoḻutu annāṭṭiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkam, naṭai uṭai pāvaṉai, eṇṇam, uṇarcci, cūḻnilai, moḻit toṭaramaippu mutaliyavaṟṟai nammavaṟṟuṭaṉ oppiṭṭu moḻi peyarkka vēṇṭum.  āṉāl, collukkuc col cila coṟṟoṭar kaḷukkup poruḷ taralām.  eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka Turn the back-òw mutuku kāṭṭu, allatu venniṭu; Face is the index of the mind-mf¤â‹ aḻaku mukattil teriyum eṉpavaṟṟaik kūṟalām.
mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu ellāt toṭarkaḷaiyum ivvāṟu moḻi peyarttal oṇṇātu.  Take the seat, breathed his last eṉṟa coṟṟoṭarkaḷai aṅṅaṉam moḻi peyarttal ākātu.  ivaṟṟai muṟaiyē amar, āvi pirintatu eṉṟu moḻi peyarkka vēṇṭum. Arms in arms eṉpataip puyattoṭu puyam eṉpataiviṭa tōḷōṭu tōḷ eṉpatē ciṟantatu.  Nose-cut eṉpatai avamāṉa maṭaital eṉṟu moḻi peyarttalē cari.9 “Money makes many things” eṉṟa āṅkilac coṟṟoṭarai īṭṭi eṭṭiṉa maṭṭum pāyum, paṇam pātaḷam maṭṭum cellum eṉṟō, paṇam pattum ceyyum eṉṟō moḻi peyarkkalām.  “As clear as a crystal” eṉpatai uḷḷaṅkai nellikkaṉi pōla eṉṟu tamiḻ marapai oṭṭi moḻi peyarkka vēṇṭum. “All that glitters is not gold” eṉṟa coṟṟoṭarai miṉṉuvatellām poṉṉalla eṉṟu moḻipeyarppatiṉum veḷuttatellām pālalla eṉṟu tamiḻ marapup paṭi moḻi peyarppatē ēṟpuṭaittu.  ‘Face is the index of the mind’ eṉṟa āṅkilac coṟṟoṭarai akattiṉ aḻaku mukattil teriyum eṉṟu moḻi peyarttal cariyāṉa moḻi peyarppu ākum.  eṉavē, moḻipeyarppil marapu muṟai piṟaḻakkūṭātu eṉpatai uṇaravēṇṭum.  moḻi peyarppaip paṭittāl aḵtu iyalpākavum toṭarpākavum kōvaiyākavum irukkavēṇṭum.  moḻi peyarkkappaṭum pakuti putitākat tamiḻil oruvarāl eḻutappaṭṭatupōl amaiyavēṇṭum; paṭippavarukku mūlappakutiyiṉ niṉaivē varakkūṭātu. ji. yū. pōp, va.ve.cu. aiyar, kavimaṇi, cakkaravartti irācakōpālāccāriyār, vipulānanta aṭikaḷ pōṉṟa moḻipeyarppukkaḷ aṟiñarkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarpput tuṟaiyil pukukiṉṟavarkaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭiyāka amaiyum irumoḻikaḷiṉ nuṭpaṅkaḷai naṉkaṟintālaṉṟi moḻi peyarppuc ciṟappāka amaiyātu.
vakuppil moḻi peyarppai naṭattumpoḻutu āciriyar cila paṭikaḷai niṉaivilirutta vēṇṭum.  moḻi peyarkka vēṇṭiya āṅkilap pakutiyai āciriyar oru muṟaikku mummuṟai muṟṟilum karuttuṇarum varaiyil teḷivāka niṟuttip paṭikka vēṇṭum.  iṭaiyiṭaiyē aruñcoṟkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷaik kūṟikkoṇṭē cellalām.  iṟutip paṭiyiltāṉ itai collavēṇṭum.  aṭuttu oru cila māṇākkarkaḷait teḷivāka, niṟuttip paṭikkum paṭic ceyyavēṇṭum.  pakutiyiṉ uṭkiṭakkaiyai māṇākkar aṟintuḷḷaṉarā eṉpatai oru cila viṉākkaḷāl terintu avarkaḷ aṟintiruntāl moḻi peyarppai vāymoḻiyākat toṭaṅkālam.  ovvoruvaraiyum ovvoru vākkiyamāka moḻi peyarkkumpaṭi ceytu avaṟṟiluḷḷa tavaṟukaḷaip piṟa māṇākkarkaḷaik koṇṭu tiruttalām.  pakuti muḻuvatum vāymoḻiyāka nalla muṟaiyil moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭa piṟaku, eḻutum payiṟciyait taralām.  toṭakkattil ivvāṟu virivāṉa muṟaiyaik kaiyāḷa vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi paṭippaṭiyāka ivaṟṟaik kuṟaittu māṇākkarkaḷ tāmāka eḻutum paḻakkattai uṇṭu paṇṇa vēṇṭum.
māṇākkar eḻutum moḻi peyarppu eḷiya iṉiya tūya tamiḻ naṭaiyil amaiyavēṇṭum.  eḻuttup piḻaikaḷ, coṟṟoṭarp piḻaikaḷ, karuttu muṭipup piḻaikaḷ mutaliyavai nēriṭāmal pārttukkoḷḷavēṇṭum.  eḻuti muṭintapiṉ māṇākkar eḻutiyavaṟṟait tirumpap paṭikkum paḻakkattai mutaliliruntē vaṟpuṟutta vēṇṭum.  ippaḻakkam iyalpāka amaiyappeṟiṉ piṟaku pala tavaṟukaḷai avarkaḷē tirutti viṭalām.  piṟaku āciriyar avaṟṟait tiruttavēṇṭum.  kaṭṭuraip payiṟcikaḷ tiruttumpoḻutu kaiyāḷavēṇṭiya kuṟippukkaḷaiyē īṇṭum kaiyāḷalām9.
māṇākkar eḻuttu vēlaiyil kāṇappeṟum kuṟaikaḷum piḻaikaḷum: cātāraṇamāka māṇākkarkaḷ eḻutum kaṭṭuraikaḷil ippiḻaikaḷaik kāṇalām: evvaḷavu coṉṉālum cilar varantaiyil (Margin) payiṟci eṇ, payiṟci koṭukkappeṟṟa tēti, kaṭṭuraiyiṉ talaippu ākiyavaṟṟaik kuṟippatillai.  cilaruṭaiya kaṭṭuraikaḷil ra-ṟa; la-ḷa-ḻa; pōṉṟa eḻuttup piḻaikaḷ malivāka irukkum; cantippiḻaikaḷ ērāḷamākak kāṇappeṟum.  oru cila kaṭṭuraikaḷil orumai-paṉmai mayakkam.  ceyviṉai-ceyappāṭṭu viṉaikaḷil tavaṟukaḷ pōṉṟa ilakkaṇap piḻaikaḷaik kāṇalām.  cilar coṟkaḷaiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷaiyum poruḷaṟintu kaiyāḷuvatillai; cilar vākkiya amaippaic cariyākak kavaṉittu eḻutuvatillai. kaṭṭuraikaḷil tiṭṭamāṉa toṭakkam, cemmaiyāṉa naṭai, cariyāṉa muṭivu ākiyavai iruppatillai.  kaṭṭuraip poruḷamaippilum kāraṇakāriyat toṭar piṉmai, talaippukkup poruntāta poruḷkaḷaip pukuttutal, tavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷaip pukuttutal, ceytik kuṟaivu pōṉṟa poruṭ piḻaikaḷum kāṇappaṭum. cila kaṭṭuraikaḷil uṇarcciyē irātu.  cila māṇākkarkaḷ pattikaḷaip pirippatillai. cilar pattikaḷiṉ amaippaik kavaṉiyātu piritteḻutuvar.  cilar niṟattaṟ kuṟikaḷaic cariyākavē iṭuvatillai.  cilar tām eḻutum kaiyeḻuttaik kavaṉittē eḻutuvatillai.
māṇākkar ceyyum piḻaikaḷai aṟiyāmaiyāl ēṟpaṭuvaṉa, kavaṉakkuṟaiviṉāl nēriṭuvaṉa eṉa iraṇṭu pirivukaḷākap pirikkalām.  aṟiyāmaiyiṉāl nēriṭum piḻaikaḷai māṇākkarkaḷukkut taṉit taṉiyākavō, kuḻu muṟaiyilō eṭuttuk kāṭṭittāṉ āciriyar tiruttavēṇṭum.  kavaṉak kuṟaiviṉāl ēṟpaṭuvaṉavaṟṟaik kavaṉakkuṟaivu illātavāṟu ceytu nīkkalām.  eḻuttukkaḷaik kavaṉittu niṟutti eḻutum paṭi ceytal, eḻutiya kaṭṭuraiyait tirumpap paṭikkac ceytal, niṟuttaṟ kuṟikaḷai ēṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil iṭac ceytal pōṉṟa vaṟṟaik kavaṉikkac ceytāl piḻaikaḷaik kuṟaikkalām.  aiyan tarat takka coṟpaṭṭikaḷaik kaiyāḷutal, kīḻvakuppukkaḷil colluvateḻutum payiṟcikaḷ tarutal, akarātip paḻakkam ēṟpaṭuttal pōṉṟa vaḻikaḷāl eḻuttuppiḻaikaḷaiyum cantippiḻaikaḷaiyum nīkkalām.  takka moḻippayiṟcikaḷiṉāl ēṉaiya piḻaikaḷaiyum kaḷaiyalām. avai nēriṭāmalum taṭukkalām. kuṟippuc caṭṭakam eḻutiya piṟaku kaṭṭuraikaḷai eḻututal, tēvaiyāṉa aḷavu vāymoḻik kaṭṭuraip payiṟci ākiya vaḻikaḷamaittāl poruṭ ṭoṭarpup piḻaikaḷ nēriṭā.  kaṭṭuraikaḷait tiruttiya piṟaku ivaṟṟai ēṟṟa muṟaiyil eṭuttuk kāṭṭi viḷakkiṉāl piḻaikaḷ nāḷaṭaivil kuṟaintu kaṭṭuraikaḷ naṉmuṟaiyil amaiyum.
eḻuttup payiṟcikaḷait tiruttutal
māṇākkarkaḷ eḻutiya kaṭṭuraikaḷait tiruttum vēlai āciriyat toḻilil mikak kaṭiṉamāṉatoru pakuti.  ippakuti arumaiyāṉatum iṉṟiyamaiyātatumākum.  eḻuttup payiṟci māṇākkarkaṭku evvaḷavu iṉṟiyamaiyātatō, atu pōlavē āciriyarkaḷ kaṭṭuraikaḷait tiruttik kuṟaikaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatum mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.  tām ceyta tavaṟu kaḷait tiruttikkoḷḷa vāyppu illāviṭil,  eḻuttup payiṟciyiṉāl māṇākkarkaḷukku yātu payaṉ? tavaṟukaḷai avvappoḻutu āṅkāṅku eṭuttukkāṭṭavēṇṭum.  oru muṟai ceyta tavaṟukaḷai mīṇṭum ceyyāmal irukka muyaṟci ceyyavēṇṭum.  uḷaviyaṟpaṭi, tavaṟukaḷai mīṇṭum mīṇṭum ceytāl atu nīkkamuṭiyāta paḻakkamākavē amaintu viṭakkūṭum.  kaṭṭurai tiruttuvatu eṉpatu oru taṉikkalai; ciṟappāṉa kalai.  kaṭṭurai tiruttuvatu tērvu viṭaittāḷkaḷai matippiṭṭu matippeṇkaḷ taruvatu pōṉṟatalla; atu tērcci peruka utavum oru cātaṉamumākum.  kaṭṭuraiyait tiruttumpoḻutu atai eḻutiya māṇākkaṉai arukil vaittukkoṇṭu tiruttiṉāl niṟainta payaṉ viḷaiyum.  ciṟcila camayamtāṉ itu  cāttiyap paṭakkūṭum; iṉṟaiya cūḻnilaiyil itu perumpālum iyalātatoṉṟu eṉṟē collavēṇṭum.  eṉavē, ētāvatu oru cariyāṉa muṟaiyai mēṟkoṇṭu itai payaṉuḷḷatākac ceyvatup mikamika iṉṟiyamaiyāta tākum.
kaṭṭurai tiruttum vēlai aluppum aruvaruppum tarakkūṭiyatāka iruntālum atai puṟakkaṇikkātu poṟuppāṉa vēlaiyākavē mēṟkoḷḷavēṇṭum.  perumpālāṉa paḷḷikaḷil ivvēlai puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭē varukiṉṟatu.  itaṟkup pala kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷa.  cila talaimaiyāciriyarkaḷ ivvēlaiyai ēḷaṉamākak karuti ataṉait tamakkup piṭikkāta, allatu mikka paṭṭaṟivu illāta, āciriyarkaḷ talaiyil aḷavukkumēl oru cumaiyākak kaṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.  allatu, tamiḻāciriyarkaḷē tamiḻk kaṭṭuraikaḷaiyellām tirutta vēṇṭum eṉṟa koḷkaiyai mēṟkoṇṭu avarkaḷaiyē tiruttumpaṭi ēvukiṉṟaṉar.  vārattiṟku 200 allatu 300 payiṟci ēṭukaḷait tiruttumpaṭi oruvarai ēvutal kaṭumaiyāṉa taṇṭaṉaiyākum.  immuṟaikaḷ viraivil māṟavēṇṭum.  ekkāraṇattālum kaṭṭurai tiruttum vēlaiyai oruvar mītu aḷavukkumēl cumattakkūṭātu.  tamiḻk kaṟpikkātu kaṭṭuraiyai ōr āciriyaraik kaṟpikka ēvutalālum payaṉ irātu.
cila paḷḷikaḷil āṇṭukku oru vakuppukku 12 mutal 20 kaṭṭuraikaḷ payiṟcikaḷākat taruvar.  tiṅkaḷ oṉṟukku iraṇṭu eḻuttuk kaṭṭuraikaḷ koṭukkalām eṉṟu tiṭṭamiṭṭu ataic cevvaṉē tiruttiṉāl nalamāka irukkum.  āṇṭiṟkup paṉṉireṇṭu eḻuttuk kaṭṭuraikaḷ ceytāl pōtum.  ēṉaiyavai vāymoḻik kaṭṭuraikaḷāka amaiyalām.  pala payiṟcikaḷaik koṭuttu avaṟṟait tiruttāmaliruppataiviṭa cila payiṟcikaḷai eḻutacceytu avaṟṟai naṉku tiruttip piḻaikaḷai māṇākkarkaṭku uṇarttutal mēlāṉatu.  ceyvaṉa tiruntac cey eṉpatai āciriyarkaḷ ceyilil mēṟ koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  eṉavē, kaṭṭuraiyait tiruttuvatu paṟṟi oru yōcaṉaikaḷaik kūṟutal ivviṭattil poruntum; avai iḷam āciriyarkaṭku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavumākum.
tiruttuvatupaṟṟi cila kuṟippukkaḷ: kaṭṭurai eḻutum muṉṉar cariyāṉa muṟaiyil vāymoḻik kaṭṭuraip payiṟci tarappeṟiṉ piḻaikaḷ kuṟaiyum; tiruttavēṉṭiya vēlaiyum atikamirātu.  kūṭiyavaraiyil māṇākkariṉ muṉṉilaiyil piḻaikaḷait tiruttuvatu nallatu.  ellāp piḻaikaḷaiyum iṅṅaṉam eṭuttukkāṭṭit tiruttuvateṉpatu naṭaimuṟaiyil iyalātatoṉṟu.  mikap perum piḻaikaḷaiyāyiṉum ivvāṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉāl atikap payaṉviḷaiyum.  ellāp payiṟcikaḷaiyum māṇakkarkaḷai vaittuk koṇṭu tiruttuvatum iyalātatu.  oru vārattil oru payiṟciyai aintāṟu māṇākkar kaḷaip poṟukki vaittuk koṇṭu ivvāṟu tiruttiṉāl, eṭṭu vārattiṟkuḷ oru vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷ ellōraiyum orumuṟai uṭaṉ vaittukkoṇṭu tirutta muṭiyum.  oru māṇākkaṉiṉ piḻaikaḷai avaṉaikkoṇṭē vakuppil tiruttumpaṭi ceyvatu payaṉuṭaiya muṟaiyāka irukkum.  āṉāl, palar muṉṉilaiyil ivvāṟu cuṭṭik kāṭṭuvatai muṉkumarap paruva māṇākkarkaḷil cilar virumpātiruttal kūṭum; avvāṟu ceyvatāl avarkaḷ maṉampuḻuṅki puṇpaṭṭu varuntavum kūṭum.  avvāṟu maṉam pātikkappaṭāmal immuṟai naṭaipeṟṟal mikac ciṟanta payaṉai aḷikkum.  eṉiṉum, oruvar tam kuṟṟattait piṟar muṉṉilaiyilō taṉittō uṇarntu koḷvatu vāḻkkaiyil aṉaivarum mēṟkoḷḷattakka  toṉṟātalāl, āciriyar ārāyntu immuṟaiyaik kaiyāḷalām.  tiruttum poḻutu piḻaikaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭutal nalla muṟaiyil amaiyavēṇṭum; kaṟkum māṇākkarkaḷum virumpi ēṟṟut tirunta muṉ varum muṟaiyil aḵtu amaintāl cālap payaṉ tarum.
toṭakkanilaip paḷḷi vakuppukkaḷil viṉākkaḷukkuc  ciṟu ciṟu viṭaikaḷē eḻutavēṇṭi yiruppatāl eḻuti muṭintatum avaṟṟait tirutti viṭalām.  tiruttik koṇṭē vakuppaic cuṟṟiyum varalām.  naṭunilai, uyarnilaip paḷḷi vakuppukkaḷil avaṟṟai ōyvu nēraṅkaḷilum viṭumuṟai nāṭkaḷilumē tirutta vēṇṭum.  cilar vakuppoḻinta vēḷaikaḷilum viṭumuṟai nāṭkaḷilum tiruttāmal iruntuviṭṭu avaṟṟai vakuppukkuk koṇṭuvantu ovvoruvaraiyum tam pakkattil vaittuk koṇṭu tiruttum muṟai virumpat takkataṉṟu.  ivvāṟu kālattai vīṇākkutal kūṭātu.  immuṟaiyāl māṇakkarkaḷ taṉikkavaṉam peṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu niṉaippatum tavaṟu.  ivvāṟu tiruttum poḻutu  ellāvaṟṟaiyum tirutti eḻuta vēṇṭiya avaciyamum illai. cila kuṟiyīṭukaḷaik kaiyāṇṭāl āciriyariṉ vēlai kuṟaivatuṭaṉ avai māṇākkariṉ cintaṉaiyait tūṇṭuvaṉavākavum irukkum.  kīḻkkāṇum kuṟiyīṭukaḷai mēṟkoḷḷalām.
kuṟi			kuṟippatu
e. pi.		eḻuttup piḻai.
		coṟkaḷ viṭupaṭṭiruppataik 				kāṭṭuvatu.
ta. na. 		tamiḻ naṭai naṉṟāka illai. 
i. pi. 		ilakkaṇap piḻai
 !		uyarvu naviṟci allatu puṉainturai.
?		karuttiṉ uṇmaiyil aiyam.
ni.		niṟuttaṟkuṟiyiṭṭatil tavaṟu. 
vi.		viṭṭuviṭuka.
vē.		vēṇṭātavai.
+		iṭam viṭuka.
x		pattiyākat toṭaṅkuka.
V		piḻaiyāṉa niṟuttaṟ kuṟi.
x		toṭaril muraṇkāṇum iṭam.
†		kūṟiyatu kūṟiyirukkum iṭam.
ivaṟṟai māṇākkarkaṭku naṉku viḷakkiviṭṭāl, ik kuṟikaḷai aṟintu, kāṭṭiya  tiruttaṅkaḷait tiruttamuṭiyum.  māṇākkariṉ kaṭṭuraikaḷil kāṇappeṟum eḻuttup piḻaikaḷ, ilakkaṇap piḻaikaḷ, cantip piḻaikaḷ, vākkiyap piḻaikaḷ  mutaliyavaṟṟai maṭṭilum tiruttaviṭṭāl pōtātu.  avaṟṟai eṭuttukkāṭṭa takka moḻip payiṟcikaḷiṉāl nīkka muyala vēṇṭum.  poruḷ varicaip poruttattil kuṟaikaḷ, uṇarcciyil veḷiyiṭum moḻik kuṟaikaḷ, poruḷukkum collukkum poruttam viḷaṅkāta kuṟaikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil āciriyar nallakavaṉam celuttavēṇṭum.  perumpālāṉa āciriyarkaḷ ivaṟṟaic cariyākak kavaṉippatē illai. ivaṟṟaip potuvāka vakuppukkaḷil eṭuttuk kāṭṭit tiruttiṉāl naṟpayaṉ viḷaiyum.  poruḷai aḻuttamākavum kōvaiyākavum uṇarcciyōṭum veḷiyiṭum kaṭṭuraikaḷ ciṟu piḻaikaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭiṉum, avai piḻaiyaṟṟu amaippuk kulaintu poruḷ melintu tōṉṟum kaṭṭuraikaḷai viṭa mēlāṉavai.  eṉavē, tiruttum āciriyarkaḷ uṇarcciyaik kavaṉikkavēṇṭum.
oru vārattil koṭutta eḻuttuk kaṭṭuraiyai aṭutta vārattiṟkuḷ tiruttiviṭṭāl nalam. anta vārattil kaṭṭuraip pāṭa vēḷaiyil atai māṇākkarkaḷiṭam koṭuttuviṭa vēṇṭum.  tiruttiya āciriyar māṇākkarkaḷ ceyta piḻaikaḷai vakaippaṭuttit tokuttu vaittuk koṇṭu vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷ aṉaivarukkum kāṭṭalām; ap piḻaikaḷiṉ tiruttaṅkaḷai uḷattil patiyumāṟum viḷakkalām;.  oru cilar naṉṟāka eḻutiya kaṭṭuraikaḷai aṉaivarukkum paṭittuk kāṭṭalām;  atē poruḷpaṟṟip puttakattiluḷḷa kaṭṭuraiyaip paṭittum kāṭṭalām; atē pāṭa vēḷaiyil ellōraiyum kuṟippēṭṭiṉ (Note book) iṭappuṟattil tiruttaṅkaḷai eḻutumāṟu ēvi, avaṟṟai muṭikkumāṟu ceyya vēṇṭum.  perumpālum māṇākkarkaḷ āciriyar civappu maiyiṉāl tiruttiyavaṟṟaip pārppatē illai. immuṟaiyaik kaiyāṇṭāl nalla payaṉ viḷaiyum. tammuṭaiya kaṭṭurai vakuppil paṭikkappaṭum, ārāyappaṭum eṉpatu paṟṟi ovvoruvarum aṟintiruntāl, māṇakkarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kaṭṭuraikaḷai naṉṟāka eḻuta muṉvarutal kūṭum.
cila āciriyarkaḷ kaṭṭuraikaḷai matippiṭuvataṟku oruvakai matippeṇ pākupāṭṭaik kaiyāḷukiṉṟaṉar.  poruḷukku ittaṉai,  naṭaikku ittaṉai, kaiyeḻuttukku ittaṉai eṉṟu pattu matippeṇṇaip pākupāṭu ceytukoṇṭu tiruttum muṟaiyai aṉaivarumē kaiyāḷalām.  itu māṇākkarkaḷ tam kuṟaikaḷai uṇarntu tam tiṟamaiyai vaḷarttuk koḷḷat tūṇṭukōlākavum irukkum.
paḷḷikaḷil eḻuttuvēlaipaṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ
toṭakkanilaip paḷḷikaḷil: toṭakkanilaip paḷḷikaḷil eḻuttu vēlaikkeṉat taṉiyāṉa tiṭṭam oṉṟum illai.  avarkaḷ paṭitta puttakaṅkaḷiliruntu cila poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟiyum, pāṭapputtakaṅkaḷil kāṇāta-āṉāl, māṇākkar kaḷaṟinta - poruḷkaḷaippaṟṟiyum oru patti allatu iraṇṭu pattikaḷ varaiyum payiṟcikaḷait taralām.
mutal vakuppil kuḻantaikaḷ tāṅkaḷ paṭitta coṟkaḷaiyum, ciṟu vākkiyaṅkaḷaiyum karumpalakai, paṭam, miṉṉaṭṭai, piṟa cātaṉaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil eḻutiyiruppaṉavaṟṟaippārttu ataip pōl tāmum eḻutum muṟaikaḷaiyum paḻakik koṇṭāl pōtumāṉatu.  iraṇṭām vakuppil eḻutu kōlaic cariyāṉa muṟaiyil piṭikkavum, teḷivāyum, viraivāyum eḻutavum kaṟpatai vaṟpuṟutta vēṇṭum.  ivvakuppil paṭitta pāṭaṅkaḷil kēṭkum viṉākkaḷukku ovvoru vākkiyattil viṭai eḻutum tiṟamai vaḷarntāl pōtumāṉatu.  mūṉṟām vakuppil moḻippāṭapputtakaṅkaḷiliruntum piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷiliruntum kēṭkappeṟum viṉākkaḷukku iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu vākkiyaṅkaḷil viṭaiyeḻutum payiṟcikaḷait taravēṇṭum.  nāṉkām vakuppil kaṭṭurai eḻutum payiṟcikku mutaṟpaṭiyāka camūka vāḻkkaipaṟṟiya kaṭitaṅkaḷum, contak kaṭitaṅkaḷum eḻutum payiṟcikaḷ taravēṇṭum.  iv vakuppil āṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil conta aṉupavaṅkaḷaippaṟṟic curukkamāka varaiyum payiṟcikaḷait taralām.  aintām vakuppil camūkac= cārpākavum , vāṇikac cārpākavum caṟṟu nīṇṭa kaṭitaṅkaḷai eḻutum payiṟcikaḷait taravēṇṭum.  āṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil muṟaiyāṉa kaṭṭurai eḻutum payiṟcikaḷait taralām.  māṇākkar kaḷaṟinta poruḷkaḷpaṟṟiya ceytikaḷai oḻuṅkāka vakuttuk koṇṭu ciṟiya aḷavil kaṭṭuraikaḷai eḻutat tūṇṭa vēṇṭum.  iv vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷukku ciṅkam, yāṉai pōṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷ paṟṟiyum; arici, māṅkāy, palāppaḻam, uṭai pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ paṟṟiyum; vīṭu, paḷḷi, tōṭṭam, viḷaiyāṭṭiṭam ākiya aṟinta iṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyum; avarkaḷaṟitta cila ūrkaḷ paṟṟiyum; kākitam ceytal, maṭpāṇṭam varaital, kūṭai muṭaital, uppu uṇṭākkal pōṉṟa ciṟu toḻilkaḷ paṟṟiyum; ūrppūmpoḻil, āṟṟaṅkarai, cantai, pukai vaṇṭi nilaiyam, malaic carivu pōṉṟa kāṭcikaḷpaṟṟiyum; maḻaināḷ aṉupavam, kōyil, tiruviḻā, kaṭaitteruvukkup pōy varutal pōṉṟa nukarvukaḷaip paṟṟiyum kaṭṭuraikaḷ varaiyat tūṇṭalām.  eḷiya paṭaṅkaḷaip pārttu kaṭṭurai varaiyum payiṟcikaḷum ivarkaḷukku ēṟṟavai.  ōnāyum kokkum, kākkaiyum nīrk kuṭamum, ōnāyum āṭṭukkuṭṭiyum eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa ciṟiya kataikaḷai eḻutum payiṟcikaḷaiyum taralām.
naṭunilaip paḷḷikaḷil: naṭunilaip paḷḷi vakuppukkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ āṟṟa vēṇṭiya eḻuttu vēlaikaḷai mika mukkiyamāṉa vaiyākak koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  kaṭṭuraikaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷ māṇakkarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyai yoṭṭiyaṉavāka irukkavēṇṭum.  māṇakkarkaḷ tamakkuḷḷē veḷiyiṭat takkaṉavākak kaṭṭuraip poruḷkaḷ amaintāl avaṟṟai avarkaḷ viruppattuṭaṉum ārvattuṭaṉum eḻutuvar.  car.  ḵpilip eṉpāriṉ karuttuppaṭi ovvōr eḻuttu vēlaiyum atai eḻutukiṉṟavar piṟarukkō allatu taṉ piṟkāla niṉaivukkut tuṇaiyākavō poruḷait teḷivāka uṇarttum nōkkattuṭaṉ eḻutavēṇṭum.  avar ovvoṉṟaiyum niṉaivup pativu (Record) ceyti allatu aṟikkai (Message) eṉṟa iruvakaikkum tokukkakkūṭum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟār.10 muṉ iyalil collappeṟṟa moḻip payiṟcikaḷai ilakkaṇap pāṭattuṭaṉ iṇaittuk kaṟpittāl ataṉāl māṇākkarkaḷ peṟum moḻiyaṟivu avarkaḷiṉ palvēṟu eḻuttu vēlai kaḷukkup peruntuṇaiyāka irukkum.  kaṭṭuraiyiṉ naṭai eḷimai, teḷivu, marapu piṟaḻāmai,  ēṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil aḻuttam ākiya paṇpukaḷaik koṇṭi ruppataiyē nōkkamākak koḷḷavēṇṭum.
vakuppukkaḷil ellāt talaippukkaḷaiyum eḻutuvataip paṟṟik kaṟpikkumpoḻutu pattiyamaippukkaḷ, pattiyiṉ nalliyalpukaḷ, niṟuttaṟ kuṟikaḷiṭutal, kōṭiṭal (Underlining) talaippamaittal, uḷ talaippukkaḷamaittal pōṉṟa palvēṟu vaḻivakaikaḷaiyum avvappoḻutu āṅkāṅkuk kaṟpikkalām. 
uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷil: tāymoḻi allatu maṇṭala moḻi mutalmoḻi nilaiyai aṭainta piṟaku māṇākkarkaḷiṉ eḻuttu vēlaikaḷ tarattil uyarnta nilaiyil irukka vēṇṭum.  poruṭpōkkil virintum, naṭaippōkkil uyarntum irukka vēṇṭum.  mēl vakuppukkaḷil kūṭiya varai ivai māṇākkar kaḷiṉ paṭaippāṟṟalkaḷai veḷippaṭuttum pāṉmaiyilum, māṇākkarkaḷiṉ taṉivīṟu veḷippaṭum muṟaiyilum amaintāl naṉṟu.  curukki varaitaluṭaṉ kaṭṭuraip poruḷkaḷaic cērttu oḻuṅkupaṭutti avaṟṟaik kāraṇa kāriya muṟaippaṭiyum, tarukka muṟaippaṭiyum eḻutum vāyppukkaḷai aṭikkaṭi nalkalām.  in nilai māṇākkarkaḷiṭam ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvuk kaṭṭurai kaḷaiyum etirpārkkalām.  
in nilai māṇākkarkaḷiṉ naṭai iṉiya, eḷiya, marapu neṟi piṟaḻāta taṉmaiyuṭaṉ irukka vēṇṭum.  pattiyamaippu, niṟuttaṟ kuṟikaḷiṭutal, talaippukkaḷ koṭuttal pōṉṟa tuṟaikaḷil uyartarap payiṟcikaḷai etir pārkkalām.  māṇākkarkaḷ eḻutiya kaṭṭuraikaḷil nallaṉavaṟṟait tērnteṭuttup paḷḷi āṇṭitaḻil veḷiyiṭalām.
moḻi peyarppukkut tarum āṅkilap pakutikaḷiṉ naṭai eḷimaiyākavum nēriyatākavum irukka vēṇṭum.  kaṭṭurai kaḷukkākat tērnteṭukkum poruḷkaḷaippaṟṟiya pakutikaḷē itaṟkum ēṟṟavai.  ip pakutikaḷai māṇākkar payilum āṅkilat tuṇaippāṭa nūlkaḷiliruntum poṟukki eṭukkalām.
uyarnilaip paḷḷi vakuppukkaḷil avarkaḷuṭaiya tuṇaippāṭap puttakaṅ kaḷiliruntu cila talaippukkaḷait tērnteṭuttu oṉṟaraip pakkaṅkaḷukku mikāmal kaṭṭuraikaḷ varaiyum payiṟcikaḷ tara vēṇṭum.
paṭaippāṟṟalukku vaḻikōlutal
tāy moḻiyil tāṉ paṭaippāṟṟalai eḷital vaḷarkkalām.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ tēvaikaḷ, maṉappāṉmai, kavarcci ākiyavaṟṟiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu moḻippāṭaṅkaḷaik kaṟpikkavēṇṭumāṉāl, cātāraṇamākak koṭukkap peṟum kaṭṭuraip payiṟcikaḷ maṭṭilum pōtā.  nāḷitaḻkaḷilum, paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷilum veḷivarum putiya ilakkiya vakaikaḷaiyum kavitaikaḷaiyum paṭikkum māṇākkarkaḷukku avaikaḷaippōl tāmum paṭaikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvam eḻutal iyalpu.  tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ ciṟitu muyaṟci eṭuttu it tuṟaiyil takka kavaṉam celuttiṉāl uyarnilaippaḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷiṭam ciṟu kaṭṭuraikaḷ, ciṟu nāṭakaṅkaḷ, ciṟu kataikaḷ. ciṟu ciṟu kavitaikaḷ mutaliya ilakkiya vakaikaḷait tāmākavē eḻutum āṟṟalai vaḷarttu viṭa muṭiyum.  paṭaippāṟṟalkaḷai vaḷarkkum muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭṭāl iḷam uḷḷaṅkaḷ paṇpaṭṭu nalla palaṉait tarakkūṭum.  am māṇākkarkaḷil palar etirkālattil ciṟanta ciṟukataic celvarkaḷākavum, putiṉa āciriyarkaḷākavum, kaviñarkaḷākavum tikaḻakkūṭum.  āyirattil allatu ilaṭcattil oruvarāvatu piṟkālattil kampaṉ, pārati pōṉṟa peruṅ kaviñaṉāka viḷaṅka iṭamuṇṭu.  eṉavē, ataṟkēṟṟa aṭip paṭaiyaip paḷḷiyil pōṭuvatum, ataṟku vēṇṭiya vāyppukkaḷaik kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷil tarutalum avaciyam.  paḷḷi itaḻ ittuṟaiyil perun toṇṭum puriyum.  paḷḷi itaḻkaḷil cila māṇākkarkaḷ arumaiyāṉa kataikaḷ, kavitaikaḷ uraiyāṭalkaḷ, kaṟpaṉaik kaṭṭuraikaḷ eḻutiyatai in nūlāciriyar naṉku aṟivār11. eṉavē, paḷḷi itaḻaip paṟṟi oru ciṟitu īṇṭu kūṟuvatu iṉṟimaiyātatākiṉṟatu.
paḷḷi itaḻkaḷ: pala nūlkaḷiluḷḷa kataikaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṭikkumpoḻutum, pala vaṇṇap paṭaṅkaḷaiyum kaṇkavar ōviyaṅkaḷaiyum kaṇṇuṟum poḻutum avaṟṟaip pōl tāmum eḻutavēṇṭum, varaiya vēṇṭum eṉṟa āval māṇākkarkaḷiṭam eḻutal iyalpu.  vāyppukkaḷ takkavāṟu koṭukkappeṟṟāl māṇākkarkaḷiṭamuḷḷa paṭaippāṟṟal naṉku vaḷarcci peṟum.  ittakaiya vāyppukkaḷai nalkuvatu paḷḷi itaḻ.  tāymoḻi uṇarcci mikkeḻuntirukkum ik kālattil ovvoru paḷḷiyilum paḷḷi itaḻkaḷ tōṉṟip paṇiyāṟṟa iṭam koṭuttal iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.
paḷḷi itaḻkaḷāl iruvita naṉmaikaḷ uṇṭu.  paḷḷiyil naṭaipeṟum palvēṟu nikaḻccikaḷaiyum ceyal muṟaikaḷaiyum peṟṟōrkaḷukkum potu makkaḷukkum aṟivittu avarkaḷuṭaiya ottuḻaippaip peṟuvatu oruvakai.  maṟṟoruvakai, māṇākkarkaḷiṭaiyē maṟaintu kiṭakkum paṭaippāṟṟal kaḷaip palvēṟu muṟaikaḷil vaḷarkkat tuṇai purivatu.  iṉṟu naṭaimuṟaiyil pala paḷḷikaḷil ivvita itaḻkaḷ tōṟṟuvikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  acciṭṭa itaḻkaḷai veḷiyiṭuvataṟku ataṟkeṉa ākum.  celavu tokai atikamāvatāl, cila paḷḷikaḷil āṇṭiṟku oṉṟum, mikac cila periya paḷḷikaḷil āṇṭiṟku iraṇṭumāka ivai veḷiyiṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  paḷḷi nikaḻccikaḷ, āṇṭu aṟikkaikaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟāl itaḻkaḷai nirappuvataiviṭa avaṟṟait taṉiyāka veḷiyiṭṭu viṭṭu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ paṭaippāṟṟalukku mutaliṭam taruvatē ciṟantatu.  talaimaiyāciriyarum piṟa āciriyarkaḷum ivvaḻiyil cintaiyaic celutti āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭum.
naṭaimuṟaiyil paḷḷikkeṉa ōr itaḻum ovvoru vakuppukkum ovvoru vakuppiṉ pirivukaḷukkum ovvōr itaḻum irukka ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceytāl ciṟanta palaṉkaḷaik kāṇa iyalum.  vakuppu itaḻkaḷaik kaiyeḻuttup paṭiyākavē veḷiyiṭalām; tiṅkaḷ oṉṟukku ōr eṇ vītamum veḷiyiṭalām.  ivvāṟu  ovvoru vakuppilum veḷivarum itaḻkaḷiluḷḷa kataikaḷ, kavitaikaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷ, ciṟu nāṭakaṅkaḷ, piṟa ceytikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟuḷ nallaṉavaṟṟait tērnteṭuttup paḷḷi itaḻkaḷil veḷiyiṭalām.  paḷḷi itaḻ accitaḻāka irukka vēṇṭum; āṇṭiṟku oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu eṇkaḷai vacatik kēṟṟavāṟu veḷiyiṭalām.  tammuṭaiya eḻuttōviyaṅkaḷ accil veḷivaruvataik kāṇum māṇākkarkaḷukkum ēṟpaṭum iṉpattiṟku iṇaiyoṉṟillai.  anta iṉpamē avarkaḷuṭaiya paṭaippāṟṟalkaḷait tūṇṭi paṉmaṭaṅku vaḷarac ceyyum.  tammuṭaiya karuttōviyaṅkaḷ eppaṭiyum accil veḷivaravēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇarcciyāl māṇākkarkaḷ vakuppukkaḷil tiṅkaḷtōṟum veḷivarum itaḻkaḷukku atikamāṉa kaṭṭuraikaḷai eḻutuvar.  kaiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷāl tāṉ ōviyat tiṟaṉ, itaḻkaḷaip palvēṟu muṟaiyil kōlamiṭṭuc ciṟappikkum tiṟaṉ ākiyavaṟṟai naṉku vaḷarkka muṭiyum.  accitaḻkaḷāl inta naṉmaikaḷai aṭaiya iyalātu.
kuṟippiṭṭa kālaṅkaḷil ivvitaḻkaḷ veḷi varuvataṟkuc cuṟucuṟuppākac ceyalāṟṟum māṇākkarkaḷaṭaṅkiya kuḻukkaḷ paṇiyāṟṟa vēṇṭum ovvoru vakuppāciriyarkaḷum tiṟamaiyuḷḷa māṇākkarkaḷait tērnteṭuttu ceyaṟ kuḻu amaittu ip paṇiyiṉai mēṟkoḷḷalām.  ik kuḻukkaḷil, karuttil vallavar, eḻuttil vallavar, ōviyattil vallavar, patippittalil vallavar ākiyavarkaḷ uṟuppiṉarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟa vāyppaḷittāl itaḻkaḷ mikac cīriya muṟaiyil veḷivarum.  veḷiyil ulavak kūṭiya accitaḻai veḷiyiṭu vataṟku oru taṉic ceyaṟkuḻu vēṇṭum.  itil it tuṟaiyil aṉupavamuḷḷa virumpip paṇiyēṟkum āciriyarkaḷum, tiṟamaiyuḷḷa oru cila māṇākkarkaḷum uṟuppiṉarkaḷāka iruntu paṇiyāṟṟalām.  itaṉāl putiya tāymoḻip pāṭattiṭṭattil kuṟippiṭappeṟṟuḷḷa kaṭṭuraikaḷaip poruḷuk kēṟṟavāṟu piriviṉai ceytu tokuttal, uḷḷuṟai tayārittal, aṭṭavaṇai tayārittal, piṉṉiṇaippukkaḷ tayārittal, accup piḻaittiruttam ceytal, eḻuttōviyaṅ kaḷukkum ēṉaiya ōviyaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟa talaippukkaḷai amaittal, kaṭṭuraikaḷil uṭ pirivukaḷukkut talaippukkaḷait tarutal, kaṭṭurai-kataikaḷai oru cila vākkiyaṅkaḷāl tiṟaṉāytal, puttakattiṟaṉāyvu ceytal, ēṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil niṟuttaṟ kuṟikaḷai iṭutal pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa tuṟaikaḷil māṇākkarkaḷukku nalla payiṟciyiṉai aḷikkalām.  itaṟkākac celaviṭap peṟum nēram moḻi kaṟpikkum tuṟaiyil ciṟanta palaṉait tarum.  aruñcoṟkaḷukkup poruḷ kūṟutal, collilakkaṇam kūṟutal, canti pirittuc collutal, ceyyuṭkaḷai aṉvayap paṭuttip patavurai, poḻippurai kūṟutal ākiyavaṟṟil maṭṭilum kavaṉam celuttic celaviṭum nērattil etir pārkkum palaṉaiviṭap paṉmaṭaṅku atikamākavē itu palaṉ aḷikkum eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyamillai.
12. ilakkiyam
etu ilakkiyam: ilakkiyam eṉṉum peyarukkut takutiyāṉa puttakaṅkaḷai aṟiñarkaḷtām iyaṟṟa muṭiyum.  paṇṭiruntē ilakkiya āciriyarkaḷ palavakaiyāṉa ciṟappuk kaḷaiyum  aṟivāṟṟalaiyum uṭaiyavarkaḷāka iruntaṉar.  evarum nūliyaṟṟal eṉpatu iyalāta ceyal aṟivāl nirampiyavar mikac ciṟu tokaiyiṉa rēyāvar.  
	ārttacapai nūṟṟoruvar āyirattoṉ ṟāmpulavar
  vārttai patiṉā yirattoruvar1 
eṉṟa veṇpāp pakutiyai nōkkuka.  ik kālattil ellōrum puttakam eḻuta vēṇṭum eṉṟu viḻaikiṉṟaṉar; eḻutavum ceykiṉṟaṉar.  avarkaḷ eḻutiya aṉaittum ilakkiyaṅkaḷākā.  maṉitaṉuṭaiya aṟiviṟkup payaṉpaṭupavai evaiyō avaiyē ilakkiyaṅkaḷ. nūlkaḷ.  māntar maṉakkōṭṭam tīrkkum nūl eṉpatai aṟika ilakkiyattiṟkut tāṉ maṉitaṉuṭaiya uḷḷuṇarcciyait tūṇṭakkūṭiya āṟṟal uṇṭu. maṉita vāḻvukku araṇāka iruntu pātukākkak kūṭiyatum atuvē.
entap puttakattaip paṭitta piṟaku mīṇṭum mīṇṭum paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa vēṭkai eḻukiṉṟatō atu tāṉ ilakkiyam; paṉmuṟai paṭittālum ciṟanta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ paṭikkuntōṟum putupputuc cuvaiyait tōṟṟuvikkum. āyun toṟuntoṟum iṉpan taruntamiḻ tērun toṟumiṉi tāntamiḻ eṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ uḷḷak karuttaic cintittup pārttāl ivvuṇmai pulappaṭum. kamparāmāyaṇam, cīvaka cintāmaṇi, cilappatikāram, tirukkuṟaḷ pōṉṟa nūlkaḷ nām ettaṉai muṟai paṭittālum avaṟṟiṭam nammuṭaiya ārvam kuṉṟuvatillai. aṟivait toṭavalla nūlkaḷ ciṟanta ilakkiyaṅkaḷākā; orumuṟai paṭittu avaṟṟiluḷḷa poruḷ kaḷai aṟintu koṇṭāl mīṇṭum nām avaṟṟaip paṭikka virumpuvatillai. āṉāl, uṇarcci nilaiyaip paṟṟum nūlkaḷ aṅṅaṉa maṉṟu; ettaṉai muṟai paṭittālum calipputtarātavai. aṉumaṉatu āṟṟalum, ilakkuvaṉatu toṇṭum, parataṉuṭaiya cakōtara vāñcaiyum, kukaṉatu naṭpum, cītaiyiṉkaṟpum, kumpakaruṇaṉuṭaiya ceyn naṉṟiyaṟitalum nam maṉattai viṭṭu eṉṟum nīṅkā tiruppavai. ittakaiya uyarnta paṇpukaḷaip peṟṟiruppavaitām kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyātu eṉṟum nilaittu niṟpavai. avaṟṟaip paṭikkun tōṟum iṉpattaip peṟalām.
	naviltoṟum nūlnayam pōlum payiltoṟum
 paṇpuṭai yāḷar toṭarpu.2
eṉṟa vaḷḷuvar vāymoḻiyē ciṟanta ilakkiya uraiyāṇi. orunūl payiluntōṟum nayam payavātāyiṉ atu ciṟanta nūlaṉṟu eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟiviṭalām.
orumuṟai paṭitta māttirattil iṉpantarum puttakaṅkaḷum, ettaṉai taṭavai paṭittālum viḷaṅkātavaikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avai ilakkiyaṅkaḷākā. oru muṟai paṭitta māttirattilē poruḷ pulappaṭātu, nuṇuki ārāya, nuṉiyiṟ karumpu tiṉṟaṟṟē eṉpatupōla varavarac cuvai payakkum nūlkaḷē ilakkiyaṅkaḷ. toṭaṅkiṉa nāḷtoṭṭu āyuṭkālam varaiyilum paṭittālum veṟuppillāmal cuvai payappavai ilakkiyaṅkaḷil uyartaramāṉavai.
avaṟṟuḷ kavitai ilakkiyaṅkaḷ iṉṉum ciṟantavai. kaviyiṉippuk kaṇṭa kalaiñaḷ vaḷḷuvaṉ, eṉṉoruvaṉ, cāntuṇaiyum kallātavāṟu eṉṟu iraṅkiyiruttalai eṇṇi uṇarka. ilakkiyaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷait tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷka.
cāttiraṅkaḷai mēṉāṭṭār aṟiviyalkaḷ (Sciences) eṉṟum, kāviyaṅkaḷai ilakkiyam (Literature) eṉṟum kūṟuvar; tamiḻil ivai muṟaiyē nūl eṉṟum pā eṉṟum vaḻaṅki varuvaṉavākak koḷḷalām. tamiḻ ilakkiya nūlkaḷ perumpālum aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu eṉṉum nāṉku kuṟikkōṭ poruḷkaḷaik kūṟum. avaṟṟai veḷiyiṭum muṟaikaḷuḷ pala vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷa. cila nūlkaḷ aṟattiṟkuriya vitikaḷaiyum vilakkukaḷaiyum nērē nītiyākap pulappaṭuttum; avaṟṟaiyē cila nūlkaḷ uvamaimukattāṉum utāraṇa mukattāṉum pulappaṭuttum; kataippōkkil avaṟṟaiyē kuṟippākavum cila nūlkaḷ uṇarttum. ellā nūlkaḷum ciṟantavaiyē yeṉiṉum, karuttukaḷai eḷiya muṟaiyil cuvai payakkum pāṅkil, kataippōkkil, kāppiya vaṭivil kūṟum nūlkaḷē ciṟantavai. vaṭa nūlār kāviyattai maṉaiviyiṉ uraikku iṇaiyākac colluvar. kaṇavaṉukkut taṉatu karuttai yaṟivikkappukum maṉaivi, mika iṉiya coṟkaḷāl maṉaṅka varum muṟaiyil mella aṟivittalaip pōla, kāviyaṅkaḷ uyarnta nītikaḷaic coṟporuḷ nayaṅkaḷōṭu maṉaṅkavarum vaṇṇam pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. vaṉappiṉaippaṟṟik kūṟavanta pērāciriyar ataṉāṟ pala ceyyuḷum uṟuppāyt tiraṇṭu perukiya toṭar nilaiyatē vaṉappeṉṉum peyarppakuti vakaiyāṉ ēṟpa teṉpatu3 eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar. vaṉappu-aḻaku. oru moḻiyiṉ vaṉappukkaḷai ammoḻiyiluḷḷa toṭar-nilaic ceyyuṭkaḷākiya kāppiyaṅkaḷāl aṟintu koḷḷalām. kāppiyaṅkaḷaip pōl vaṉappiṉaip pulappaṭuttuvatil talaimai yuṭaiyaṉa piṟavakai ilakkiyaṅkaḷalla eṉpatu īṇṭu aṟiya vēṇṭiyatoṉṟu. ivvāṟu ilakkiyattaippaṟṟiya pala karuttukkaḷai āciriyarkaḷ aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
ilakkiya vaṭivaṅkaḷ: oru moḻiyiluḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ceyyuḷ vaṭivam, urainaṭai vaṭivam eṉa iru vaṭivaṅkaḷil amaiyum. tamiḻ moḻiyiluḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷum pā vaṭivamākavum uḷḷaṉa; urai naṭaiyākavum uḷḷaṉa. ivviruvakai vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, vaḷarcci, avaṟṟiṭam kāṇappeṟum vēṟṟumaikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟait tamiḻmoḻiyāciri yarkaḷ oruvāṟu potup paṭa uṇarntiruttal iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.4
iṉṟu ceyyuḷ eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻaṅkum nūlkaḷ vēṟu; tolkāppiyar kālattil attoṭarāl vaḻaṅkiya nūlkaḷ vēṟu. iṉṟu pāvaiyum ceyyuḷaiyum oru poruḷ kuṟitta coṟkaḷākavē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm. tolkāppiyar kālattil ceyyappaṭuvaṉa ellām ceyyuḷ eṉṟa peyarāl vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉa. urai, pāṭṭu, nūl ākiya mūṉṟumē ceyyuḷ eṉṟa collāl uṇarttap peṟṟaṉa. ceyyuḷiyaliṉ mutal nūṟpāvil kūṟappeṟum māttirai, eḻuttiyal, acai mutaliya muppattunāṉkiṉaik koṇṭaṉavellām ceyyuṭku uṟuppākum eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttalaik kāṇka. pā eṉpatum ceyyuḷ uṟuppukkaḷil oṉṟākum.
tamiḻcceyyuḷait tolkāppiyar ēḻuvakaiyākap pirittup pēcuvar. avai pāṭṭu, urai, nūl, vāymoḻi, pici, aṅkatam, mutuccol eṉpaṉa. avaṟṟai,
pāṭṭuṭai nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
aṅkata mutuccollō ṭavvēḻ nilattum
vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varaippiṉ
nāṟpeya rellai yakattavar vaḻaṅkum
yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar.5
eṉṟa nūṟpāvāl aṟiyalām.  inta ēḻuvakaik ceyyuḷkaḷaiyum āciriyar aṭivaraiyaṟai uḷḷaṉa, aṭivaraiyaṟai illaṉa eṉṟu iru pirivākavum pirittuk kāṭṭuvar.  aṭivaraiyaṟai uḷḷaṉa vaṟṟil pākkaḷ yāvum aṭaṅkum; aṭivaraiyaṟai illaṉa vaṟṟil nūl, urai, pici, mutumoḻi, mantiram,kuṟippueṉṟaāṟumaṭaṅkum..6 itovvoṉṟaiyumpaṟ¿āṇṭu¡kaṇLkoḷka.  nāḷaṭaivil ceyyuḷ eṉṟa col poruḷāl curuṅkikkoṇṭē vantu iṉṟu `pā ceyyuḷ oṉṟaiyē kuṟikkiṉṟatu.  tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ `pā ceyyuḷ, uraic ceyyuḷ ākiyavaṟṟaik kuṟittum, avaṟṟiṉ naṭai vēṟṟumai kuṟittum ōraḷavu aṟintukoḷḷutal iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.
pā naṭaiyum urai naṭaiyum: em moḻiyilum pānaṭaiyē urai naṭaikku muṟpaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu āyvāḷarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.  ilakkiya naṭaikaḷil aṭivaraiyaṟaiyuṟṟu naṭakkum pā naṭaiyē mikavum toṉmai vāyntatu.  ceṟivu vāynta ceyyuḷ naṭaiyē ciṟanta karuttukkaḷai niṉaivil vaittukkoḷvataṟku ēṟṟatu.  ārāycci ceyyum aṟiviyal muṟaikaḷ ēṟpaṭāta akkālattu iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷait turuvi ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ uṟuppukkaḷai alacippārkkātu kaṟpaṉaiyaiyē tuṇaiyākak koṇṭu makkaḷ tam maṉak kaṇṇāl avaṟṟaik kaṇṭu vantaṉar.  kāraṇakāriya muṟai avarkaḷiṭattu amaiyavillai; ātalāl ataṉai viḷakki virittu ārāyntu kūṟum urainaṭaiyum tōṉṟavillai.  eṉavē, mkkālattilkkkaḷjmTṟavēṇṭiyamṉaittaiyumbcayyuḷtṭivilēyēTṟiṉār. kataikaḷaiyum pā vaṭivil amaittaṉar; kaṭavuḷaippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷum, kaṭavuḷukkum makkaḷukkumuḷḷa toṭarpaik kūṟum camayak karuttukkaḷum ceyyuḷ vaṭivilēyē amaintaṉa. ivvāṟu kūṟuvar kūrthōp eṉṉum āṅkila aṟiñar.  emarcaṉ eṉpārum, icaiyum etukaiyum kuḻantaikku iṉpam aḷippaṉavām eṉṟum, ilakkiya varalāṟṟil kavitai naṭaiyē urai naṭaikku muntiyatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar.  immarapaiyum varalāṟṟaiyum oṭṭiyē nam moḻiyum amaintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu.  tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟṟai aṟivōr pākkaḷē tamiḻil mutalil eḻuntaṉa eṉpataiyum, piṉṉarē urai naṭai vantatu eṉpataiyum eḷitil uṇarvar.  iṉṉum oru cila moḻikaḷiluḷḷaṉapōl tamiḻ moḻiyil palatuṟaikaḷilum kalaikaḷilum urainaṭai ilakkiyaṅkaḷ palkip perukavillai.
naṭai eṉpatu tamiḻil oḻukkattaik kuṟikkum7; cellum celavaiyum kuṟikkum. naṭa eṉpatu iraṇṭukkum pakutiyākum.  celavaik kuṟikkumpoḻutu atu toḻiṟ peyarākiṉṟatu; piṟa poruḷaik kuṟikkumpoḻutu toḻilāku peyarākum.  ac col ceyyuḷ naṭai, urai naṭai eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkum poḻutu ceyyuḷ uraikaḷ cellum celavu eṉpataik kuṟikkum.  kāl naṭaiyiṉ celavu oruvarai ōriṭattiliruntu piṟitōriṭattiṟkuk koṇṭuceluttuvatu pōl innaṭaikaḷum ceyyuṭ poruḷaiyum uraikkum uraipporuḷaiyum oruvar maṉattiliruntu piṟitoruvar maṉattiṟkuk koṇṭu pōyc cērkkiṉṟaṉa.8  avvāṟu koṇṭupōyc cērkkum karuvikaḷ ceyyuḷ urainaṭaikaḷil vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷākum.
coṟceṟivu, poruṭṭeḷivu, icai nalam, aṇi nalam mutaliya nalliyalpukaḷ amaiya, karutiya poruḷmēl pala coṟkaḷait toṭuttu amaippatuvē ceyyuḷ naṭaiyākum.  oru poruḷmēl pala col koṇarntu īṭṭal ceyyuḷ ceytal eṉṟum, pala col toṭarntu poruḷ kāṭṭu vaṉavaṟṟuḷ ocai taḻuviyavaṟṟaip pāṭṭeṉṉalām eṉṟum uraiyāciriyarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.  ceyyuḷ naṭaikkuḷ pukāta nilaiyil makkaḷ potuvākap pēcummoḻi urai naṭaiyākum.  kaṟṟavarkaḷum maṟṟavarkaḷum eḷitil poruḷ uṇarntu koḷḷakkūṭiyatāy ceyyuḷ naṭaiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭu aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaiyil nām pēcip payiṉṟu varum naṭaiyai urainaṭai eṉṉalām.  aḵtu uraittup pōtaliṉ uraiyum, tēṅki niṟkāmal naṭantu pōtaliṉ naṭaiyum āyiṟṟu.  ilattīṉ moḻiyiluḷḷa Oratio Pedestris eṉṟa toṭar tamiḻiluḷḷa pēccu naṭai eṉṟa toṭarōṭu oppiṭattakkatāka uḷḷatu.  aṉṟiyum,
	pūvaru maḻalai yaṉṉam
