uyarnilaippaḷḷikkuriya pāṭanulkaḷil cila pāṭaṅkaḷ paḻaiya uraiyāciriyarkaḷiṉ naṭaivēṟṟumaiyaik kāṭṭak kūṭiyaṉavāka iruttalvēṇṭum.  aṉṟiyum, avai av vuraiyāciriyarkaḷ uraikaṇṭa ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku muṉṉuraiyāka amaintāl nalam.  karuttu vēṟṟumaikaḷukku iṭamillā ikkālac cuvaikkēṟṟa putiya poruḷkaḷaippaṟṟiyum, putiya karuttukkaḷaippaṟṟiyum palvēṟu āciriyarkaḷāl eḻutap peṟṟa kaṭṭuraikaḷai urainaṭaip pakutiyākat tokukkalām.  ilakkiyac cuvaiyai vaḷarkkum ilakkiyap pakutikaḷ, paṇṭai nāṭakaviyalai viḷakkum pakutikaḷ. nuṇkalaikaḷ, icaik karuvikaḷ, aṟiviyal putumaikaḷ, uṟpattip perukkap perun toḻil muṟaikaḷ, kuṭumpa aṟiviyal, kirāmap puṉaramaippu paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ, uḷnāṭṭu veḷināṭṭup piracciṉaip paṟṟiya  ceytikaḷ, ciṟanta aṟiñarkaḷiṉ coṟpoḻivukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷākak koḷḷalām.  pēraṟiñarkaḷiṉ coṟpoḻivukaḷaiyum, ciṟanta kavitaip pakutikaḷiṉ urainaṭaiyākkaṅkaḷaiyum, periyārkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷ, taṉ-varalāṟukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiliruntu tērnteṭutta pakutikaḷaiyum; ilakkiyamaṇam kamaḻum kaṭitaṅkaḷaiyum, ilakkiyac cuvaiyūṭṭum ciṟukataikaḷaiyum, ciṟu urainaṭai nāṭakaṅkaḷaiyum ivaṟṟuṭaṉ cērttuk koḷḷalām,  veḷināṭṭuc celavukaḷ, putiyanāṭu kaṇṭa varalāṟukaḷ ākiyavai urainaṭaip pāṭaṅkaḷāka amaintāl māṇākkarkaḷ avaṟṟai virumpippaṭippar.
naṭaiyum payiṟcikaḷum:  urainaṭaip pakutikaḷil mēṟ koḷḷap peṟum coṟṟoṭar amaippu muṟai, naṭai ākiyavai vakuppu nilaikkēṟṟavāṟu māṟumpaṭi amaiya vēṇṭum.  aṉṟiyum naṭai, iṉimai, eḷimai, tūymai mutaliya paṇpukaḷaik koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.  vakuppukkut takuntavāṟu naṭai paṭippaṭiyāka uyarntu ceṉṟāl niṟainta payaṉai viḷai vikkum.  ilakkaṇa amaitikaḷum, coṟṟoṭarkaḷum, maraput toṭarkaḷum vakuppu uyara uyara paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarntu pōtal nalam.  urainaṭaiyil cantaṅkaḷ, mōṉaikaḷ, col vittaikaḷ pōṉṟa muṟaikaḷai mēṟkoḷḷutalait tavirttal vēṇṭum.  vaḻakkaṟṟa coṟkaḷ, paḻaiya vaḻakkāṟukaḷ (Usages) pōṉṟavaṟṟai urainaṭaiyil pukutti naṭaiyiṉ viraivāṉa pōkkaik keṭuttal kūṭātu.  pāṭaṅkaḷiṉ kīḻttarappeṟum moḻippayiṟcikaḷ pāṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷai yoṭṭiyiruttal vēṇṭum1.  māṇākkarkaḷ karuttuṇarntu tam vākkilēyē vaittu vaḻaṅkakkūṭiya aruñ coṟkaḷ mutaliyavai moḻip payiṟcikaḷāka amaitalvēṇṭum.  avai arumai nōkkip paṭippaṭiyāy oḻuṅku ceyyappeṟalvēṇṭum.  aṉṟiyum, eḻuttup payiṟcikaḷ ilakkiyac cuvaiyait tūṇṭak kūṭiyaṉavāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  ovvoru pāṭattiṉ īṟṟilum moḻip payiṟcikaḷum, aruñcol viḷakkaṅkaḷum, piṟa viḷakkaṅkaḷum curukkamāka iṭampeṟac celtal vēṇṭum.  urainaṭaiyil kaiyāḷappeṟum cila aruñ coṟkaḷ iṭam nōkkip poruḷuṇarat-takkaṉavāyiruttal vēṇṭum.  appaṭiyiruntāl ap paḻakkam avarkaḷai akaṉṟapaṭippiṉpāl īṭupaṭat tuṇai ceyyum.  oru putiya collai oru pāṭattil palvēṟu muṟaikaḷil kaiyāṇṭāl, māṇākkarkaḷ ataṉ poruḷai eḷitilaṟintu  tāmum ataip pala iṭaṅkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttum payiṟciyiṉaip peṟuvar.  ittakaiya coṟkaḷai ovvoru pāṭattiṉ īṟṟilum tokuttuk kāṭṭavēṇṭum.  aṉṟiyum, cila ilakkaṇa vaḻakkukaḷ, cantikaḷ, vēṟu ilakkaṇa muṟaikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaik kaṟpikkum orucila moḻip payiṟcikaḷum, vāykkuṭ paṭippataṟkāṉa payiṟcikaḷum, vīṭṭu-vēlaip pakutikaḷum ippakutiyil iṭampeṟutal iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.
camayam, oḻukkam mutaliyavaipaṟṟiya pakutikaḷ:  camayam oḻukkam mutaliyavaipaṟṟit tērnteṭukkappeṟum pakutikaḷ camayap piṇakkai uṇṭu paṇṇāmalum, pala camayattiṉarukku uriyavaiyāyum allatu ottaṉavāyum iruntāl ellōrum puttakaṅkaḷai virumpi ēṟpar.  tirukkuṟaḷ, nālaṭiyār pōṉṟa aṟanūlkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷ ellā camayattiṉarukkum potu vāṉavai.  tēvāram, tiruvācakam, tivyappirapantam, tiruvaruṭpā, tiruvicaippā ākiya nūlkaḷum; tāyayumāṉavar, paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ, vīramāmuṉivar, arikiruṣṇapiḷḷai, ve. pa. cuppiramaṇiya mutaliyār, matāṉ cākipu ākiyōriṉ pāṭalkaḷum ippakutikku uriyavai.  tēmpāvaṇi, cīṟāppurāṇam, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaiyum ippakutikku ēṟṟavai.  vētanāyakam piḷḷaiya varkaḷiṉ camaracak kīrttaṉaikaḷaiyum tārāḷamāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷalām.  makkaḷil eppakutiyiṉaraiyāvatu puṇpaṭuttum karuttukkaḷ nūlkaḷil iṭampeṟalākātu.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka matuvilakkaip paṟṟiya koḷkaikaḷ ellōrum oppuk koḷḷattakkavai.  tēnīr, kāḵpi mutaliyaṉa kuṭittal kaḷkuṭi pōṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatait tavirttal vēṇṭum.  maṟṟum tirunāvukkaracar, tiruñāṉacampantar pōṉṟa camayap periyārkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷ piṟa camayattiṉarkaḷiṉ maṉam puṇpaṭāta muṟaiyil eḻutappeṟal vēṇṭum.
piṟa ceytikaḷ:  pāṭap poruḷkaḷil evvita piḻaikaḷukkum iṭantaralākātu.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, iyaṟkai eytiya periyārkaḷ iṉṟum ivvulakil vāḻvatākak kūṟutalait tavirttal vēṇṭum; patippukkup patippu itaik kavaṉittal avaciyam.  aruvikaḷiṉ (Water falls) āḻam, malaikaḷiṉ uyaram, natikaḷiṉ nīḷam, periyārkaḷ vāḻnta kālam, kaittoḻilkaḷ, toḻil tuṟaikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaippaṟṟiya takavalkaḷil piḻai nēriṭāmal pārttuk koḷḷalvēṇṭum.  tamiḻ nūlkaḷil nakaiccuvaiyiṉaik kāṇpatu kutiraikkompāka uḷḷatu;  oṉṟiraṇṭu attipūtta mātiri kāṇappeṟiṉum, avai mēlnāṭṭu ilakkiyaṅkaḷil Humour eṉṟu kūṟappeṟum.  tarattil illaiyeṉṟutāṉ collavēṇṭum.  āṉāl, ikkālattil tōṉṟiyuḷḷa maṟumalarcci ilakkiyaṅkaḷil attakaiya nakaiccuvaip paṇpu talaikāṭṭat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu.  kalvi, nāṭōṭi, e. vi. É., putumaippittaṉ pōṉṟār eḻutiyuḷḷavaṟṟilum, ulakamcuṟṟiya tamiḻaṉ ē. kē. ceṭṭiyār eḻutiyuḷḷa pirayāṇap puttakaṅkaḷilum mēṉāṭṭu Humour uṭaṉ oppiṭattakka cila nakaiccuvaip pakutikaḷaik kāṇalām.  avaṟṟaip pāṭa nūlkaḷil cērkkalām.  nakaiccuvai poruntiya kataikaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ, nāṭōṭip pāṭalkaḷ, taṉippāṭalkaḷ, cilēṭaic ceyyuṭkaḷ, cīṭṭukkavikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaip pāṭanūlkaḷil mikutiyākac cērkka vēṇṭum.
iṉṟu veḷiyiṭappeṟum pāṭapputtakaṅkaḷ yāvumē maṉaniṟaivu peṟakkūṭiya muṟaiyil illai.  ellōrumē tamakku ellāt takutiyum iruppatāka eṇṇikkoṇṭu pāṭanūlkaḷai eḻutat tuṇikiṉṟaṉar; māṇākkariṉ nilaik kēṟṟavāṟu nūl eḻutuvatu eḷitāṉa ceyalaṉṟu.  pāṭa nūlkaḷiṉ uṟpattiyil kāṇum ūḻalkaḷaṉaittaiyum uyar nilaikkalvi aṟikkai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṟatu pārāṭṭat takkatu.2  nalla nūlkaḷai uṟpatti ceyvatil ivvaṟikkai kūṟum yōcaṉaikaḷ aṉaittum naṭaimuṟaiyil mēṟ koḷḷat takkavai.  aṟikkai kuṟippiṭum kuḻuvoṉṟu uruvāki mēṟpaṭi yōcaṉaikaḷ ceyalpaṭṭāl, nalla muṟaiyil pāṭa nūlkaḷ tōṉṟum eṉpataṟku aiyamillai.
2. tuṇaippāṭa nūlkaḷ
āḻnta paṭippait  tavira, viraivākavum vāykkuḷḷum paṭikkavēṇṭiya piṟitoru paṭippum uṇṭu.  itai akaṉṟa paṭippu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar.  itaṉ iṉṟiyamaiyā iyalpukaḷ viraivāka vāykkuṭ paṭittalum, karuttuṇartalumākum.  akaṉṟa paṭippil mūṉṟu nōkkaṅkaḷ niṟaivēṟukiṉṟaṉa.  mutalāvatu: aṟivu nōkkam.  itai veṭ eṉṟa aṟiñar mika aḻakākak kūṟukiṉṟār.3 avar nūliṉ itayattait taṉiyāka eṭuttu viṭutal eṉpatē aṟivu nōkkam eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟār.  m~jhtJ, Éiuthf üÈ‹ âu©l fU¤ij¥ ãÊªbjL¤jny mj‹ bghUshF«.;  viraivākap paṭikkumpoḻutē karuttuṇartal, cintaṉaik karuttukkaḷaip pōṟṟutal, uyir nilaik karuttai uṭaṉaṟintukoḷḷutal ākiya tiṟaṉkaḷāl innōkkam niṟaivēṟum.  iraṇṭāvatu:  ilakkiya nōkkam, ulaka aṟivaiyum potu aṟivaiyum vaḷarttup paṭippil oru paṟṟai uṇṭākkit tāmākavē paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa āvalaiyum paḻakkattaiyum tōṟṟuvippatē ilakkiya nōkkamākum.  mūṉṟāvatu: moḻi nōkkam.  moḻiyaṟivai vaḷarttalum coṟkaḷañciyattaip perukkutalum moḻi nōkkattiṉ pāṟpaṭum.  āḻntapaṭippil arumaiyākak kāṇum putiya coṟkaḷaiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷaiyum tam vākkil vaittu vaḻaṅkum payiṟciyiṉaip peṟutal pōlavē, akaṉṟa paṭippil kaṇṇuṟum putiya coṟkaḷ coṟṟoṭarkaḷiṉ āṭcikaḷait teḷivāka aṟintukoḷḷum vāyppiṉaippeṟutal vēṇṭum.  ivaiyum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ coṟkaḷañciyattuṭaṉ cērntu avarkaḷiṉ karuttuṇarum āṟṟalai vaḷarkkum.  vāykkuṭ paṭippil peṟum naṉmaikaḷaiyum nōkkaṅkaḷaiyum akaṉṟa paṭippiṟkuriyaṉavāka iṇaittuk koḷḷalām.4  akaṉṟa paṭippiṉ nōkkaṅkaḷai niṟaivēṟṟum vaḻikaḷil tuṇaip pāṭanūṟ paṭippum oṉṟākum.  eṉavē, tuṇaippāṭa nūlkaḷaip paṟṟi īṇṭu oru ciṟitu kuṟippiṭuvōm.
nalla tuṇaippāṭa nūlkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷ:  pāṭa nūlkaḷiṉ naṟpaṇpukaḷākak kuṟippiṭṭa aṉaittaiyum appaṭiyē tuṇaippāṭa nūlkaṭkum koḷḷalām.  puṟattōṟṟam, akat tōṟṟam, poruḷamaippu,  nūlāciriyarkaḷ, urainaṭaip pakutikaḷ, naṭai, camayam oḻukkampaṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ, piṟaceytikaḷ ākiya talaippukkaḷil āṇṭuk kūṟiyaṉavaṟṟil poruntuvaṉavaṟṟai yellām īṇṭu appaṭiyē iṇaittuk koḷḷalām.  avaṟṟait tavira tuṇaippāṭa nūlkaḷ vēṟu cila kūṟukaḷaiyum koṇṭiruttal iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum.
tuṇaippāṭa nūlkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ aṟiyāta coṟkaḷ, coṟṟoṭarkaḷ, maraput toṭarkaḷ, vēṟu moḻi vaṭivaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaiyāḷutal kūṭātu.  karuttuṇarvatil ivaiyum naṭaiyum taṭaiyāka iruttalkūṭātu.  māṇākkarkaḷ āciriyariṉ tuṇai ciṟitumiṉṟi, vēṇṭumāṉāl akarātit tuṇaiyuṭaṉ, nūlkaḷait tāmākavē paṭittu makiḻa vēṇṭum; paṭittu muṭittōm eṉṟa veṟṟiyuṇarcci eḷitākak kaivarappeṟutal vēṇṭum.
ittakaiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyil atikamākat tōṉṟi varukiṉṟaṉa.  āṉāl, perumpālum vakuppukku vaikkappeṟum nūlkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṉ aṟivu nilaiyiṉai oṭṭiyaṉavāka illātiruppatu mikavum varunta;tatakkatu.  pāṭa nūṟ kuḻuviṉarāl ēṟkappeṟṟa nūlkaḷuḷḷum oru vakuppuk kuriyaṉavākavuḷḷa nūlkaḷ pala kūṟukaḷil orē mātiriyāka iruppatillai.  inta nūlāciriyar aintām paṭivattilum, ep. ē. vakuppilum, pulavar vakuppilum orē nūlaip paṭikkum vāyppiṉaip peṟṟār eṉṟāl, nūlkaḷait tērnteṭuppōr evvaḷavu kavaṉak kuṟaivāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum, allatu collakkūṭāta kāraṇaṅkaḷukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.
nūlkaḷ palvēṟu tuṟaikaḷaippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷait taruvaṉavākavum iruttalvēṇṭum.  pāṭa nūlkaḷaik kuṟippiṭum poḻutu 	urainaṭaip pakutikaḷ eṉṟa talaippil kūṟap peṟṟa pala karuttukkaḷaiyum tuṇaippāṭa nūlkaḷukkum ēṟṟavaiyākak koḷḷalām.  tamiḻil pirayāṇa nūlkaḷ kuṟaivāka uḷḷaṉa.  ulakam cuṟṟum tamiḻaṉ, citpavānanta aṭikaḷ, ti. cu. avināciliṅkam, ṭākṭar mu. varatarācaṉār, cōma. ilakkumaṇaṉ ceṭṭiyār (cōmale) eḻutiyuḷḷavai pōl ivaikaḷ cuvaiyāka eḻutappeṟṟāl māṇākkarkaḷ avaṟṟaik katai nūlkaḷaip pōlavē virumpip paṭippar.
tuṇaippāṭa nūlkaḷaip payiṟṟum muṟai:  tuṇaip pāṭa nulkaḷai āḻnta paṭippiṟkuriya pāṭa nūlaippōl vakuppil kaṟpikkavēṇṭiya avaciyam illai.  appaṭik kaṟpittālum, poḻutup pōkkākak kaṟpittāl pōtum.  pāṭanūlaik kaṟpikkumpoḻutu mēṟkoḷḷappeṟum aruñcol viḷakkam, coṟṟoṭar maraputtoṭar viḷakkaṅkaḷ, avaṟṟaic conta vākkiyaṅkaḷil vaittup payiṟṟuvikkum paṉmuṟaip payiṟci, ariya collāṭci ilakkaṇakkuṟiyīṭu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ viḷakkaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa muṟaikaḷai mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatillai.
tuṇaippāṭa nūlkaḷai māṇākkarkaḷē vīṭṭil paṭittu varavēṇṭum; tuṇaippāṭap paṭippukkeṉa pāṭa vēḷai otukkappeṟṟiruntāl atil avarkaḷ annūlkaḷaip paṭikkalām.  tēvaiyāṉāl appāṭa vēḷaiyil āciriyar nūliluḷḷa kataiyai allatu poruḷaippaṟṟip paranta nōkkāka ārāyalām; katai naṭakkum kālam, iṭam, cūḻnilai, katai māntarkaḷ, tuṇaikkuṟippukkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟai muṉṉuraiyāka eṭuttukkūṟi annūlaip paṭippatil māṇākkarkaḷiṭaiyē ārvattai eḻuppiviṭalām.  nulaip paṭikkat toṭaṅkumuṉ cintaṉaiyaik kavarakkūṭiya cila viṉākkaḷait tantu avaṟṟiṟku viṭai kaṇṭu varumāṟu ēvalām.  av viṉākkaḷ ivvāṟu amaiyalām; kataip pōkku, katai māntarkaḷiṉ kuṇa nalaṉkaḷ, nikaḻccik kuṟippu, varuṇaṉai mutaliyavaṟṟaip paṟṟiya ciṟu kuṟippukkaḷ mutaliyavai eḻutivarumāṟu ēvalām; allatu ciṟu ciṟu viṉākkaḷ tantu avaṟṟiṟku viṭai kāṇumāṟu tūṇṭalām.  putiya muṟai viṉākkaḷiṉ mūlamum avarkaḷuṭaiya paṭippaṟivai aḷantu kāṇalām.
vīṭṭil māṇākkarkaḷ annūlkaḷaip paṭittuvanta piṟaku mēṟkaṇṭa viṉākkaḷai viṭuttu viṭai kūṟumāṟu ēvalām.  iv viṉākkaḷ yāvum māṇakkarkaḷ karuttuṇarntu paṭittu varukiṉṟaṉarā eṉpatai aṟivataṟkut tuṇai ceypavai.  tuṇaippāṭa nūlkaḷil paṭittaṟintavaṟṟai orucila talaippuk kaḷil kaṭṭuraikaḷāka varaivatilum payiṟcikaḷ taralām.  kaṭṭurai varaivataṟkeṉak kūṟappeṟṟa vitikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟik kaṭṭurai eḻututal naṭaipeṟal vēṇṭum.  kaṭṭurai tiruttum muṟaikaḷai mēṟkoṇṭu avaṟṟai āciriyar tiruttavum vēṇṭum.
3. potuppaṭippukkuriya nūlkaḷ
nūlakap paṭippukkākap pāṭanūṟ kuḻuviṉarāl ēṟkap peṟṟa pala urainaṭai nūlkaḷaip potup paṭippukkāṉa nūlkaḷākak karutalām.  perumpālum avaikaḷum tuṇaip pāṭa nūlkaḷaippōlavē eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  iṉṟu patippāḷarkaḷ nūliṉ puṟattōṟṟattil kavaṉam celutti mēṟ caṭṭaiyuṭaṉ nūlkaḷaip patippikkiṉṟaṉarēyaṉṟi, nūlkaḷiṉ akattōṟṟattil nalla muṟaiyil kavaṉam celuttavillai.  akattōṟṟattil kavaṉam celutti orucila āciriyarkaḷāl eḻutappeṟṟa nūlkaḷaip patippāḷarkaḷ ātarippatillai; avai accuppoṟi ēṟum pēṟṟaip peṟāmal kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
inta nūṟṟāṇṭil paḻaiya muṟaiyilum putiya muṟaiyilum pala urainaṭai nūlkaḷ veḷivantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.  ilakkiya ārāycci nūlkaḷ, kāla ārāycci nūlkaḷ, palvēṟu poruḷkaḷaippaṟṟiya urainaṭai nūlkaḷ, ilakkaṇa ārāycci nūlkaḷ ākiyavai paḻaiya muṟaiyil veḷivantavai.  putiya muṟaiyil pala maṟumalarcci ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mēṉāṭṭu ilakkiya vakaikaḷai yoṭṭi veḷivantuḷḷaṉa; nāṭōṟum veḷivantu koṇṭumirukkiṉṟaṉa.  paḻaiya muṟaiyil veḷivantavaṟṟaip pulamai mikkavarkaḷ maṭṭiluntāṉ paṭittu iṉpuṟa muṭiyum.  putiyamuṟaiyil veḷivantuḷḷavaṟṟaip ellōrum paṭittuc cuvaikkalām.  putiya muṟaiyil veḷivarupavai iraṇṭu vakaikaḷil vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa.  mutal vakai nūlkaḷil ciṟukataic celvaṅkaḷ, putiṉaṅkaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷ, tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷ pōṉṟa mēṉāṭṭu urainaṭai ilakkiya vakaikaḷaic cērnta vaṟṟaik kuṟippiṭalām.  ivaṟṟāl tamiḻ urainaṭaic celvam vaḷarkiṉṟatu eṉṟu colla vēṇṭum.  iraṇṭāvatu vakaiyil ceytittāḷkaḷ, vāra, piṟai, tiṅkaḷ, āṇṭu itaḻkaḷaik kuṟippiṭalām.  ceytittāḷ āciriyarkaḷ, pēcuvatupōlavē eḻutavēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇattāl eḻutukiṉṟaṉar.  mutalvakai ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kaṟṟavarkaḷiṭaiyēyum, iraṇṭāvatu vakai ellōriṭamum celvākkup peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  pāmaramakkaḷum ciṟuvar ciṟumikaḷum iraṇṭāvatu vakai ilakkiyaṅkaḷaip peritum virumpukiṉṟaṉar.  inta iruvakai ilakkiyaṅkaḷum ēṟkappeṟṟālum peṟāviṭṭālum paḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷ avaṟṟaip paṭittu maṉappaciyait taṇittuk koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.  nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷāka makkaḷ etirnōkkiyirunta vēkamāṉa naṭai ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ itayaṅkaḷaik kauvi niṟkiṉṟatu; vaḷarntavar kaḷum an naṭaiyil viruppaṅ koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.  anta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ cintaikkuriya peruviruntāka uḷḷaṉa.  pāṭanūlkaḷ, tuṇaippāṭa nūlkaḷ, nūlakap paṭippiṟkuriya nūlkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟai virumpāta māṇākkarkaḷum innūlkaḷaip peru viruppuṭaṉ paṭittu iṉpa muṟukiṉṟaṉar.
maṟumalarcci ilakkiyaṅkaḷil mukkiyamāṉavai putiṉaṅkaḷ, ciṟukataikaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷ, nāṭakaṅkaḷ, tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷ ākiyavaiyākum.  ivai ovvoṉṟaiyumpaṟṟi īṇṭu oru ciṟitu nōkkuvōm.
putiṉaṅkaḷ: putiṉam mēṉāṭṭu ilakkiya vakaikaḷil oṉṟu.  taṉṉuṭaiya ōviyattait tāṉē pārttu makiḻum iyalpu maṉitaṉukku uṇṭu.  taṉ iyaṟkai, tāṉ vāḻum vāḻkkai ivaikaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷai maṉitaṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kaṇṭu tuykka virumpukiṉṟāṉ.  eṉavē, makkaḷ maṉattil tōṉṟum uṇarccikaḷ pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaiyē ip putiṉaṅkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷāka amaikiṉṟaṉa; avai tōṉṟum nilaikkaḷaṉkaḷākavum uḷḷaṉa.  putiṉam oru periya katai.  oruvarukkoruvar toṭarpuḷḷa pala katai māntarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyil oru perum pakutiyai muḻu vivaraṅkaḷuṭaṉ tarum ōviyam itu.  putiṉattiṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta cila uṟuppukkaḷ uḷa. kataiyamaippu (Plot), katai māntariṉ paṭaippu (Characterisation), uraiyāṭal (Dialogue), kālam (Time) iṭam (Place of action), vāḻkkait tattuvam (Pilosophy of life) ākiyavaiyē ivvuṟuppukkaḷākum.  ivai aṉaittum oru putiṉattil orē aḷavākavō, aṉaittumō irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟa varaiyaṟai illai.  avai aṉaittum irukkalām; ovvoṉṟum mikkum kuṟaintum varalām.  ivaṟṟiṉ virivai mēṉāṭṭut tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷil virivākak kāṇalām.  haṭcaṉ eḻutiya ilakkiya ārāycci nūlil icceytikaḷaik kaṇṭukoḷka.5
tamiḻnāṭṭu vāḻkkaiyaippaṟṟiya ciṟanta putiṉaṅkaḷ iṉṉum atikamākat tōṉṟavillai.  āṉāl, orucila eḻuttāḷarkaḷ tamiḻ moḻikku nalla putiṉaṅkaḷait tantirukkiṉṟaṉar.  vaṅkāḷam, kucarātti, marātti, inti ākiya moḻikaḷiliruntu pala putiṉaṅkaḷ tamiḻil moḻi peyarkkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  mūla nūlkaḷ yāvum nalla ilakkiyap paṇpukaḷuṭaṉum, cuvaiyuṭaṉum, katai vaḷattuṭaṉum ilaṅkuvataip pōlavē moḻipeyarppu nūlkaḷum nalla muṟaiyil ākkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
mēl nāṭṭil putiṉaṅkaḷ oru ciṟanta ilakkiya nilaikku uyarntiruppatupōla nam nāṭṭil uyaravillai; kāraṇam, avarkaḷ moḻiyil karuttaic celuttiyiruppatupōla nammavarkaḷ celuttavillai.  tāmahārṭi, kēṉaṉṭāyil, ec. ji. vel pōṉṟa putiṉa āciriyarkaḷ tām eḻutum poḻutu vaḻukkaḷaṟṟa naṭaiyai mēṟkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.  ākavē, avai palkalaik kaḻaka māṇākkarkaḷukkup pāṭamāka amaiyum perumaiyaiyum peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  nam nāṭṭup putiṉa āciriyarkaḷum tām eḻutum moḻiyil karuttaic celuttip palavita vaḻukkaḷai nīkki eḻutiṉāl avaiyum ciṟanta nilaikku uyarum.  nalla muṟaiyil amainta oru cila putiṉaṅkaḷ (e-ṭu. ṭākṭar mu. va. eḻutiyavai) tamiḻil illāmal illai.  avaṟṟait tērnteṭuttu māṇākkarkaḷaip paṭikkumpaṭiyāka āciriyar tūṇṭalām.
ciṟu kataikaḷ:  ciṟu katai oru taṉippaṭṭa ilakkiya vakai.  ciṟu kataikaḷ ellām ōviyaṅkaḷ.  avaikaḷaip paṭaippataṟkuk kalaittiṟaṉ vēṇṭum.  avaṟṟaip paṭippōriṉ uṇarcci uccanilaikkup pōkavum kūṭum; cilacamayam avai kavitaip pāṉmaikkum uyaralām.  appaṭi uyarvatu mēkattiṉ naṭuvil miṉveṭṭut tōṉṟuvatu pōlirukkum.  nīṇṭa varuṇaṉaikaḷ, periya katai māntar mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟu kataiyil iṭam illai.  aṭakkattuṭaṉ amaikkap peṟṟa oru cila coṟṟoṭarkaḷ uṇarcciyiṉ aṉalaikkāṭṭum.
āṅkila eḻuttāḷarāṉa rāparṭ lūyi ṭīveṉcaṉ eṉpār ciṟu katai eḻuttāḷarkaḷukku oru yōcaṉai kūṟukiṉṟār:  eṉakkut terintavaraiyilum ciṟu katai eḻutuvataṟku mūṉṟu vaḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  oru nikaḻcciyait tērnteṭuttu ataṟkuriya katai māntarkaḷaip paṭaikkalām; allatu oru katai māntarait tērnteṭuttu ataṉmītu nikaḻccikaḷaip poruttalām; allatu oru cūḻnilaiyait tērnteṭuttu ataṟkup poruttamāṉa nikaḻccikaḷaiyum katai māntarkaḷaiyum tērntu amaikkalām.  ivar kūṟum yōcaṉai putiya ciṟu katai eḻuttāḷarkaḷukku oru ciṟanta vaḻikāṭṭiyāka irukkum eṉpataṟku aiyamillai.
tamiḻnāṭṭil ciṟu katai vaḷarccikku mutaṉ mutalil vaḻikāṭṭiyavar kālañceṉṟa va. ve. cu. ayyar avarkaḷ.  avaraip piṉpaṟṟi ērāḷamāṉa ciṟukatai eḻuttāḷarkaḷ tōṉṟi eṇṇaṟṟa ciṟukataic celvaṅkaḷait tamiḻukku aḷittuḷḷaṉar.  ivaṟṟuḷ pala taḻuvalkaḷākavum moḻi peyarppākavum piṟantavai.  putumaippittaṉ, ku. pa. irāja kōpālaṉ pōṉṟa oru cila ciṟu katai eḻuttāḷarkaḷ cāvā ilakkiyattaṉmaiyai peṟṟuḷḷa cila ciṟu kataic celvaṅkaḷai iyaṟṟiyuḷḷaṉar.  putiṉak kalaiyaiviṭac ciṟu kataik kalai tamiḻil nalla vaḷarcci peṟṟuḷḷatu.
kaṭṭuraikaḷ:  kaṭṭurai (Essay) eṉpatu oru poruḷaip paṟṟip pala karuttukkaḷai icaittuk kōvaipaṭa varaiyum coṟperukku eṉpatākum.  maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ itaik kōppurai eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar.  ituvum mēṉāṭṭu ilakkiya vakaikaḷil oṉṟākum.  urainaṭai naṉṟāka vaḷarāta tamiḻ moḻiyil kaṭṭurai ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mēṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭupavaipōl avvaḷavu atikamāka illai.  iṉṟu tōṉṟiyuḷḷa oru cila kaṭṭuraikaḷum ellōrum paṭikkum nilaiyil amaiyavillai; aṟiñarkaḷum pulavarkaḷum paṭikkat takkavāṟutāṉ avai amaintuḷḷaṉa.
āṅkila moḻiyil evarum paṭittuc cuvaikkakkūṭiya muṟaiyil ērāḷamāṉa kaṭṭurai ilakkiyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.  palvēṟu poruḷpaṟṟiya kaṭṭuraikaḷai aṅku ērāḷamākak kāṇalām.  āṅkilak kalvi peṟṟa oru cila tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ āṅkila marapai yoṭṭi oru cila kaṭṭuraikaḷai varaintuḷḷaṉar.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka makiḻnaṉ6 eḻutiyuḷḷa pala kaṭṭuraikaḷaik kuṟippiṭalām.  kaṭalum kaṭalaiyum arañceyya virumpu yatārttavāti lōka virōti maṅkai paṅkaṉ vaṇṇārap periyāṇṭi pōṉṟa kaṭṭuraikaḷ paṭikkap paṭikka iṉpañ curappavai; paṭippavar vāyil tēṉūṟṟait tarum pāṉmai yuṭaiyavai; ilakkiya maṇamum nakaiccuvaiyum tatumpi niṟpavai.  ‘fš»’, vÞ.É.É., ‘ehnlho’, t.uh., â.ja.u., ».t.jafªehj‹ pōṉṟa maṟumalarcci eḻuttāḷarkaḷum pala nalla kaṭṭuraikaḷait tōṟṟuvittuḷḷaṉar.  avarkaḷ aṭipaṟṟi iṉṟu ettaṉaiyō kaṭṭuraikaḷ veḷivantavaṇṇa mirukkiṉṟaṉa.
nāṭakaṅkaḷ:  oru kataiyai eṭuttukkoṇṭu atil varum katai māntarkaṭkellām naṭikarkaḷai amaittu uraiyāṭal kaḷālum ceyalkaḷālum akkataip poruḷaip palar kāṇa araṅkattil naṭittukkāṭṭuvatai nāṭakam eṉṟu ikkālattil nām kūṟukiṉṟōm.  muttamiḻil oṉṟākiya nāṭakat tamiḻ paṟṟiya paṇṭaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyil iṉṟu kāṇappeṟavillai; avai eppaṭiyō aḻintupaṭṭaṉa.  nāṭakam eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar puṉainturaivakai eṉṟu poruḷ virittaṉar.7  iḷampūraṇarum ip poruḷi ṉaiyē piṟitoru vakaiyāl kūṟiṉar.  āṅkilattil paṭittaṟkum naṭittaṟkum uriya pala nāṭaka nūlkaḷ iruppatu pōl tamiḻ moḻiyil iṉṉum atikamākat tōṉṟavillai.  iṉṟu maṟumalarcci eḻuttāḷarkaḷ eḻutivarum nāṭakaṅkaḷ nilaittu niṟkum taṉmai vāyntavai aṉṟu.
iṭaikkālattil kōvilkaḷ. nāṭakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟu vataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntaṉa.  mutal irājairājaṉ kālattil irāja rājēcuvara nāṭakam eṉṟa peyarāl āṇṭutōṟum oru nāṭakam naṭikkappeṟṟu vantatākak kalveṭṭu oṉṟāl aṟikiṉṟōm.8  ittakaiya oru cila ceytikaḷāl ki. pi. pattām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil tamiḻ nāṭakaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatu aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.  mēṟ kūṟiyavāṟu cila nāṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉum ivvakai nūlkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil ceḻittu vaḷaravillai.  pāṭṭiyal nūlkaḷilum uḻattippāṭṭu (paḷḷu), kuṟattippāṭṭu (kuṟavañci), noṇṭi nāṭakam eṉṟa mūṉṟu tavira vēṟu nāṭaka nūlkaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai.  ivai ki. pi. 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiyavai.
pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil mēṉāṭṭuk kalvi nāṭṭil paravat toṭaṅkiyatu.  āṅkilam kaṟṟa oru cilar vaṭamoḻi nāṭaka ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyum muṟaikaḷaiyum piṉpaṟṟi nāṭakaṅkaḷai iyaṟṟiṉar.  ivarkaḷil talai ciṟantavar paritimāṟ kalaiñar eṉṟa vi. kō. cūriyanārāyaṇa cātiriyār āvār.  avar iyaṟṟiya māṉa vijayam uyarnta centamiḻ naṭaiyil amaintuḷḷatu.  vēṟu cilar vaṭamoḻi nāṭakaṅkaḷait taḻuvuvatil viruppamiṉṟi ṣēkpiyar, rāsīṉ, mōliyar mutaliya mēṉāṭṭu nāṭaka āciriyarkaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi nāṭakam iyaṟṟiṉar.  avarkaḷuḷ pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷai talaiciṟantavar.  avar iyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇīyam nāṭakaṅkaḷil taṉicciṟappuṭaiyatāka eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavak kūṭiyatu.
paḻantamiḻ ilakkiya nāṭaṅkaḷ illai eṉṟālum, iṉṟaiyat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil nāṭakaṅkaḷ eṉṟu karutat takkavai cila uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟuḷ ilakkiya maṇam evvaḷavu uḷḷatu eṉpatai ārāyntutāṉ muṭivu kaṭṭavēṇṭum.  kāla veḷḷattai etirttu niṟkum āṟṟalaiyuṭaiyavai ettaṉai eṉpatu kālap pōkkiltāṉ teriyavarum.  taṟkālattu nāṭakaṅkaḷ illāta kuṟaiyai nīkkiyavar tiru.  pammal campanta mutaliyār.  avar tamiḻil iyaṟṟiya nāṭakaṅkaḷil perum pālāṉavai eḷiya urainaṭaiyil uḷḷaṉa.  avar naṭikarākavum uḷḷamaiyāl nāṭakaṅkaḷait tiṟaṉāyntuṇarum āṟṟal peṟṟavarākavum tikaḻkiṉṟār.  iṉṟu maṟumalarcci eḻuttāḷarkaḷ pala nāṭakaṅkaḷai eḻuti varukiṉṟaṉar.
mēṉāṭṭut tiṟaṉāyvāḷarkaḷ oru nalla nāṭakattil toṭakkam, kuḻappam, uccanilai, vīḻcci, muṭivu eṉṟa nilaikaḷ iruppatākac kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  iṅṅaṉam aintu pirivukaḷāka oru nāṭakam amaiyavēṇṭum eṉṟa nipantaṉai illai.  tiṟaṉāyvu ceyvataṟkākavē ivvāṟu pirittuk kūṟappaṭṭatu.  ciṟanta nāṭakaṅkaḷil muraṇ eṅku toṭaṅkukiṉṟatō aṅkutāṉ cūḻcciyum toṭaṅkum.  muraṇ muṭiyum iṭattil cūḻcciyum muṭintuviṭum.  ivviraṇṭu maiyaṅkaḷiṉ iṭaiyiltāṉ kataiyiṉ ciṟanta pakuti amaintirukkum.  pōrāṭṭattiṉ toṭakkam, naṭappu, muṭivu eṉpaṉa itaṉuḷ amaintuviṭum.  etirmāṟāṉa karuttukkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu porumpoḻutu kuḻappam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.  ik kuḻappam vaḷarntukoṇṭē ceṉṟu ōr uccanilaiyai aṭaikiṉṟatu.  annilaiyiṉ piṉṉar muṭivu veḷippaṭat toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu.  immuṭivu naṉmaikkuc cātakamākavō aṉṟit tīmaikkuc cātakamākavō irukkalām.  uccanilai ēṟpaṭṭa piṟaku nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷ aṉaittum varappōkum muṭivukku araṇ ceyvaṉavākavē irukkum.  ēṟattāḻa oru paṭampōṭṭu viḷakkakkūṭiya muṟaiyil ivai amaiya vēṇṭum.
mēṟkūṟiya kūṟukaḷ yāvum niṟainta nāṭaka nūlkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyil mikavum arukiyē kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  āṉāl, aṉṟāṭam veḷivarum paruvaveḷiyīṭukaḷil kāṇap peṟum orucila nalla nāṭakaṅkaḷaip paṭikkumāṟum, aṭikkaṭi vāṉoli nilaiyaṅkaḷiliruntu oli parappappeṟum orucila uyarnta nāṭakaṅkaḷaik kēṭkumpaṭiyum māṇākkarkaḷait tūṇṭalām.
tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷ:  tamiḻ ilakkiyattukku ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvut tuṟai putitu.  eṉavē, tamiḻmoḻiyil tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷē illai eṉṟu tuṇivākak kūṟi viṭalām.  ilakki yattiṉ aṭippaṭaiyāṉa uṇmaikaḷai ārāyntu atu maṉitaṉukku iṉpam koṭukkum irakaciyattai aṟivatu uṇmaiyāṉa ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvākum.  kamparāmāyaṇa racaṉai eṉṟa talaippil va.  ve.  cu. aiyarāl eḻutappeṟṟu muṟṟuppeṟāta kaṭṭuraiyē tamiḻmoḻiyil mutalil tōṉṟiya tiṟaṉāyvu nūlākak karutalām.  atil avar kamparāmāyaṇattiṉ kāviya amaippaippaṟṟi nuṇṇiya aṟivuṭaṉum, cuvait taṉmaiyuṭaṉum viḷakkiyuḷḷār.  kampaṉai vāṉmīki, hōmar mutaliya peruṅkaviñarkaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭṭu pala kūṟukaḷil kampaṉ avarkaḷai veṉṟuḷḷāṉ eṉṟu kāṭṭum kalaittiṟaṉ pōṟṟuvataṟkuriyatu.  avar ceytuḷḷa ārāycci putumaiyākavum taṟkāla muṟaikaḷait taḻuviyum uḷḷatu.
ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvu eṉpatu oru ilakkiyattiṉ uṇmaiyāṉa kuṇaṅkaḷai ārāyntu muṭivu kāṇutalumākum.  eṉavē, ilakkiyattiṉ ciṟanta paṇpukaḷ evai eṉpataiyum, nūlil nuvalappeṟum poruḷ, amaippu, collum muṟai, nūlāciriyaṉatu kaṟpaṉai āṟṟal, uṇarccikaḷ, itayat tuṭippukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum, avai oṉṟōṭoṉṟu koṇṭuḷḷa toṭarpukaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum aṟitalumē uṇmaiyāṉa ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvu ākum.  iṉṟu tamiḻil matippuraikaḷ eṉṟa talaippil veḷiyākum tiṟaṉāyvukaḷ ellām viruppu veṟuppukkaḷukku uṭpaṭṭavai alla eṉṟu colvataṟkillai.  it tiṟaṉāyvukaḷaik koṇṭu nāṭōṟum palkivarum tamiḻ nūlkaḷil nallaṉavaṟṟait tērnteṭukka muṭiyum eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu.  tiṟaṉāyvāḷaṉ nūlai matippiṭuṅkāl taṉatu viruppu veṟuppukkaḷaiyum contak karuttukkaḷaiyum kaṭci vēṟṟumaikaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum koṇṭu avaṟṟai nūlāciriyaṉiṉ karuttukkaḷuṭaṉum koḷkaikaḷuṭaṉum oppiṭṭō maṟuttō nūlkaḷai matippiṭutal kūṭātu.
mēl nāṭukaḷil Literary Criticism eṉṟa peyarāl vaḻaṅkap peṟum nūlkaḷ pōṉṟavai oṉṟiraṇṭu ippoḻututāṉ tamiḻ moḻil tōṉṟat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa.  tamiḻiluḷḷa ovvoru ilakkiyattiṟkum ovvoru tiṟaṉāyvu nūlāvatu uyariya muṟaiyil viraivil tōṉṟavēṇṭum.  ṣēkpiyar nāṭakaṅkaḷukku pirāṭli eḻutiyuḷḷavai pōṉṟa tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷ tamiḻil pala tōṉṟavēṇṭum.  āṅkilam kaṟṟa tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ ittuṟaiyil ākkavēlai ceyya muṉ varutal vēṇṭum.
4. takaval nūlkaḷ
vaḷarntavarkaḷ paṭippataṟkum kuḻantaikaḷ paṭippataṟkum ēṟṟa takaval nūlkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyil iṉṉum atikamākat tōṉṟavillai; intiya moḻikaḷilēyē iṉṉum atikam tōṉṟavillai eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟalām.
piṟanāṭṭu nallaṟiñar cāttiraṅkaḷ
	tamiḻmoḻiyil peyarttal vēṇṭum;
iṟavāta pukaḻuṭaiya putu nūlkaḷ
	tamiḻmoḻiyil iyaṟṟal vēṇṭum.
eṉṟu pāratiyārum mēṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷiliruntu pala nūlkaḷai moḻi peyarkkavēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.  oru cila nalla nūlkaḷ ippoḻututāṉ tōṉṟak toṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa.  paḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷukkeṉa avarkaḷiṉ paruva nilaikkēṟṟavāṟu pautika iyal, uyiriyal, tāvara iyal, vētiyiyal, vāṉiyal, nilaviyal, nilauṭkūṟṟiyal, uṭaliyal pōṉṟa tuṟaikaḷil Basic Science series, Wonder book series eṉṟa toṭarkaḷil iṉiya, eḷiya, teḷivāṉa āṅkila moḻiyil eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷa nūlkaḷaip
pōl tamiḻ moḻiyil pala nūlkaḷ tōṉṟutal vēṇṭum.  iṉṉum āṅkila moḻiyil veḷiyākiyuḷḷa palvēṟu takaval nūlkaḷaippōl tamiḻ moḻiyilum veḷivarutal vēṇṭum.  kalaik kaḷañciyam eṉṟa talaippil tamiḻil veḷivantuḷḷa nūl arumaiyāṉa takavalnūl; uyariya muṟaiyil mēṟkōḷ nūlākat tuṇaiceyvatu.  tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakam ceytuḷḷa iccīriya paṇi tamiḻ makkaḷāl eṉṟeṉṟum pārāṭṭutaṟkuriyatu.
iṉṟu tamiḻ makkaḷiṭam takavalkaḷait tarum nūlkaḷākap paḻakkattiliruppavai pañcāṅkam, pukaivaṇṭik kāla aṭṭavaṇai, orucila tamiḻ akarātikaḷ, tamiḻ leksikaṉ, apitāṉa cintāmaṇi ākiyavaiyē.  akarātikaḷum tēvaikkēṟṟavāṟu iṉṉum atikamākat tōṉṟavillai.  tāymoḻiyil varalāṟu, camūkam, tarainūl, kaṇitam mutaliya pāṭaṅkaḷaip payiṉṟu uyarnilaik kalviyai muṭittukkoṇṭa māṇākkarkaḷ paḷḷikaḷai viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiyatum mēlum at tuṟaikaḷil aṟivai vaḷarttukkoḷḷa virumpiṉāl tāymoḻiyil attuṟaikaḷil takka nūlkaḷ illai.  kiṭaikkum oṉṟiraṇṭu nūlkaḷum nalla naṭaiyil uṟcākam ūṭṭum muṟaiyil eḻutap peṟavillai.  kalvi muṭintatum tamiḻil eḻutappeṟṟa it tuṟai nūlkaḷai māṇākkarkaḷ viruppattuṭaṉ pārppatē illai.  pala tuṟaikaḷil evarum virumpip paṭikkum muṟaiyil nalla naṭaiyil ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷ iṉit tōṉṟutal vēṇṭum.  tāymoḻiyil nalla takaval nūlkaḷ illai eṉpatai uyarnilaik kalvi aṟikkaiyum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷatu.9  aṟiñarkaḷ at tuṟaikaḷil ākka vēlaikaḷ ceyya vēṇṭum; patippāḷarkaḷ avaikaḷai aḻakiya muṟaiyil acciṭa muṉ varavēṇṭum.  necavut toḻil, maravēlai, ulōka vēlai, piṉṉal vēlai, tōṭṭa vēlai pōṉṟa viruppac ceyalkaḷ (Hobbies) paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyil iṉṉum tōṉṟa villai.  vārtā kalvit tiṭṭam ōriraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷil ceyaṟ paṭṭiruppatālum, aṭippaṭaik kalvittiṭṭam naṭaimuṟaikku vantuviṭṭatālum, kuṭicait toḻil vaḷarcciyaippaṟṟip palar akkaṟai koḷvatālum tēṉī vaḷarttal, kākitam ceytal, cōppu ceytal pōṉṟa toḻilkaḷaippaṟṟiya oṉṟiraṇṭu nūlkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyil veḷivantuḷḷaṉa.  ivai eḷiyamuṟaiyil eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ip puttakaṅkaḷai toṭakkam mutal iṟutivarai paṭikka vēṇṭiyatillai.  tēvaik kēṟṟavāṟu cantarppam nēriṭumpoḻutu cila pakutikaḷaip paṭittukkoṇṭāl pōtumāṉatu.
5. payiṟcik kuṟippēṭukaḷ
tāymoḻiyil pala payiṟcik kuṟippēṭukaḷ veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa.  kaṇitam, camūka iyal, tarai iyal pōṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷil pala payiṟcik kuṟippēṭukaḷ veḷivantiruppataik kāṇkiṉṟōm.  mēlnāṭukaḷil ippāṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa payiṟcikaḷ evvaḷavō muṉṉēṟṟam aṭaintuḷḷaṉa.  āṉāl, nam nāṭṭil orucila āciriyarkaḷāl eḻutappeṟṟavaiyē pala patippukaḷil veḷiyāki varukiṉṟaṉa; putitāka oruvarum ittuṟaikaḷil eḻutavillai.  tamiḻppayiṟcikkeṉa ākpōrṭu yūṉivarciṭi pirasārāl putumuṟait tamiḻ appiyācaṅkaḷ eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, vakuppukaḷukku oru varicai ēṭukaḷ veḷiyiṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ivaṟṟait tavira vēṟu payiṟcik kuṟippēṭukaḷ itukāṟum veḷivantatākat teriyavillai.  āṅkila moḻip payiṟcikkeṉa `cīṭ vork, `ṭek vork, piṟapayiṟcikaḷ eṉṟu veḷi vantuḷḷa moḻip payiṟcik kuṟippēṭukaḷ pōṉṟavai tamiḻmoḻiyil veḷi varutal vēṇṭum.  cila āṇṭukaḷāka kuḻantaikaḷukkeṉa veḷivarum paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷilum, piṟa veḷiyīṭukaḷil ciṟuvarkkeṉa otukkappeṟṟuḷḷa pakutiyilum viṭukataikaḷ pōṉṟa orucila moḻip payiṟcikaḷ veḷiyiṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  avai poḻutupōkkukkākavum, mutalil pārppavarkaṭkuc ciṟitu taṭumāṟṟattai uṇṭupaṇvataṟkākavum utavupavai.  oru cila kuṟukkuc coṟpōṭṭikaḷ ittakaiyavai.
itukāṟum ilakkaṇap pāṭanūlkaḷil kāṇappeṟum orucila payiṟcikaḷ moḻip payiṟcikaḷākak karutappeṟṟaṉa.  ilakkaṇa vitikaḷaiyum nūṟpākkāḷaiyum maṉappāṭam ceytavaṉālum orucila ilakkaṇap payiṟcikaḷaic ceyva taṉālum moḻip payiṟci tāṉāka ēṟpaṭum eṉṟa tavaṟāṉa eṇṇam nilaviyatu.  āṉāl, ilakkaṇap payiṟci vēṟu, moḻip payiṟci vēṟu eṉpatai mika aṇmaiyil tāṉ kalviyāḷarkaḷ uṇarntaṉar.  poruḷaṟivuṭaṉ ceyyum payiṟcikaḷum, palvēṟu moḻi vaṭivaṅkaḷil ceyyum payiṟcikaḷum āṅkila moḻiyiluḷḷavaipōl tamiḻilum ceyyap peṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṟa iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyai uṇarntaṉar.10  iṉṉum ammuṟaiyil pala nūlkaḷ veḷi varavillai.  paḷḷip pāṭat tiṭṭattaiyoṭṭi veḷivantuḷḷa nūlkaḷait tavira uyartarak kaṭṭurai ilakkaṇam eṉṟa talaippil oru nūl iraṇṭu pakutikaḷāka veḷivantuḷḷatu.11 atu reṉṉum māriṭṭiṉum (Wren and Martin) iṇaintiyaṟṟiya āṅkila ilakkaṇa nūlait taḻuvi eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  itupōṉṟa pala nūl tamiḻ moḻiyil veḷivarutal vēṇṭum.  tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ it tuṟaiyil āṟṟa vēṇṭiya paṇi mikkuḷḷatu.
pāṭanūlkaḷait tavira ēṉaiya nūlkaḷ paḷḷiyil ōr oḻuṅkil cēmittu vaikkap peṟṟirukkum iṭam paḷḷi nūlakamākum.  iṉṟu nāṭōṟum veḷivarum ceytit tāḷkaḷ, paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷ mutaliyaṉavaṟṟaip paṭikka vacatiyāka amaikkappeṟṟuḷḷa maṟṟōr iṭam nūlakattuṭaṉ iṇaintuḷḷa paṭippakamākum.  paḷḷikaḷil ivai iraṇṭum evvāṟu ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟatu? moḻip paṭippiṟku ivai evvāṟu tuṇaipurikiṉṟaṉa? eṉpaṉavaṟṟaip paṟṟiyum ciṟitu aṟintu koḷḷutalvēṇṭum.  eṉavē, inta iraṇṭu nilaiyaṅkaḷaippaṟṟi ōraḷavu īṇṭu aṟintu koḷḷuvōm.
nūlakam:  nūlakattiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyai iṉṟu makkaḷ uṇarat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉar.  nūlakak kaḻakaṅkaḷ māvaṭṭaṅkaḷtōṟum niṟuvappeṟṟup palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil nūlakaṅkaḷait tōṟṟuvittuḷḷaṉa.
   maṉitarelām aṉpuneṟi kāṇpa taṟkum
maṉōpāvam vāṉaippōl viriva ṭaintu
taṉimaṉita tattuvamām iruḷaip pōkkic
cakamakkaḷ oṉṟeṉpa tuṇarva taṟkum,
iṉitiṉitāy eḻuntauyar eṇṇa mellām
ilakuvatu pulavartaru cuvaṭic cālai;
puṉitamuṟṟu makkaḷputu vāḻvu vēṇṭil
puttakacā laivēṇṭum nāṭṭil yāṇṭum.12
eṉṟa kaviñaṉ kural emmaruṅkum kēṭkap peṟṟuc ceyaṟpaṭat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu.  uyarnilaik kalvi aṟikkaiyum nūlakattiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmai paṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷatu.13  intac cūḻnilaiyil paḷḷikaḷil nūlakam nalla muṟaiyil amaintu māṇākkariṉ moḻiyaṟiviṉaiyum poruḷaṟiviṉaiyum vaḷarkkavēṇṭum.  avvappoḻutu putitāka veḷivarum vāra itaḻkaḷ, piṟai itaḻkaḷ, tiṅkaḷ itaḻkaḷ, āṇṭitaḻkaḷ, vēṟu ciṟappitaḻkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaiyum, tāymoḻiyil palvēṟu tuṟaikaḷil veḷivarum nūlkaḷaiyum māṇākkarkaṭkuk kiṭṭumpaṭi ceytu ivaṟṟai vaḷarkkalām.
nūlakam-vakaikaḷ:  cātāraṇamākap paḷḷi nūlakattil potu nūlakam, mēṟkōḷ nūlakam, vakuppu nūlakam eṉṟu muppirivukaḷ irukkum; ivvāṟu pirikkappaṭṭiruntāltāṉ cīriya muṟaiyil nūlakam ellōrukkum payaṉpaṭum.  potu nūlakattil ellā vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷum, āciriyarkaḷum paṭikkakkūṭiya pala kalaikaḷpaṟṟiya nūlkaḷ irukkum.  mēṟkōḷ nūlakattil potu akarātikaḷ, kalaiccol akarātikaḷ, vāḻkkai varalāṟṟuc curukka akarātikaḷ, tamiḻp pērakarāti, kalaikkaḷañciyaṅkaḷ, apitāṉa cintāmaṇi, nikaṇṭukaḷ, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, purāṇaṅkaḷ, ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ ākiyavai irukkum.  centamiḻc celvi, centamiḻ, tamiḻppoḻil, tamiḻar nēcaṉ, kalaimakaḷ, amutacurapi, kalaikkatir, aṇukkatir, tarumacakkaram mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaḻinta āṇṭut tokutikaḷ iruttal avaciyam.  ciṟuvarkku viruppattait tarum kaṇkavar ōviyaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa aṟivu vaḷarkkum nūlkaḷum, pukaivaṇṭi vaḻi kāṭṭikaḷ, pañcāṅkaṅkaḷ, vēṟu aṭṭavaṇai nūlkaḷ, āṇṭu nūlkaḷ (Year books) ākiyavaṟṟiṟkum iṅku iṭam aḷikkalām.  vakuppu nūlakattil ovvoru vakuppiṟkumuriya nūlkaḷaip pirittut taṉittaṉiyāka vaittalvēṇṭum.  annūlkaḷai māṇākkarkaḷ vāṅkavum tiruppik koṭukkavum ēṟṟa vacatikaḷ iruttalvēṇṭum.  ataṟkeṉa ovvoru vakuppiṟkum pērēṭukaḷ (Catalogues) vaittu avaṟṟil koṭukkal vāṅkalkaḷait tētivāriyākap patintuk koḷḷalām.  mutal iraṇaṭu pirivukaḷum nūlakak kaṇkāṇiyāriṉ pārvai yilum, mūṉṟāvatu pirivu antanta vakuppu āciriyariṉ pārvaiyilum iruntāl ceyal eḷitākavum tiṟamaiyākavum naṭaipeṟum.  māṟṟiyamaikkap peṟṟa uyarnilaik kalvittiṭṭattil tāy moḻip pāṭat tiṭṭattil 1,2,3 paṭivaṅkaḷukku vārattiṟku oru pāṭavēḷaiyum, 4,5,6 paṭivaṅkaḷukku iraṇṭu vāraṅkaḷukku oru pāṭavēḷaiyum otukkappeṟṟiruppatāl vakuppu āciriyarkaḷ vakuppu nūlakattiṉ poṟuppai ēṟṟāltāṉ nūlakappaṭippu tiṟamaiyāka naṭaipeṟa iyalum.
ivviṭattil uyarnilaik kalvi aṟikkai nūlakattaip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭum ceytikaḷai niṉaivukūrtal mikavum poruttamāka irukkum.14  nūlakattiṟkuriya kaṭṭaṭam mika aḻakāṉatāka amaitalvēṇṭum.  kāṟṟōṭṭamuḷḷatākavum pōtumāṉa veḷiccattai yuḷḷatākavum amainta iṭam ēṟṟatu.  nūlakattil pala periyārkaḷ, aṟiñarkaḷiṉ paṭaṅkaḷai vaittal vēṇṭum; aṟaiyil amarntu paṭippataṟ kēṟṟa irukkai vacatikaḷ naṉṟāka amaikkappeṟal vēṇṭum.  iraṇṭāvatāka vēṇṭappaṭupavai nalla nūlkaḷ, paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷ, ārāycci itaḻkaḷ mutaliyavai.  ivaṟṟait tērnteṭukkum poṟuppai oru ciṟiya kuḻu ēṟṟu naṭattiṉāl naṉṟu; kuḻuvil paṇiyāṟṟuvōr paṭippatil ārvamuḷḷa varkaḷākavum, nāḷitaḻkaḷil veḷivarum matippuraikaḷaik kavaṉikkak kūṭiyavarkaḷākavum iruttal vēṇṭum.  mūṉṟāvatāka, nūlkaḷai māṇākkar kaikkuk kiṭṭumpaṭi ceyyum muṟai naṉku amaitalvēṇṭum.  nūlaka muṟaikaḷil naṉku tērccipeṟṟa oruvar pārvaiyil nūlakam amaintu nūlakamuṟaikaḷaip paṟṟic ciṟitu payiṟcipeṟṟa āciriyarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavāl itai naṉṟāka amaikkalām.
vakuppu nūlakattil irukkavēṇṭiya nūlkaḷ:  vakuppu nūlakaṅkaḷukku māṇākkarkaḷiṉ iyalpaiyum cuvaiyaiyum tiṟaṉaiyum aṟintu avaṟṟiṟkēṟṟa, paṭippil ārvam ūṭṭavalla nūl vakaikaḷait tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.  maṉattiṟkuk kavarccitarum nikaḻccikaḷum karuttai īrkkum ceyalkaḷum niṟainta ciṟukatai nūlkaḷ, putiṉaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟait tērn teṭukkalām.  vaḻiccelavukaḷ (Travels) paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ, putiya nāṭukaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭitta varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ cintaikku viruntāka irukkum.  aṟiviyal tuṟaikaḷil putiyatu puṉainta varalāṟṟaik kūṟum kaṇṇaiyum karuttaiyum kavaravalla viḷakkap paṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya nūlkaḷai māṇākkarkaḷ virumpuvar.  eḷiya iṉiya naṭaiyil eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷa nam nāṭṭuk kāṭci kaḷaiyum, ayalnāṭṭuk kāṭcikaḷaiyum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ, palporuḷpaṟṟiyaṉavum palvēṟu naṭaikaḷ koṇṭuḷḷaṉavumāṉa nūlkaḷ, eḻiluṇar āṟṟalai vaḷarkkum pala tiṟappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ, potu aṟivaiyum palatuṟai aṟivaiyum vaḷarkkum nūlkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai āciriyarkaḷ tērnteṭuttalvēṇṭum.  ovvoru nūlilum kuṟaintatu aintu paṭikaḷāvatu irukkumāṟu nūlkaḷai vāṅkavēṇṭum.
nūlkaḷai vāṅkuvatil āciriyariṉ poṟuppu:  vakuppu nūlakattiṟku nūlkaḷait tērnteṭukkum āciriyarkaḷ cila kuṟippukkaḷai niṉaivil vaittukkoṇṭu nūlkaḷait tērnteṭukkavēṇṭum.  ovvoru vakuppukkum uriyaṉavākat tērnteṭukkum nūlkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṉ aṟivunilai, maṉanilai, poruḷaṟivu, maṉappāṉmai ākiyavaṟṟiṟkup porutta māṉavaikaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ ārvattaip piṇaittu iḻuttuc cella valla katainūlkaḷ iṅku iṭampeṟal vēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷ virumpip paṭikkum nūlkaḷai vāṅkavēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, āciriyarkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa-vaṟṟaittāṉ paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu vaṟpuṟutti nūlkaḷai vāṅkutal tavaṟu.  kīḻvakuppukkaḷukkuc ciṟu ciṟu kataikaḷaik koṇṭa nūlkaḷum, mēl vakuppukkaḷukkuc ciṟu kataic celvaṅkaḷum putiṉaṅkaḷum irukkalām.  piñcu maṉaṅkaḷaik kulaikkum nūlkaḷai aṟavē nīkkutal vēṇṭum.  kīḻvakuppukkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ virumpip paṭikkum nūlkaḷai vāṅkiṉālum, mēl vakuppukkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ kaṭṭāyam paṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu āciriyar karutum nūlkaḷai vāṅkalām.  kūṭiyavarai oruvarālēyē eḻutappeṟṟa nūlkaḷāyiruppiṉ naṉṟu; palarāl eḻutappeṟṟirukkum pāṭa nūlkaḷ pōṉṟavai kūṭā.  akaṉṟa paṭippukkuriya innūlkaḷ iṉiya eḷiya tūya naṭaiyiṉaik koṇṭaṉavāyum, eḻuttup piḻaikaḷum ēṉaiya piḻaikaḷumiṉṟi aḻakāṉa kaṇkavar viḷakkappaṭaṅkaḷaik koṇṭiruppaṉavākavum iruttal naṉṟu.  vakuppukkēṟṟa periya eḻuttukkaḷil nalla tāḷil acciṭappeṟṟup puṟa, akat tōṟṟaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu nalla kaṭṭaṭam amainta nūlkaḷāka iruttal avaciyam.  tāmākavē paṭittaṟintōm eṉṉum makiḻcciyai aḷikkattakkaṉavāy, māṇākkarkaḷukkup paṭippatil ārvam eḻuppakkūṭiya poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟuvaṉavāy iruntāl ciṟanta naṉmai uṇṭu.  āciriyarkaḷ ceytit tāḷkaḷil veḷivarum matippuraikaḷaip paṭittu oru taṉik kuṟippēṭṭil kuṟittu vaittukkoḷḷavēṇṭum; āṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil tēvaiyāṉa nūṟpāṭṭiyalai āyattam ceyyumpoḻutu atil ivaṟṟaic cērttalvēṇṭum.  iṉṟu aṟivu vaḷarccipaṟṟiya nūlkaḷ, kalaikaḷpaṟṟiya nūlkaḷ, aṟiviyal tuṟai nūlkaḷ mutaliyavai ettaṉaiyō tāy moḻiyil veḷivantavaṇṇa mirukkiṉṟaṉa.  āciriyarkaḷ avaṟṟaip paṭittup paricīlaṉai ceytu ēṟṟavaṟṟai vāṅka vēṇṭiya paṭṭiyalil cērkkavēṇṭum.
paṭippil ārvattai ūṭṭum muṟai:  paḷḷip pāṭa vēḷait tiṭṭattil nūlakappaṭippiṟkāka otukkappeṟṟuḷḷa nērattilum piṟa camayaṅkaḷilum āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkar kaḷiṭaiyē paṭikkum ārvattai ūṭṭutalai mutaṟ kaṭamaiyākak koḷḷavēṇṭum; mutalāya kaṭamaiyum atutāṉ.  perumpālum āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṭam avarkaḷ paṭikkum nūlkaḷil kāṇakkiṭakkum mukkiyamāṉa coṟṟoṭarkaḷaiyum, (Phrase) maraput toṭarkaḷaiyum puttakattil paṭikkum poruḷkaḷaiyum paṟṟic curukkamāka eḻutumpaṭi colli avarkaḷiṭam iyalpākat tōṉṟum paṭikkum ārvattai vērilēyē citaittu viṭukiṉṟaṉar.  jārj sāmsaṉ eṉṟa aṟiñar, ilakkiya maṅkai paḷḷi āciriyarkaḷiṉ kaiyilum tērvāḷarkaḷiṉ kaiyilum cikkuvatu avaḷatu pōtāk kālamē; avaḷatu aḻakaiyum polivaiyum viṭa avaḷ tarum cītaṉattaiyē mukkiyamākak karutum pēr vaḻikaḷ avarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟic ceṉṟatai āciriyarkaḷ cintittup pārkkavēṇṭum.15  pala nūlkaḷaip paṭittu māṇākkarkaḷ paṭippatil oru cuvaiyaiyum iṉṟiyamai yāmaiyaiyum tammiṭaiyē vaḷarttuk-koḷḷukiṟavarai āciriyarkaḷ immātiriyāṉa vēlaikaḷai avarkaḷukkiṭak kūṭātu.  avai vaḷarntāl ittakaiya vēlaikaḷai āciriyarkaḷ avarkaḷukkut taravēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyum illai.  avarkaḷukkē eṉṉa ceyyavēṇṭum eṉpatu tāṉākap pulappaṭum.  inta uṇmaiyai aṟintu āciriyarkaḷ ēṟṟavaṟṟaic ceyyavēṇṭum.
nūlakap paṭippai mēṟkoḷḷum muṟai:  ivviṭattil nūlakap pāṭa vēḷaiyil tammiṭam varum māṇākkarkaḷukku āciriyar evvāṟu an nērattaip payaṉ peṟum vakaiyil celaviṭa vaḻikāṭṭavēṇṭum eṉpataippaṟṟi oru ciṟitu kavaṉittal poruttamē.  payiṟṟu muṟaikaḷai ārāynta poḻutu ‘mēṟ pārvaip paṭippu’ eṉṟa oru muṟaiyaik kuṟippiṭṭōm aṉṟō?16  āṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa cila ciṟanta kūṟukaḷaik kaiyāṇṭu nūlakap paṭippaip payaṉuṟumpaṭi ceyyalām.  avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟai īṇṭu eṭuttuk kūṟuvōm.
innūlaka vakuppil āciriyarkaḷ tērnteṭukkappeṟṟa nūlkaḷai māṇākkarkaḷiṭam eṭuttuk koṭuttu avarkaḷai vāykkuṭ paṭikkumāṟu ceyyavēṇṭum.  oru nūlukkup pala paṭikaḷ irukkumāyiṉ vakuppaic ciṟuciṟu pirivukaḷākap pirittu oru pirivukku oru nūlaiyum, maṟṟoru pirivukku maṟṟoru nūlaiyum koṭuttup piṉṉar avaṟṟai māṟṟik koḷḷumpaṭi ceyyalām.  kīḻ vakuppukkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ virumpum nūlkaḷaik koṭukkavēṇṭu mēyaṉṟi cila nūlkaḷaik koṭuttu avaṟṟaittāṉ paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttutal paṭippatil uḷḷa cuvaiyaiyē citaittuviṭum.  mēl vakuppukkaḷil āciriyarkaḷ, māṇākkarkaḷ paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu tām karutum nūlkaḷaip paṭikkumāṟu tūṇṭalām.
potu vakuppil kaṟpikkumpoḻutu iṉṉa ceytikaḷai iṉṉa mēṟkōḷ nūlil kāṇalām eṉṟu kūṟiyiruntāl anta nūlil ceytikaḷaik kāṇumpaṭi vaḻi kāṭṭalām.  nūlakattil nūlkaḷaip paṭikkumpoḻutu viḷaṅkāta ceytikaḷ, poruḷ teriyāta coṟkaḷ ākiyavaikaḷai mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷ, akarāti nūlkaḷ pōṉṟavaikaḷaip puraṭṭi aṟintu koḷḷum muṟaikaḷaiyum kāṭṭalām.  ippaṭi vaḻikaḷaik kāṭṭi akaṉṟa paṭippup paḻakkattai naṉku uṇṭākki viṭṭāl atu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai muḻuvatum nilaipeṟa iṭamuṇṭu.  itaṟkut tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ naṉku kaṟṟu aṟintavarkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum.
intap pāṭa vēḷaiyil caṟṟup piṟpōkkāṉa māṇākkar kaḷai naṉku kavaṉikkalām.  aṭikkaṭi avarkaḷiṭam ceṉṟu aṉpuṭaṉ naṇpaṉpōl paḻaki avarkaḷ aiyaṅkaḷai akaṟṟa vēṇṭum.  mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷai evvāṟu kaiyāṇṭu teriyātaṉavaṟṟait terintukoḷḷalām eṉpatai avarkaḷukku naṉku viḷakkavēṇṭum.  paṭippu viraivākac ceṉṟāltāṉ atil paṟṟu ēṟpaṭṭup pala cuvaikaḷaik kāṇalām eṉṟum vaṟpuṟuttutalvēṇṭum.  pala vaṇṇap paṭaṅkaḷ, viḷakkap paṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟait tāṅkiyaṉavum, eḷiya iṉiya naṭaiyil eḻutap peṟṟaṉavum, periya eḻuttukkaḷil acciṭap peṟṟaṉavumāṉa nūlkaḷaikkoṇṭu im māṇākkarkaḷukkup paṭippil cuvaiyūṭṭalām.  caṟṟu muṟpōkkāṉa māṇākkarkaḷ ip pāṭavēḷaiyil niṟainta payaṉai aṭaivar; pala nūlkaḷai viraivākap paṭittu aṟivup paciyaiyum pōkkik koḷvar.
āḻnta paṭippai mēṟkoḷḷumpoḻutu ceyyap peṟum payiṟcikaḷaip pōṉṟavaikaḷai iṅku vaṟpuṟuttak kūṭātu.  orōvaḻi putiya coṟṟoṭarkaḷaiyum maraput toṭarkaḷaiyum aruñ coṟkaḷaiyum viḷakkalām.  paṭikkum poḻutu karuttai maṭṭilum uṇarntāl pōtum eṉṟum, katai allatu kaṭṭuraiyiṉ pōkkaiyum toṭarcciyaiyum aṟintu koṇṭāl pōtum eṉṟum, aṭikkaṭi vaṟpuṟuttutal iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.  toṭarntu vāykkuḷḷēyē paṭikkum calippaip pōkka cila camayaṅkaḷil cila nūlkaḷiluḷḷa mukkiya iṭaṅkaḷaiyum, ciṟanta naṭaiyil eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷa pakutikaḷaiyum paṭittukkāṭṭi anta nūlkaḷiṉpāl avarkaḷiṉ kavaṉattai īrkkalām.  itaṉāl palar anta nūlkaḷaip paṭittup payaṉ peṟa vaḻiyuṇṭu.
nāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷ, nāṭōṭip pāṭalkaḷ, taṉippāṭalkaḷ, kummi, cintu, temmāṅku, kuṟavañci, paḷḷu, noṇṭi nāṭakaṅkaḷ, ciṟappup peyar akarātikaḷ, palporuṭ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil māṇākkarkaḷiṉ kavaṉattait tiruppiviṭṭu anta nūlkaḷil cuvaiyūṭṭalām.  ilakkiyam, tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷ, moḻi nūlkaḷ, moḻi varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ, ilakkiya ārāycci nūlkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaip paṭikka cila muṟpōkkāṉa māṇākkarkaḷai ēviviṭalām.  ciṟuvarkkāṉa tiṅkaḷ, piṟai, vāra veḷiyīṭukaḷ, kañcikaikaḷ, viḷakka itaḻkaḷ mutaliyavai kaḷum, kaṇṇaiyum karuttaiyum kavarum vaṇṇap paṭaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa itaḻkaḷum vakuppu nūlakattil vaikkap peṟṟiruntāl, māṇākkarkaḷiṭam paṭippuṇarcci iyalpāka eḻa iṭam uṇṭu.  aṉṟāṭac ceytit tāḷkaḷaiyum iṅku iṭampeṟac ceytu ceytikaḷai aṟintukoḷḷum vāyppiṉaiyum nalkalām.
ovvoru vakuppu māṇākkaṉuṭaiya peyar.  avaṉ eṭukkum nūliṉ peyar, eṭutta tēti, tiruppik koṭutta tēti mutaliya vivaraṅkaḷaṭaṅkiya pativēṭu oṉṟai vaittu avvap poḻutu patintu koḷḷalām.  ovvoru māṇākkaṉukkum ovvoru pakkam viṭṭu eḻutiṉāl avaṉ anta āṇṭil eṉṉeṉṉa nūlkaḷaip paṭittāṉ, avaṉ ettaṉai nūlkaḷaip paṭittāṉ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa ceytikaḷai āciriyar aṟintu koḷḷalām.  mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷait tavira, nūlaka vakuppil paṭittu muṭiyāta nūlkaḷai vīṭṭiṟku eṭuttuc cella icaivum aḷikkalām.
paṭippakam:  iṉṟu tāymoḻiyil ērāḷamāṉa ceytit tāḷkaḷum paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷum nāḷtōṟum veḷi vanta vaṇṇa mirukkiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟuḷ nallaṉavaṟṟait tērnteṭuttu vāṅki māṇākkarkaḷukku avaikaḷaip paṭikkum vāyppiṉai aḷittal vēṇṭum.  itaṟku amaiti nilavakkūṭiya aṟaiyoṉṟu irukkai vacatikaḷuṭaṉ irukkavēṇṭum.  itaṉ poṟuppai nūlakak kaṇkāṇippāḷar ēṟkavēṇṭum.  itil māṇākkarkaḷ amaitiyuṭa ṉiruntu ceytit tāḷkaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum pārkkum paḻakkattai vaḷarttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  paḷḷi māṇākkat talaivaṉum vakuppu māṇākkat talaivarkaḷum nūlakak kaṇkāṇippāḷaruṭaṉ ottuḻaittu ivṟaiyil nalla amaitiyai nilavac ceyyalām.  paṭikkum tāḷkaḷai vaittup pōṟṟum uṇarcci ovvoruvariṭamum vaḷaravēṇṭum.
nūlakattaiyum paṭippakattaiyum ciṟanta muṟaiyil amaittuc ceyaṟpaṭac ceytāl tāymoḻip payiṟciyil avaṟṟait tiṟampaṭavum payaṉuṟavum kaiyāḷalām.  tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ inta iraṇṭu cātaṉaṅkaḷaiyum takka muṟaikaḷil payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷavēṇṭum.  nūlkaḷil mukavuraiyil collakkūṭiya ceytikaḷ, pāṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya āciriyarkkuriya kuṟippukkaḷ, āciriyarkkuriya piṟatēvaikaḷ ākiyavai pāṭanūlkaḷil iṭam peṟutal tavaṟu.  ivaṟṟai āciriyar nūl eṉṟa taṉic ciṟu nūlākavō cuṟṟaṟikkai mūlamākavō veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭum.
kuḻantaikaḷuk keṉavum ciṟuvar ciṟumiyarkaḷukkeṉavum ērāḷamāṉa tamiḻp pattirikaikaḷum paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷum cila āṇṭukaḷāka veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa.  toṭakka nilaip paḷḷikaḷilum oru paṭippakattai ēṟpaṭutti ivai aṉaivarukkum kiṭṭumpaṭi ceytalvēṇṭum.  ivvāṟu ceytāl, ettaṉaiyō ēḻaik kuṭumpaṅkaḷiliruntu varum kuḻantaikaḷ avaṟṟaip paṭittup payaṉaṭaiyak kūṭum.  iṉṟu pala uyarnilaip paḷḷikaḷil peyaraḷavil paṭippakam eṉa oṉṟu uḷḷatu; pala paḷḷikaḷil peyaraḷavilkūṭa paṭippakam illai.  pala kallūrikaḷil innilaiyē nilavukiṉṟatu.  intanilai viraivil māṟa vēṇṭum.
16. kalvittuṟai aḷaviyal
kalvittuṟaic cōtaṉaikaḷ:  kuḻantaikaḷ paḷḷikaḷilum paḷḷikaḷukku veḷiyilum kaṟṟavaṟṟai aḷantaṟiyap payaṉpaṭuttum cōtaṉaikaḷaik kalvittuṟaic cōtaṉaikaḷ (Educational tests) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭalām.  āciriyarkaḷ kaṟpittalāl kuḻantaikaḷ aṟintu koṇṭavaṟṟaiyum, vakuppaṟai, paḷḷi aṉupavaṅkaḷāl aṟintu koṇṭa vaṟṟaiyum, paḷḷikku veḷiyil peṟum aṉupavaṅkaḷāl aṟintu koṇṭavaṟṟaiyum taṉittaṉiyākap pirittu aṟivatum ovvoru vakaiyilum aṟintukoṇṭatu evvaḷavu eṉpataik kaṇakkiṭṭu aṟivatum iyalāta ceyal.  eṉavē, ellāt tuṟaikaḷilum kaṟṟavaṟṟai oru cērattāṉ aṟintukoḷḷa iyalum.  cātāraṇamākak kalvittuṟaiyil mūvakaic cōtaṉaikaḷ naṭaimuṟaiyiluḷḷaṉa.  avai:  (1) vāymoḻic cōtaṉaikaḷ; (2) kaṭṭuraic cōtaṉaikaḷ; (3) puṟavayat tērvukaḷ (Objective examinations) mūṉṟāvatu vakaiyil irupirivukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  oṉṟu, tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉaikaḷ piṟitoṉṟu, tarappaṭuttāta cōtaṉaikaḷ allatu putu muṟaic cōtaṉaikaḷ.  ivai ovvoṉṟaiyumpaṟṟi oru ciṟitu virivāka ārāyvōm.
1. vāymoḻic cōtaṉaikaḷ
māṇākkarkaḷ kaṟṟavaṟṟai aḷantaṟivataṟkuc cātāraṇamāka vāymoḻic cōtaṉaikaḷ mēṟkoḷḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  āṉāl, ivaṟṟāl aḷantaṟiyum muṭivukaḷaic cariyāṉavai eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu.  vakuppiṟku aintāṟu māṇākkarkaḷ iruntāl ivaṟṟaik kaiyāḷa muṭiyum.  iṉṟuḷḷatupōl vakuppiṟku nāṟpatu allatu aimpatu māṇākkarkaḷiruntāl vāymoḻic cōtaṉaikaḷaik kuṟukiya kālattil evvāṟu veṟṟikaramākak kaiyāḷa muṭiyum? māṇākkarkaḷ kūṟum viṭaikaḷaiyum avacara avacaramāka matippiṭa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.  avaṟṟaic cariyā tavaṟā eṉṟu nitāṉittup pārppataṟkukkūṭa nēram irātu.  tavira, ovvoru māṇākkaṉum aṟintuḷḷavaṟṟaic cātāraṇamāka ovvoruvaṉukkum vāykkak kūṭiya mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku viṉākkaḷaikkoṇṭu aḷantaṟivatu ciṟitum poruttamaṉṟu.  inta mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku viṉākkaḷilum oṉṟiraṇṭu kaṭiṉamāka amaintālum, allatu avaṟṟiṉ viṭaikaḷai avaṉ aṟiyāviṭiṉum avaṉ cōtaṉaiyil tavaṟak kūṭumallavā? itaṉāl avaṉai oṉṟum aṟiyātavaṉ eṉṟu muṭivu kaṭṭuvatu poruntumā? eṉavē, cintaṉaiyiṉ kāraṇamāka viṭaiyiṟukkak kūṭiya viṉākkaḷaik kaiyāḷuvataṟku immuṟai ciṟitum poruntātu.  tavira, māṇākkarkaṭkum āḻntu aṟutiyiṭṭu viṭaiyiṟuppataṟku vēṇṭiya kālattaiyum tara iyalātu.  eṉavē, vāymoḻi viṉākkaḷai aḷavuk karuviyākak koḷvataiviṭa kaṟpikkum upāyamākak koḷvatē ēṟpuṭaittu eṉṟu kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.  ākavē, vāymoḻi viṉākkaḷ iṉṟaiya navīṉa aḷaviyalil iṭam peṟavillai.
2. kaṭṭuraic cōtaṉaikaḷ
kaṭṭuraic cōtaṉaiyai nām naṉku aṟivōm.  itu pallāṇṭukaḷāka naṭaimuyil iruntu varuvatu; mūṉṟu allatu iraṇṭarai maṇikkāla aḷavil aintu allatu āṟu viṉāk kaḷaik kēṭṭu oru pāṭa muḻuvatilum māṇākkaṉukku irukka vēṇṭiya oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu āṇṭu aṟivai aḷantu aṟiya muyalkiṉṟatu intac cōtaṉai.  putumuṟaic cōtaṉaikaḷ celvākkup peṟṟa piṟaku itaṉ celvākku kuṟaiyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu.  eṉiṉum, intac cōtaṉaiyai aṭiyōṭu nīkka vaḻiyēyillai; kallūri vakuppukkaḷil itutāṉ iṉṟum āṭci celuttivarukṉiṟatu.  kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ iccōtaṉai cariyāṉa aḷavu karuviyalla eṉpataip pallāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē meyppittu viṭṭaṉar.  itiluḷḷa kuṟaikaḷaiyum naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.  mukkiyamāṉa oru cilavaṟṟai maṭṭilum īṇṭuk kavaṉippōm.
aḷantaṟiyum ellaik kuṟaivu:  itaṉāl aḷantaṟiyak kūṭiya poruḷaṟivu mikak kuṟaivu.  ciṟiya kāla aḷavil āṇṭu muḻuvatum paṭitta pāṭa aṟivai aintāṟu viṉākkaḷaik koṇṭu tēṟiviṭa muṭiyum eṉṟu eṇṇuvatu poruntāta toṉṟu; appaṭi aḷappatil oru cila mukkiya ellaip parappukkaḷai maṭṭilumtāṉ aṟiya iyalum.  oru viṉāvait tavaṟākap purintukoḷpavar allatu avviṭaiyiṉ pakuti kaṟpikkappaṭṭapoḻutu paḷḷikku varātiruntavar cariyāka viṭaiyiṟukka muṭiyātu pōtal kūṭum.  itaṉāl avaraip poruḷ aṟivil kuṟaivuḷḷavar eṉṟu muṭivu kaṭṭutal poruntātu.
tiruttuvōriṉ maṉanilai:  tērvāḷarkaḷ māṇākkar kaḷiṉ viṭaiyēṭukaḷai orē potu nōkkamum orēvita nōkkamum koṇṭu tiruttukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkillai.  viṭaikaḷiṉ takutiyai yaṟintu ēṟṟa matippeṇkaḷaik koṭuppatu tērvāḷarkaḷiṉ maṉanilaiyaiyē poṟuttuḷḷatu eṉpataṟku ettaṉaiyō aṉupavaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  mukkuṇavacattāl muṟai piṟaḻntupōvatuṭaṉ nillātu ciṉam, makiḻcci mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kēṟpa matippu eṇkaḷ kuṟaintum mikuntum pōtalkūṭum.  ittakaiya nilaikaḷaik kaṇṭu māṇākkarkaḷ illāḷ ūṭi niṉṟakālattil intat tērvāḷar ivvāṟu kuṟaivāka matippiṭṭār pōlum! eṉṟu eḷḷi nakaiyāṭuvatai nām kāṇāmal illai.  pērāciriyar pālārṭ ittakaiya nikaḻcci oṉṟiṉait tam nūlil kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.1 1920-il amerikka nāṭṭup palkalaikkaḻakam oṉṟil varalāṟṟup pāṭattil oru tērvu naṭantatu.  antat tērviṉ viṭaiyēṭukaḷai āṟu pērāciriyarkaḷ tirutta vēṇṭum.  100-kku 60 matippeṇkaḷukkuk kuṟaivāka vāṅkuvōr tērvil tavaṟiyavarkaḷ.  tavaṟum ellaiyiluḷḷavarkaḷiṉ viṭaiyēṭukaḷai ellōrum pārkka vēṇṭum.  maṉaccāṉṟukkuc ciṟitum māṟātu paṇiyāṟṟum oruvar taṉakku viḻikāṭṭiyāka irukkumporuṭṭu mātiri viṭaikaḷai eḻutikkoṇṭu tiruttiṉār.  tavaṟutalāka inta viṭaiyēṭu tavaṟum nilaiyiluḷḷavarkaḷiṉ viṭaiyēṭu kaḷuṭaṉ kalantuviṭṭatu.  ataiyum oruvariṉ viṭaiyēṭu eṉṟu ēṉaiyōr niṉaittu ataṟkum matippeṇkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉar.  atu 40-liruntu 80 varaiyilum matippeṇkaḷ peṟṟatu.  taṉṉuṭaiya tērvilēyē tavaṟum pērāciriyar nilaiyaik kāṇum poḻutum itu pōṉṟa piṟa cāṉṟukaḷālum ik kaṭṭuraic cōtaṉaiyil oru peruṅkuṟai iruppatāka uṟuti ceyyavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.  ivai iraṇṭum poruṅkuṟaikaḷ.  vēṟu kuṟaikaḷum uḷḷaṉa.  pērāciriyar pālārṭ tiruttuvōraip paṟṟik kūṟum karuttu ivviṭattil cintikkattakkatu.  maruttuvar kaḷiṉ tavaṟukaḷ kallaṟaikkuk kīḻ maṟaintu kiṭappataip pōlavē, tērvāḷarkaḷiṉ tavaṟukaḷ potumakkaḷ pārvai yiliruntu maṟaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.2
piṟa kuṟaikaḷ:  kaṭṭurai eṉpatu pala kūṟukaḷaik koṇṭa tiṟaṉāl uṇṭākkappaṭṭa ōr paṭaippu, ovvoruvarum tattamakkēṟṟavāṟu aḷavukōlkaḷai māṟṟikkoṇṭu atait tiruttuvar.  kaiyeḻuttu, moḻinaṭai, poruḷaik kōvaiyākat tokuttukkūṟum pōkku, ariya collāṭci, maraputtoṭar āṭci, niṟuttaṟkuṟikaḷai iṭutal pōṉṟa kūṟukaḷum oru kaṭṭuraiyiṉ tarattai aṟutiyiṭuvataṟku mēṟkoḷḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  iṉṉum vēṟu kuṟaikaḷum uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟai aṟiñarkaḷ eḻutiya aḷaviyal nūlkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷka.
niṟaikaḷ:  iṉi, ic cōtaṉaikaḷāl niṟaikaḷē illāmal illai.  mukkiyamāṉavaṟṟai maṭṭilum īṇṭu kūṟuvōm.
(1) ivvakaic cōtaṉaikaḷai āyattam ceyvatum eḷitu; kaiyāḷuvatum eḷitu.  iccōtaṉaikaḷiṉ viṉākkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷai āciriyarkaḷ naṉku aṟivar; avaṟṟiṟku viṭai yiṟuppataiyum māṇākkarkaḷ naṉku aṟivar.  viṉākkaḷukku evvāṟu viṭaiyiṟuppatu eṉpataippaṟṟi māṇākkarkaṭku viḷakkattēvaiyē illai.
(2) paḷḷik kalvit tiṭṭattiluḷḷa ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷukkum immuṟai āyvukaḷai eḷitākak kaiyāḷalām.  kaṇakkaṟivut tiṟaṉ, kaiyeḻuttut tiṟaṉ, paṭipput tiṟaṉ pōṉṟavaṟṟai iv vāyvukaḷāl aḷantaṟiya iyalāviṭiṉum, vēṟu tuṟaikaḷil avai peritum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(3) poruḷkaḷiṉ varaiyaṟaivukaḷ kūṟuvatu, teḷivu paṭuttik kāṭṭuvatu, tokuppatu, vakuppatu, virippatu, curukkuvatu mutaliya tiṟaṉkaḷai yellām ivvakai āyvukaḷāltāṉ aḷantaṟiya iyalum.  uyarnilai vakuppu māṇākkarkaṭku, mukkiyamākak kallūri palkalaikkaḻaka māṇākkarkaṭku, iccōtaṉaikaḷ payaṉpaṭum.
ivaṟṟait tavira, vēṟu pala niṟaikaḷum kāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  avai yāvum kūṟuvōriṉ maṉappāṉmaiyaip poṟuttaṉa vēyaṉṟi, ārāycciyiṉ muṭivukaḷ aṉṟu.  eṉavē, avai īṇṭu eṭuttuk kūṟappeṟavillai.
kaṭṭuraic cōtaṉaiyaic cīrpaṭuttuvatu:  itupaṟṟip pala aṟiñarkaḷ ārāyntuḷḷaṉar.  avarkaḷ kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷ iraṇṭu pirivukaḷil aṭaṅkum.  avai: (i) cōtaṉaikkut tērnteṭukkum poruḷum viṉākkaḷai amaikkum muṟaiyum; (ii) viṭaiyēṭukaḷait tiruttum muṟaikaḷ.  ovvoruvarum vakuttuḷḷa vitikaḷ yāvum nilaiyāṉavai alla.  eṉavē, ellāk kālattukkum ellōrālum kaiyāḷakkūṭiya mūṉṟu vitikaḷai maṭṭilum īṇṭuk kūṟuvōm.
(1) cōtaṉaiyiṉ tiṭṭamāṉa nōkkattai āciriyarkaḷum māṇākkarkaḷum teḷivāka aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  kaṭṭuraic cōtaṉaiyil karuttu. ārāyum āṟṟal pōṉṟa kūṟukaḷukkuttāṉ mukkiyattuvam tarutal vēṇṭum.  kavarcci karamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷ, toṭarpukaḷai uṇarttum iṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟai cōtaṉaikkuriya pakutikaḷākak koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  muṭivu kūṟutal, tokuttuk kūṟutal, ciṟappāṉa nikaḻccikaḷiliruntu potu vitiyākkik kūṟutal pōṉṟa pakutikaḷait tēṟuvatu kaṭiṉam.  āṉāl, avaṟṟaippōl māṇākkarkaḷiṉ maṉappaṇpukaḷait teḷivākak kāṭṭum vēṟu pakutikaḷē illaiyeṉṟu kūṟi viṭalām.
(2) cōtaṉai ceyyappaṭum pakuti cōtaṉaiyiṉ nōkkattai yoṭṭiyiruttal vēṇṭum.  pāṭa nōkkattai yoṭṭic cōtaṉaikaḷ amaivatu ciṟappākum.  putumuṟaic cōtaṉai viṉākkaḷaippōlavē iccōtaṉai viṉākkaḷum avakācam koṭuttu āḻntu cintittu āyattam ceyyap peṟumāṉāl, iccōtaṉaikaḷāl māṇākkarkaḷ peṟum matippeṇkaḷum kūṭiyavarai tiṭṭamāka irukkum; aḷavīṭṭiṉ taramum perukum.
(3) viṭaiyēṭukaḷait tiruttuvataṟkut tiṭṭamāṉa vitikaḷai vakuttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  iv vitikaḷ tēvaiyaṟṟa kūṟukaḷai vilakkuvaṉavāka amaital vēṇṭum.  ittakaiya vitikaḷ aḷavukōlaic cīrpaṭutti aḷavīṭukaḷiṉ tarattaiyum uyarttum.



3. tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉaikaḷ
tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉaikaḷ eṉpatu aṭippaṭaiyil tēṟavēṇṭiya poruḷ cārpuṟṟē irukkum; avaṟṟiṟkup potunilai aḷavaikaḷ (Norms) aṟutiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  avaikaḷ pōtumāṉa aḷavu ērāḷamāṉa pērkaḷukkuk koṭukkap peṟṟu avarkaḷ peṟum matippeṇkaḷaik koṇṭu potunilai aḷavaikaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  intac cōtaṉaikaḷai atē vayatu allatu atē aṟivu nilaiyuḷḷavarkaḷukkuk koṭuttāl, avarkaḷum atē matippeṇkaḷaip peṟuvar.  ittakaiya cōtaṉaikaḷ it tuṟaiyil naṉku payiṟci peṟṟavarkaḷaik koṇṭu cōtaṉa āyattam ceyyum aṭippaṭaiyāṉa vitikaḷait taḻuvi uruvākkap peṟṟavai.  kaṭanta aimpatu āṇṭukaḷāka mēṉāṭukaḷil intac cōtaṉai iyakkam tōṉṟi ceyaṟpaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.  mutalām ulakap perum pōrukkup piṟakutāṉ inta iyakkam nalla muṟaiyil vaḷarntatu.
tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉaikaḷ pala tuṟaikaḷaip paṟṟiyavai.  mutaṉ mutalāka aṟitiṟaṉ cōtaṉaikaḷaittāṉ tarappaṭuttu vatil aṟiñarkaḷ kavaṉam celuttiṉar.  iṉṟu aṟivut tuṟai,  paḷḷittuṟai allāta piṟa tuṟaikaḷilum cōtaṉaikaḷ āyattam ceyyap peṟṟut tarappaṭuttap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ivvāṟu tarappaṭuttap peṟṟa cōtaṉaikaḷ kaṟpittal tuṟaiyil aḷattal muṟaiyil ciṟanta muṉṉēṟṟattaik kaṇṭuḷḷaṉa; aḷattalaic cariyāṉa muṟaiyilum nēriya muṟaiyilum koṇṭucelutti yuḷḷaṉa.  aṇmaiyil mēṉāṭukaḷil palvēṟu vitamāṉa tarappaṭuttap peṟṟa cōtaṉaikaḷ acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa; avai kalvit tuṟaiyil pala  pirivukaḷaip paṟṟiyavai.  avaṟṟuḷ aṭiyiṟ kuṟippiṭappeṟṟuḷḷa oru cilavaṟṟaip paṟṟit tāymoḻi āciriyarkaḷ aṟintiruttal mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.
	(i)	aṟitiṟaṉ cōtaṉaikaḷ
	(ii)	nāṭṭac cōtaṉaikaḷ
	(iii)	akkaṟaiyaṟi cōtaṉaikaḷ
	(iv)	muṉṉaṟi cōtaṉaikaḷ
	(v)	aṭaivuc cōtaṉaikaḷ
	(vi)	kuṟaiyaṟi cōtaṉaikaḷ
ivai ovvoṉṟaiyumpaṟṟi oru ciṟitu kūṟuvōm.
(i) aṟitiṟaṉ cōtaṉaikaḷ (Intelligence tests):  ivaṟṟil taṉiyāḷ cōtaṉaikaḷ, kuḻu allatu kūṭṭac cōtaṉaikaḷ eṉa iruvakai uḷa.  muṉṉavaṟṟiṉ tāyakam ḵpireñcu nāṭu.  piṉṉavaṟṟiṉ piṟappiṭam amerikka nāṭu.  taṉiyāḷ cōtaṉaikaḷ aṟivu nilaiyaic cariyāka aḷakkavalla aḷavu karuvi eṉṟu kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar; accōtaṉaikaḷtām iṉṟu mikavum celvākkup peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  cōtaṉaiyiṉ nīḷam, palvēṟu tulaṅkalkaḷaik kāṇal, ovvoruvarum taṉittaṉiyākak kuṟippukkaḷai aṟiyum muṟai, tērvāḷar ovvoruvariṭamum veḷippaṭum ovvoru tulaṅkalaiyum kāṇum vāyppu, cōtaṉaikaḷaik kaiyāḷuvatil tarappaṭuttappeṟṟa muṟaikaḷ, tiruttum muṟai ākiya kūṟukaḷ iccōtaṉaikaḷaic ciṟanta aḷavu kōlākki yuḷḷaṉa.  payiṟciyum aṉupavamumpeṟṟa nipuṇaraik koṇṭu tāṉ cōtaṉaikaḷaik kaiyāḷa vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, cātāraṇa āciriyarkaḷāl ivaṟṟaik kaiyāḷamuṭiyātu.  āyiṉum, iccōtaṉaikaḷ kāṇum muṭivukaḷukku evvāṟu viḷakkam taruvatu eṉpatai ovvoru tāymoḻi yāciriyarum aṟintirut tal vēṇṭum. 
cōtaṉaikaḷiṉ ēṟpuṭaimai (Validity) cila aṭip paṭaiyāṉa koḷkaikaḷaip poṟuttuḷḷatu.  aṟitiṟaṉ eṉpatu kuṭivaḻi vanta iyaṟkaip paṇpu; cūḻnilaiyālum payiṟciyālum ataṉai māṟṟa muṭiyātu.  atu maṉa vāḻvaip paṟṟiya potup paṇpēyaṉṟi tiṭṭamāṉa paṇpaṉṟu.  atu toṭakkattil uṭal vayatiṟkēṟṟavāṟu vēkamākavum kumarap paruvam eytiya piṟaku mikavum metuvākavum mutiṟciyaṭaintu irupatāvatu āṇṭil ataṉ vaḷarcci niṉṟu viṭukiṉṟatu.  aṇmaiyil tārṉṭaik pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ ceyta ārāycci kāḷal oruvariṉ naṭupparuvam varaiyilum aṟitiṟaṉ vaḷarcci taṭaippaṭavillai eṉṟu kaṇṭaṟiyappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  muṉ-kumarap paruvattil aṟitiṟaṉ vaḷarcci mikak kuṟaivāka iruppatāl ṭermaṉ tiruttiyamaitta piṉēyiṉ cōtaṉaikaḷil paṉṉiraṇṭu, patiṉāṉku vayatiṟku viṉākkaḷum vaḷarntavar kaḷukku iraṇṭu cōtaṉaikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
piṉēyiṉ āyvu 1905-il mutaṉ mutalāka ḵpireñcu nāṭṭil veḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu.  piṟaku piṉēyum caimaṉum cērntu 1908-lum, 1911-lum mīṇṭum tiruttaṅkaḷ ceytu veḷiyiṭṭaṉar.  amerikkāvil ivvāyvukaḷait taḻuviyum tiruttiyum 1911-il kāṭārṭ eṉpārum 1912-il kuhalmāṉ eṉpārum, 1916-il ṭermeṉ eṉpārum, 1922-il herri eṉpārum, 1937-il ṭermeṉ, merril eṉpārkaḷum veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.  iṟutiyākak kūṟappaṭṭa cōtaṉaikaḷ tām iṉṟu amerikkāvil palarāl aṟintavai yākavum naṭaimuṟaiyil uḷḷavaiyākavum uḷḷaṉa.  piṉē mūṉṟu mutal patiṉmūṉṟu vayatuvarai ḵpireñcu nāṭṭuk kuḻavikaḷiṉ naṭattaiyaik kaṇṭaṟiya āyattam ceyta cōtaṉai kaḷaip pōlavē, tamiḻnāṭṭuk kuḻantaikaṭkēṟṟa cōtaṉaikaḷai nam nāṭṭu uḷaviyalār ārāyntu āyattam ceyya vēṇṭiyatu iṉṟiyamaiyāta tākum.
kuḻuc cōtaṉaikaḷ (Group tests):  piṉēyiṉ āyvukaḷaik kaiyāḷuvatu kaṭiṉamāka iruppatālum, avaṟṟai ovvoru kuḻantaikaḷukkum taṉittaṉiyākak koṭukka vēṇṭiyiruppatālum avai āciriyarkaḷukkuc ciṟitum naṭaimuṟaiyil payaṉpaṭavillai.  ākavē, uḷaviyalār pala kuḻantaikaḷukkuc cērntāṟpōṟ koṭukkavalla eḻuttu muṟaic cōtaṉaikaḷai āyattam ceytu avaṟṟai tarappaṭutti yuḷḷaṉar.  ivaṟṟiṟkeṉa kuṟippukkaḷ, viṭaikaḷ, potunilai aḷavaikaḷ ākiyavai uḷḷaṉa.  kuṟippukkaḷai mika naṉṟāka aṟintukoṇṭu evarum ivaṟṟai eḷitil kaiyāḷalām.  āciriyarkaḷ paḷḷikaḷil ic cōtaṉaikaḷaik kaiyāṇṭu taṉippaṭṭa māṇākkarkaḷiṉ aṟivaippaṟṟik kavarcciyāṉa takavalkaḷai aṟintu koḷḷalām.  āciriyarkaḷ nāṭōṟum māṇākkarkaḷuṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻaku vatāl pala takavalkaḷai aṟintu koḷḷa iyalum eṉṟum, ittakaiya cōtaṉaikaḷ tēvaiyillai yeṉṟum orucilar niṉaittal kūṭum; atu tavaṟu.  cilacamayam ittakaiya cōtaṉai kaḷāl viyattaku muṭivukaḷaiyum kāṇṭalkūṭum.  vakuppil tōṟṟattil mantamākak kāṇum māṇākkaṉ cōtaṉaiyil nalla matippeṇkaḷaip peṟutalkūṭum.  nalla aṟivuḷḷa ammāṇākkaṉ yātu kāraṇattālō paḷḷivēlaiyil mantamatiyiṉaṉ pōl kāṭciyaḷittut taṉ matiyai āciriyarkaḷ aṟiyātu ceytuviṭṭāṉ.  aṟitiṟaṉkaḷ innilaiyait teḷivākkum.  aṉṟiyum, iccōtaṉaikaḷāl mikak kuṟukiya kālattil māṇākkarkaḷaippaṟṟip pala takavalkaḷai aṟintu koḷḷa iyalum.  ōr āciriyar āṇṭiṉ oru paruvattil tam vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷaippaṟṟi aṟiyum takavalkaḷai avar orumaṇi nērattil aṟintukoḷḷa iyalum.
kuḻuc cōtaṉaikaḷ kaṭanta mutalām ulakap perum pōrk kālattil amerikkāvil mutaṉ mutalil tōṉṟiṉa.  irāṇuva ālḵpāc cōtaṉaikaḷ irāṇuva pīṭṭāc cōtaṉaikaḷ avaṟṟuḷ mukkiyamāṉavai.  piṉṉavai ceykaic cōtaṉaikaḷ; paṭaiyāḷarkaḷait tērnteṭuppataṟku iccōtaṉaikaḷ peritum payaṉpaṭuttappeṟṟaṉa.  pōrukkup piṟaku oṭṭi, ṭermeṉ pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ paḷḷikaḷil payaṉpaṭak kūṭiyavāṟu attakaiya cōtaṉaikaḷai āyattam ceytu 1918-kkum, 1925-kkum iṭaippaṭṭa kālattil veḷiyiṭṭaṉar.  1925-kkup piṟaku ettaṉaiyō cōtaṉaikaḷ veḷivantuḷḷaṉa.
ceykaic cōtaṉaikaḷ (Performance tests):  piṉē kaṇṭa cōtaṉaikaḷ paṭikkat terintavarkaṭku maṭṭilumē payaṉpaṭak kūṭiyavai. paṭiyātavarkaḷaiyum, ceviṭarkaḷākavum, ūmaiyarkaḷāka uḷḷavarkaḷaiyum evvāṟu ārāyvatu? avarkaḷuṭaiya ceyalkaḷaik koṇṭutāṉ avarkaḷuṭaiya aṟivu nilaiyai ārāyavēṇṭum.  intac ceykaic cōtaṉai kaḷil coṟkaḷukkup patilāka pulaṉīṭāṉa poruḷkaḷ (Concrete things) amaintirukkum.  avarkaḷukkut tarappeṟum piracciṉai pulaṉīṭāṉavaiyāka iruntālum aḵtu ārāyntu muṭivu kāṇa vallatākavē irukkum.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, pala vaṭivamāṉa marattuṇṭukaḷai oru marattuṇṭiṉiṉṟum veṭṭi eṭuttu ivarkaḷiṉ etirē vaittu veṭṭi yeṭutta attuṇṭukaḷai mīṭṭum anta marattuṇṭil evvāṟu patiyavaikkiṉṟaṉar eṉpataṉaik kavaṉittāl porutta muṇarum aṟivu evvāṟu avarkaḷiṭaiyē vaḷarntuḷḷatu.  eṉpatai aṟiyalām.  ovvoru tuṇṭiṉaiyum poruttuvataṟku eṭuttukkoḷḷum kālam, avarkaḷ tavaṟu ceytalkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai ākiyavaṟṟai yellām kuṟittuvaittu oppiṭṭu nōkkiṉāl vayatiṟkēṟpa ivai māṟi varuvataṉaik kāṇṭalkūṭum.  ittakaiya potu nilai aḷavukōlaikkoṇṭu ellāk kuḻantaikaḷiṉ aṟivu nilaiyaiyum aḷakkalām.
(ii) nāṭṭac cōtaṉaikaḷ:  paḷḷi yāciriyarkaḷukku ittakaiya cōtaṉaikaḷaik kaiyāḷum vāyppukkaḷē irāteṉiṉum, avarkaḷ ac cōtaṉaikaḷiṉ potu iyalpukaḷaip paṟṟiyāvatu aṟintiruttal avaciyam.  oru kuṟippiṭṭa tuṟaiyil tiṟaṉai vaḷarttukkoḷḷavalla oruvariṭam amaintuḷḷa āṟṟalaittāṉ nāṭṭam (Aptitude) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar.  oru poṟi nuṭpa nāṭṭac cōtaṉaiyaik koṇṭu oruvar karuvikaḷaiyum piṟa poruḷkaḷaiyum mēṟ koṇṭu ceyyavalla oruvaratu kaivēlaittiṟaṉai aḷantaṟital kūṭum.  potut tiṟaṉukkum taṉittiṟaṉukkum toṭarpu iruntālum, ataṟku vitivilakkum uḷḷatai aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷaṉar.  ittuṟaiyil virivāṉa ārāyccikaḷ naṭatti iyalpāka amainta potuttiṟaṉuṭaṉ tiṭṭamāṉa maṉap pōkkukaḷaiyum aṟintuḷḷaṉar.  taṉi nāṭṭac cōtaṉaikaḷaik koṇṭu āciriyarkaḷum māṇākkarkaḷum toḻil tuṟaikaḷilum kalvittuṟaikaḷilum ūkkavalla tiṭṭamāṉa maṉappōkkukaḷaik (Definite tendencies) kaṇṭaṟiyalām.  iṉṟu pala tuṟaikaḷaiyum ārāyavalla cōtaṉaikaḷ āyattam ceyyappeṟṟu veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ivai yāvum muṉṉaṟi cōtaṉaikaḷē.  oru kuṟippiṭṭa cūḻnilaiyil iccōtaṉaikaḷaik kaiyāḷvataṟku āciriyarkaḷ aḷaviyal tuṟaiyil aṇmaiyil veḷivantuḷḷa nūlkaḷaip paṭittaṟiya vēṇṭum; allatu attuṟaiyil taṉippayiṟciyum aṉupavamum peṟṟuḷḷavarkaḷiṉ tuṇaiyai nāṭal vēṇṭum.
(iii) akkaṟaiyaṟi cōtaṉaikaḷ:  ivai mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa nāṭṭac cōtaṉaikaḷuṭaṉ neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiyavai.  ivaṟṟaikkoṇṭu oruvar ettoḻilil akkaṟai kāṭṭukiṉṟār eṉpatai yaṟiyalām.  ivaṟṟai āyattam ceyta aṟiñarkaḷ akkaṟai (Interest) eṉpatu kuṭivaḻi varum paṇpu eṉṟu kūṟavillai.  aṭippaṭaiyāṉa maṉappōkkukaḷ mika iḷamai yilēyē oruvariṭam nilai peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa uḷaviyal uṇmaiyiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil ic cōtaṉaikaḷ āyattam ceyyappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ippōkkukaḷ naṭuk kumarapparuvattil allatu piṉ-kumarapparuvattil mika aḻuttamākap paṭintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.  eṉavē, ip pōkkukaḷaikkoṇṭu oruvar iyalpāka virumpum toḻil tuṟaiyai aṟutiyiṭalām.  ṭāṉ pōrṭu palkalaik kaḻakattaic cērnta ṭrāṅ eṉṟa pērāciriyar ittuṟaiyil naṉmuṟaiyil ārāyccikaḷ ceytuḷḷār.
(iv) muṉṉaṟi cōtaṉaikaḷ:  piṉṉaiya vaḷarcciyi-ṉaippaṟṟi muṉṉarē aṟivu koḷuttavalla cōtaṉaikaḷ muṉṉaṟi cōtaṉaikaḷ (Prognostic tests) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ivai kuḻantaikaḷiṉ takutip pāṭṭiṉai ārāyntu ak kuḻantaikaḷ evvettuṟaikaḷil muṉṉukku varuvataṟkillai eṉpatai uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟaik koṇṭu oru kuḻantai kaṇitam, moḻi, icai mutaliya tuṟaikaḷil muṉṉukku varakkūṭumā eṉpatai ārāyalām.  āṉāl, naṭaimuṟaiyil ivaṟṟai atikamākap payaṉpaṭuttu vatillai.  kāraṇam, inta aḷavu karuvikaḷil makkaḷ iṉṉum atika nampikkai vaikkavillai; avarkaḷ nampukiṉṟa aḷavukkum avai iṉṉum cemmai yaṭaiyavillai.  āciriyarkaḷum potumakkaḷum avai kuḻantaikaḷiṉ tiṭṭamāṉa nāṭṭaṅkaḷai evvāṟu muṉṉarē cariyāka aṟivikkakkūṭum eṉṟu aiyuṟukiṉṟaṉar.
(v) aṭaivuc cōtaṉaikaḷ (Achievement tests):  mukkiyamākak kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam māṇākkarkaḷ peṟum aṟiviṉai aḷantaṟiyavalla cōtaṉaikaḷaittāṉ ippeyarāl kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar.  payaṉṭum nōkkattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu ivai māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  eṉavē, oru tiṭṭamāṉa nōkkattai yoṭṭi ivaṟṟaik kaiyāḷavēṇṭum.  aṉṟiyum, attiṭṭamāṉa nōkkattai yoṭṭi avai ākkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉavā eṉpataic cariyāka aṟintutāṉ payaṉpaṭuttavumvēṇṭum.  nōkkattai yoṭṭi ic cōtaṉaikaḷ pala vakaiyākap pirikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ovvoru tuṟaiyilum potuvāṉa aṟivu nilaiyiṉai aḷantaṟiya valla kaṇakkeṭuppuc cōtaṉaikaḷ (Survey tests) oru vakai.  ivaṟṟaippōlavē, kaṟṟa pakutikaḷil takavalkaḷai maṭṭilum ārāya valla takaval cōtaṉaikaḷ (Information tests) maṟṟāru vakai.  perumpālum ivai vayatiṟku ēṟpa amaiyāmal vakuppiṟku ēṟpa amaintirukkum.  kalvit tuṟaiyil tarappaṭuttiya ic cōtaṉaikaḷiṉ payaṉ aḷavaṟṟatu.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ potu aṟivu nilaiyai āyavum, taṉippaṭṭōriṉ kuṟaikaḷai āyavum māṇākkarkaḷait taram vāriyākap pirikkavum, oru kuḻuviṉ tiṟaṉaip piṟitoru kuḻuviṉ tiṟaṉuṭaṉ oppiṭṭup pārkkavum, kaṟpikkum muṟaikaḷiṉ tiṟaṉkaḷai ārāyavum ivai iṉṟu peritum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaipaṟṟiya muḻu vivaraṅkaḷai aḷaviyal nūlkaḷil (Books on Measurement) kaṇṭu koḷka.
(vi) kuṟaiyaṟi cōtaṉaikaḷ (Diagnostic tests):  kaṟpatilō kaṟpippatilō uḷḷa kuṟaikaḷaik kāṇavalla cōtaṉaikaḷaik kuṟaiyaṟi cōtaṉaikaḷ eṉṟu muṟai vallār kuṟippiṭuvar.  kaṟpittal tuṟaiyil ivvakaic cōtaṉaikaḷiṉ tēvai iṉṟiyamaiyātatu.  navīṉa kalvi muṟaiyil ivaṟṟiṉ avaciyam mikavum vaṟpuṟuttapṭukiṉṟatu.  ovvoru pāṭattilum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ tiṭṭamāṉa kuṟaikaḷaiyum niṟaikaḷaiyum aḷantaṟiyavalla cōtaṉaikaḷ āyattam ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, kaṭṭurait tuṟaiyil ittakaiya cōtaṉaikaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṉ vākkiya amaippil kuṟaikaḷ, kaṭṭuraip poruḷkaḷaik kōvaiyāka  amaittalil kuṟaikaḷ, teḷivāṉa cintaṉaiyiṉmai ākiyavaṟṟaik kāṭṭutal kūṭum.  māṇākkar kaḷiṉ ārāyntu pārkkum muṟaiyil kuṟaikaḷ, oru kuṟippiṭṭa tuṟaiyil uṟṟu nōkkiyaṟitalil kuṟaikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟai aḷantaṟiya valla cōtaṉaikaḷum āyattam ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  tiṭṭamākak kuṟaikaḷai aḷantu kāṭṭavalla cōtaṉaiḷait tāṉ ciṟanta cōtaṉaikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭum.  ittakayai cōtaṉaikaḷāl māṇākkarkaḷ kaṟṟalil tam kuṟaikaḷait tiṭṭamāka aṟital muṭiyum.  āciriyarkaḷum kaṟpittalai evvevvakaikaḷil tiṟamaiyāka māṟṟiyamaittal kūṭum eṉṟum, tām kaiyāṇṭa muṟaikaḷil eṉṉeṉṉa kuṟaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum aṟintu koḷḷutal kūṭum.
intac cōtaṉaikaḷiṉ payaṉkaḷ: piṟa cōtaṉaikaḷāl kāṇa iyalāta oru tiṭṭamāṉa kūṟukaḷai tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉaikaḷāl aḷantaṟiyalām.  avaṟṟaip paṟṟi īṇṭu oru ciṟitu nōkkuvōm.
(1) oppiṭutal: ic cōtaṉaikaḷ oru vakuppu allatu paḷḷiyaip piṟitōr ūriluḷḷa atē vakuppu allatu paḷḷiyuṭaṉ oppiṭṭup pārttaṟkup payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ic cōtaṉaikaḷiṉ potunilai aḷavaikaḷ itaṟku mikavum mukkiyamāṉavai.  oppiṭutalpaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷai aḷaviyal nūlkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷka.
(2) kuṟaiyaṟital:  kaṟpittal tuṟaiyil kuṟaiyaṟital mikavum mukkiyamāṉa ōr kūṟu ākum.  uḷaviyal aṟivu vaḷaravaḷara itaṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyai aṟiñarkaḷ naṉku uṇarntu varukiṉṟaṉar.  tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉaikaḷaik koṇṭu oru kuṟippiṭṭa tuṟaiyil kuṟaikaḷai aṟiyalām.
(3) takuti pirittal: kalviyaṟivuc cōtaṉaikaḷai maṭṭilum koṇṭu māṇākkarkaḷai aṟivu nilaikkēṟṟavāṟu takuti pirittalai aṟiñarkaḷ cariyeṉa oppukkoḷvatillai.  orē mātiriyāṉa pāṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷum pāṭa nūlkaḷum illāmai kāraṇamāka āciriyarkaḷ tām āyattam ceyta cōtaṉaikaḷaik koṇṭē māṇākkarkaḷait taram pirikkiṉṟaṉar.  iṉṟu kalvittuṟai aṟiñarkaḷ āciriyarkaḷ naṭaimuṟaiyil taram pirippatil kaiyāḷum muṟaikku aṉukūlamāka aṟitiṟaṉ cōtaṉaikaḷaiyum tuṇaik koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar.  māṇākkarkaḷaip pala tuṟaikaḷilum koṇṭu celuttuvataṟku vaḻikāṭṭiyāka iruppataṟku nāṭṭac cōtaṉaikaḷ muṉṉaṟi cōtaṉaikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟaik kaiyāḷa vēṇṭum eṉak karutukiṉṟaṉar.
(4) orē mātiriyāṉa cōtaṉai vaṭivaṅkaḷ: oru cōtaṉaiyiṉāl vaḷarcciyai aṟutiyiṭuvataiviṭa orē nilaiyil ākkappeṟṟa iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu cōtaṉaikaḷiṉ matippeṇkaḷaik koṇṭu aṟutiyiṭuvatu ciṟantatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ karukiṉṟaṉar.  tarappaṭuttiya oṉṟiraṇṭu cōtaṉai kaḷaik koṇṭu aṟivu vaḷarcciyiṉai oppiṭṭup pārkkalām.
(5) kaiyāḷutal: cōtaṉai vaṭivaṅkaḷai eḷitāka amaittāltāṉ nampakak kūṟu (Validity factor) uyar nilaiyil irukkum.  pattup pakutikaḷ uḷḷa oru cōtaṉaiyaiviṭa, pala pakutikaḷākap pirikkappaṭāta cōtaṉai ciṟantatu.  ovvoru pakutikkum iṭai iṭaiyē viḷakkak kuṟippukkaḷ tantu cōtaṉaikaḷait taruvataiviṭa, oru cila viḷakkaṅkaḷuṭaṉ eppoḻutu toṭaṅkuvatu, eppoḻutu muṭippatu eṉṟa kuṟippai maṭṭilum tantu cōtaṉai taruvatu naṉṟu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.  cōtaṉaikaḷaik kaiyāḷuvataṟku muṉ avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkak kuṟippukkaḷai āciriyarkaḷ teḷivāka aṟintu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.  iṉṟu perumpālōrālum kaiyāḷavalla cōtaṉaikaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.  cōtaṉaikaḷaik koṭuttal; tiruttutal, muṭivukaḷukku viḷakkan tarutal pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ viḷakkak kuṟippukkaḷai aṟintu koṇṭavarkaḷ ivvakaic cōtaṉaikaḷaip payaṉuḷḷa muṟaiyil eḷitākak kaiyāḷalām.
4. putumuṟaic cōtaṉaikaḷ
tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉaikaḷiṉ  iyakkam celvākkup peṟṟavuṭaṉ atē muṟaiyil āciriyarkaḷāl āyattam ceyyap peṟum putumuṟaic cōtaṉaikaḷum celvākkup peṟat toṭaṅkiṉa.  ivai tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉaikaḷ alla.  ivai naṭaimuṟaikku vantavuṭaṉ itukāṟum vakuppu aṟaikaḷil naṭaipeṟṟu vanta tēṟum muṟaikaḷum māṟṟam aṭaintaṉa; nāḷaṭaivil intap putumuṟaic cōtaṉaikaḷai āyattam ceyvatilum muṉṉēṟṟattaik kāṇavēṇṭum eṉpatil kalvi nipuṇarkaḷiṉ kavaṉam ceṉṟatu.  eṉavē, ittuṟaiyil naṉmuṟaiyil payiṟci peṟṟa aṟiñarkaḷum uḷaviyal vallu narkaḷum putu muṟaic cōtaṉaikaḷai āyattam ceyvatil ārāyccikaḷai mēṟkoṇṭaṉar.  ivarkaḷ ceyta ārāycci kaḷiṉ viḷaivākac cōtaṉaikaḷai āyattam ceyvatil cila vitikaḷ vakuppappeṟṟaṉa; attuṟaiyil putiya vaḻikaḷum varaiyaṟai ceyyappeṟṟaṉa.
tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉaikaḷai naṭaimuṟaiyil vakuppaṟai kaḷil payaṉpaṭutta iyalātu.  ēṉeṉil, avaṟṟai āyattam ceyta nipuṇar vakuppaṟaikaḷil kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ kaṟpittalil ententak kūṟukaḷil aḻuttam koṭuttuk kaṟpittaṉar eṉpatai aṟiya iyalātu.  eṉavē, tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉaikaḷ paḷḷikaḷai oppiṭṭup pārppataṟkum, orē vakuppukkaḷil pala pirivukaḷai oppiṭuvataṟkum, ōr ūruṭaṉ piṟitoru ūrai ottup pārppataṟkum maṭṭilumē payaṉpaṭuttalām.  eṉavē, ataṉ aṭippaṭaiyil āyattam ceyyap peṟṟaṉavum tarappaṭuttap peṟātaṉavum āciriyarkaḷāl avvappoḻutu āyattam ceyyap peṟuvaṉavumāṉa putu muṟaic cōtaṉaikaḷē vakuppaṟaikaḷil naṭaimuṟaiyil payaṉpaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaikaḷaipṟṟit tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ aṟintu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
putumuṟaic cōtaṉaikaḷil kiṭṭattaṭṭa muppattaintukku mēṟpaṭṭa vakaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  eṉṟālum, ivaṟṟai yellām aṟiñarkaḷ maṟittuṇar vakai (Recognition type), niṉaivu kūrvakai (Recall type) eṉṟu iruṅkūṟiṭṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.  maṟittuṇar vakaiyil cari-tavaṟu cōtaṉaikaḷ, palviṭaiyiṟ poṟukkuc cōtaṉaikaḷ, poruttaṟ cōtaṉaikaḷ aṭaṅkum; niṉaivukūr vakaiyil nirappuc cōtaṉaikaḷ mutaliyavai aṭaṅkum.  cōtaṉait tuṟaiyil muṉṉēṟṟam aṭai ya aṭaiya aṟiñarkaḷ putiya vakaikaḷaik kaṇṭukoṇṭē varukiṉṟaṉar.  eṉavē, ivvakaikaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaiyum vaḷarntukoṇṭē varukiṉṟatu.  āyiṉum, ēḻu allatu eṭṭuvakaik cōtaṉaikaḷē naṭai muṟaiyil payaṉpaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  ellā vakaikaḷaip paṟṟiyum īṇṭu kūṟuvataṟku iṭam illai.  avaṟṟai aḷaviyalai viḷakkum aṟiñarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷil kaṇṭukoḷka.  orucila vakaikaḷ maṭṭilum īṇṭu tarappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
1. cari-tavaṟu cōtaṉaikaḷ:  cila kuṟitta toṭar moḻikaḷaik koṭuttu avaṟṟuḷ varum karuttukkaḷ cariyā, tavaṟā eṉpataṟkēṟpa kākkaip puḷḷiyaiyō cuḻiyaiyō iṭumpaṭi kūṟutal.  ivaṟṟaiyē viṉāvāka amaittu ām illai teriyātu eṉṟa viṭaikaḷil oṉṟai eḻutumāṟum ēvalām.
(e-ṭu)-1.
aṭiyiṟ kāṇum coṟkaḷil cilavaṟṟil eḻuttup piḻaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  cariyāṉavaṟṟiṟku nērāka ‘Î’ eṉṟum piḻaiyāṉavaṟṟiṟku nērāka ‘x’ eṉṟum kuṟikaḷai iṭuka.
1. eṇṇai		11. kēḷvaraku
2. viḷāmpaḻam		12. pūṣāri
3. cilavu		13. cantaṇam
4. kōrttāṉ		14. vaikkal
5. elumiccai		15. ūruṇi
6. nārāyaṇaṉ		16. amāci
7. kōmpai		17. araṅkēṟṟam
8. muṟukaṉ		18. vāḷaippaḻam
9. irumpu		19. tirucciṟṟampalam
10. maṅkaiyarkkaraci		20. viyāḷaṉ
(e-ṭu)-2.
	1.	poṅkalum cuṇṭalum tiṉṟārkaḷ.  (toḻilāku peyar.)
	2.	pūṅkuḻalāḷ eṉap pulavar pōṟṟiṉar.  (aṉ moḻit tokai).
	3.	veṇṭāmarai malarntatu.  (paṇputtokai.)
	4.	paṉikkālam, iṟantakālam, irākukālam, muṟkālam, nikaḻkālam eṉak kālam āṟu vakaippaṭum.
	5.	kāṟṟil aṭipaṭṭa pañcu. (vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiya lukaram.)
2. palviṭaiyiṟ poṟukkuc cōtaṉaikaḷ:  muṟkūṟiya vaṟṟaiviṭa ivai caṟṟu arumaiyum vaḷarcciyumuṭaiyavai.  ivaṟṟuḷ oru poruḷpaṟṟiya pala toṭarmoḻikaḷil etu cari eṉṟu māṇākkarkaḷ oṉṟait tērnteṭukka vēṇṭi varum.  ilakkiya āciriyarkaḷ, avarkaḷ vāḻntakālam, ilakkiya māntarkaḷ, tokai nūlkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai ivaṟṟāl tēṟalām.  putu muṟaic cōtaṉaikaḷil iv vakaiyai māṇākkarkaḷum āciriyarkaḷum peritum virumpukiṉṟaṉar.  ovvoru viṉāvukkum mūṉṟiliruntu aintuvarai toṭar moḻikaḷ koṭukkappeṟum.
(e-ṭu.) kīḻkkāṇum viṉākkaḷil ovvoṉṟiṉ kīḻum aintu viṭaikaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  poruttamāṉavaṟṟiṉ eṇṇai maṭṭilum valappuṟattiluḷḷa pakara vaḷaivukkuḷ eṭuttu eḻutuka.
1. naḷaveṇpāvai iyaṟṟiya pulavar:
(a) oṭṭakkūttar
(ā) kampaṉ
(i) pukaḻēnti
(ī) nakkīrar
(u) ceyaṅkoṇṭār			[	]

2. cāttaṉār iyaṟṟiya nūl:
(a) maṇimēkalai
(ā) kaliṅkattupparaṇi
(i) puṟanāṉūṟu
(ī) tiruvācakam
(u) periyapurāṇam			[	]

3. pāṇṭavarkaḷiṉ uṭaṉ piṟantavaṉ:
(a) cicupālaṉ
(ā) karṇaṉ
(i) vituraṉ
(ī) acuvattāmaṉ
(u) kaṭōṟkajaṉ			[	]
3. nirappuc cōtaṉaikaḷ:  kōṭiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil ēṟṟa coṟkaḷai amaittuk toṭarmoḻiyai muṭittuk kāṭṭal.  ivvakaic cōtaṉaikaḷ kīḻ vakuppu māṇākkarkaṭku mikavum ēṟṟavai.  ēṟṟa collai eḻutuvataṟku muṉ orumuṟai toṭar moḻiyaip paṭittu, piṉ collai amaittu, piṉṉum orumuṟai toṭar moḻiyaip paṭittut toṭarmoḻi cariyāṉa poruḷaik koṭukkiṉṟatā eṉṟu pārkkumpaṭi aṟivuṟutta vēṇṭum.
(e-ṭu). kīḻē varum toṭarmoḻikaḷaik kōṭiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil ēṟṟa coṟkaḷai amaittu muṭittuk kāṭṭuka.
	1.	mutalvēṟṟumai ____ eṉappaṭum.
	2.	____ ____ ____ nikaḻkāla iṭai nilaikaḷ ākum.
	3.	oru moḻiyiṉ īṟṟeḻuttu valliṉa ukaramākavum ayal eḻuttu neṭilākavum iruppiṉ atu ____  ukaramākum.
4. poruttaṟ cōtaṉaikaḷ: iraṇṭu varicaikaḷākat toṭar moḻikaḷai uṇmaiyum poruttamum muṟaiyum piṟaḻa amaittu avaṟṟai poruttumpaṭi ceytal.  nikaḻccikaḷ, avai nikaḻnta āṇṭukaḷ ; pōrkaḷ, avaṟṟai naṭattiya cēṉait talaivarkaḷ; pulavarkaḷ avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷ; paḻamoḻikaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷ; maraputtoṭarkaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ poruḷkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟai ivvāṟu poruttac ceyyalām.  kāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷaiyum varicaip paṭuttalām.  kuṟitta poruḷaṟivait tēṟa itu ciṟanta muṟaiyākum.
(e-ṭu) kīḻkkaṇṭavaṟṟil a piriviluḷḷavaṟṟai ā piriviluḷḷavaṟṟuṭaṉ poruttik kāṭṭuka.  pakaravaḷaivukkuḷ viṭaiyiṉ eṇṇaimaṭṭilum eḻutiṉāl pōtumāṉatu.

5. iṉam pirikkum cōtaṉaikaḷ :  ōr iṉam, allatu oru tokutiyil cērāta, aṭaṅkāta coṟkaḷai avvaricaiyi liruntu aṭittu viṭumpaṭi ceytal.  avaṟṟiṉ kīḻk kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭum paṭiyum ēvalām. (e-ṭu) kīḻkkaṇṭavaṟṟil ovvoṉṟilum avviṉattil aṭaṅkāta oru col uḷḷatu.  accollai maṭṭilum aṭiyil kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭuka.

6. oppuc cōtaṉaikaḷ: koṭutta oru poruḷ etaṉai ottuḷḷatu eṉṟu kāṭṭac ceytal.  avvāṟu kūṟuvatil caṟṟu māṇākkarkaṭku utaviyāka irukkumpaṭi iḵtu atai ottiruppatupōl eṉṟu ōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭaik kūṟip piṉ koṭuttuḷḷa poruḷ etai ottuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟumpaṭi ēvavēṇṭum.
(e-ṭu) kīḻkkāṇum vākkiyaṅkaḷil kōṭiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷai ēṟṟa coṟkaḷāl nirappuka.

cōtaṉaikaḷaik kaiyāḷutalum tiruttutalum: ic cōtaṉaikaḷaik kaiyāḷum muṟaikaḷaippaṟṟi atika viḷakkam tēvaiyillai.  māṇākkarkaṭkuriya kuṟippukkaḷait teḷivākavum viḷakkamākavum amaittu viṭṭāl iccōtaṉaikaḷaik kaiyāḷutal eḷitu. ic cōtaṉaikaḷai māṇākkarkaṭkuk koṭuttu avarkaḷ viṭaiyiṟukkumpoḻutu māṇākkarkaḷ viṉavum evvita aiyaṅkaḷaiyum akaṟṟuvatil āciriyarkaḷ talaiyiṭakkūṭātu.  ovvoru vakaic cōtaṉaikaḷilum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷaik koṭuttu māṇākkar viṭaiyiṟukkum muṟaiyait teḷivākaviḷakki viṭṭāl āciriyarkaḷ utaviyai māṇākkarkaḷ nāṭār.  eṅkāvatu accup piḻaikaḷiruntāl, āciriyar cōtaṉaiyil kalantukoḷḷum aṉaivariṉ kavaṉat taiyum īrttu appiḻaitaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭi aiyattai nīkkalām.
ic cōtaṉaikaḷiṉ viṭaikaḷait tiruttutal mikavum eḷitu. viṉākkaḷukkēṟṟa viṭaikaḷai muṉṉatākavē āyattam ceytu koḷḷavēṇṭum.  ovvoru vakaic cōtaṉaikkum ovvoru muṟai uṇṭu.  cariyāṉa viṭaikaḷai vaṇṇap peṉcilāl aṭaiyāḷamiṭṭut tiruttiṉāl piṉṉāl avaṟṟaik kaṟpittalil payaṉpaṭuttuvatu eḷitāka irukkum. uṟutiyāka aṟiyāmal uttēcamāka viṭaiyiṟuppatai oḻippataṟku upāyaṅkaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ellāvakaik cōtaṉai kaḷilum uttēcamāka viṭaiyiṟuppataṟku vāyppukaḷ illai cari-tavaṟu cōtaṉaikaḷilum, pal viṭaiyiṟ poṟukkuc cōtaṉai kaḷilum avarkaḷ uttēcamāka viṭaiyiṟuttal  kūṭum.  uttēcamāka viṭaiyiṟuppatait tavirppataṟku,
